# Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah

ברטנורא על משנה נדה

Link: [https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah)

חיבור התשתית הקדום של חז״ל, המתעד מגוון דעות הלכתיות ממסורת התורה שבעל פה. נחתם בשנת 200 לספירה לערך.

16th-century commentary printed in most editions of the Mishnah, including summaries of talmudic discussions and legal conclusions.

Sections: Chapter-Mishnah-Comment

Location: Mishnah/Rishonim on Mishnah/Bartenura/Seder Tahorot

Originally from [Sefaria](https://www.sefaria.org) via [TorahLM.org](https://torahlm.org) — see [licensing](https://torahlm.org/sefaria_license).

Version (En): Bartenura on Mishnah, trans. by Rabbi Robert Alpert, 2020 (Segments: 419)


## Chapter 1


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:1)


**שַׁמַּאי אוֹמֵר כָּל הַנָּשִׁים דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן.** מִשּׁוּם דְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר אַרְבַּע נָשִׁים דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן, תָּנֵי נַמִּי שַׁמַּאי אוֹמֵר כָּל הַנָּשִׁים, לַאֲפוֹקֵי מִדְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


שמאי אומר כל הנשים דיין שעתן- since Rabbi Eliezer states (see Mishnah 3 of this chapter): “Four women [fall into the category of those for whom the] time [of first seeing blood] suffices,” it is also taught [here]: “Shammai states: For all women [it is] sufficient for them [to reckon uncleanness from] their time [of discovering a flow],” to exclude from [the statement] of Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:2)


**דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן.** נָשִׁים הָרוֹאוֹת דָּם דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן לְטַמֵּא טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנָּגְעוּ בָּהֶן מִשְּׁעַת רְאִיָּתָן וָאֵילָךְ, וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן מִקַּמֵּי הָכִי נַמִּי הָיָה דָּם וְכָתְלֵי בֵּית הָרֶחֶם הֶעֱמִידוּהוּ וְהָיְתָה טְמֵאָה מִקֹּדֶם. דְּכָל הַנָּשִׁים מְטַמְּאוֹת בַּבַּיִת הַחִיצוֹן וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יָצָא הַדָּם לַחוּץ, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) דָּם יִהְיֶה זֹבָהּ בִּבְשָׂרָהּ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהוּא בַּבָּשָׂר וְלֹא יָצָא לַחוּץ, טְמֵאָה. וְטַעֲמָא דְּשַׁמַּאי דְּלֹא חָיֵשׁ דִּלְמָא מִקַּמֵּי הָכִי הֲוָה דָּם, שֶׁאִם אַתָּה אוֹמֵר כֵּן, לִבּוֹ שֶׁל אָדָם נוֹקְפוֹ תָּמִיד בִּשְׁעַת בִּיאָה וּפוֹרֵשׁ מֵאִשְׁתּוֹ וְנִמְצֵאתָ מְבַטֵּל בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל מִפְּרִיָּה וּרְבִיָּה:


דיין שעתן – women who see blood know their time to make unclean/defile people who were pure who came in contact with them at the time of their seeing [blood] and onward, and we don’t say from before this also there was blood and the sides/walls of the womb preserved it and that she was [ritually] impure beforehand, for all women defile in the vagina and even though blood did not come outside, as it is written (Leviticus 15:19): “[When a woman has a discharge,] her discharge being blood from her body, [she shall remain in her menstrual impurity seven days; whoever touches her shall be impure until evening,],” even though it is in her body and did not come outside, she is impure. And the reason of Shammai is that he was not concerned that perhaps before this there was blood, for if you say that certainly he (i.e., the husband) may always have scruples at the time of sexual intercourse and separate himself entirely from his wife (see Tractate Niddah 3b) and it is found that he would make the daughters of Israel idle from [the commandment] of “being fruitful and multiplying.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:3)


**מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה.** בָּדְקָה הַיּוֹם וּמָצְאָה טְהוֹרָה, וּבָדְקָה לְסוֹף שָׁבוּעַ וּמָצְאָה טְמֵאָה, חוֹשְׁשִׁין לְמַגָּעָהּ מִבְּדִיקָה רִאשׁוֹנָה וָאֵילָךְ, שֶׁמָּא עִם סִלּוּק יָדֶיהָ רָאֲתָה וְכָתְלֵי בֵּית הָרֶחֶם הֶעֱמִידוּהוּ. וּלְבִטּוּל פְּרִיָּה וּרְבִיָּה לֹא חָיְשִׁינַן, דְּדַוְקָא לְטָהֳרוֹת הוּא דִּמְטַמִּינַן לְהוּ מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה, וְלֹא לְבַעְלָהּ. וְשַׁמַּאי אוֹמֵר, לְטָהֳרוֹת נַמִּי לֹא מְטַמִּינַן, דְּאִם כֵּן לִבּוֹ נוֹקְפוֹ וּפוֹרֵשׁ נַמִּי מִתַּשְׁמִישׁ:


מפקידה לפקידה (from the present examination to the last one) – she examined herself today and found herself to be [ritually] pure, but she examined herself at the end of the week and found herself to be [ritually] impure. We are concerned about her contact from the first examination and onwards, lest at the moment she removed her hands, he saw her and the sides of the womb preserved it. But regarding being idle from engaging from “being fruitful and multiplying” we are not concerned, for specifically for [ritually] pure women is that we make them unclean from the present examination to the last one, but not for her husband. But Shammai states, that we also don’t make [ritually] pure women unclean, for if so, he (i.e., the husband) may always have scruples at the time of sexual intercourse and separate himself also from sexual relations [with his wife].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:4)


**לֹא כְדִבְרֵי זֶה.** שַׁמַּאי דְּמֵקֵל טְפֵי וְאֵינוֹ עוֹשֶׂה סְיָג לִדְבָרָיו:


לא כדברי זה – Shammai who is more lenient and doesn’t make a fence for his words.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:5)


**וְלֹא כְדִבְרֵי זֶה.** הִלֵּל דְּמַחְמִיר יוֹתֵר מִדַּאי. דְּוַדַּאי כֻּלֵּי הַאי יָמִים רַבִּים לֹא מוֹקְמִי כָּתְלֵי בֵּית הָרֶחֶם דָּם:


ולא כדברי זה – Hillel who is much too stringent, for certainly all those many days, we don’t maintain the sides/walls of the womb with blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:6)


**מֵעֵת לְעֵת מְמַעֵט עַל יַד מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה.** שְׁנֵי זְמַנִּים הֻזְכְּרוּ בְּאִשָּׁה לְטַמֵּא טָהֳרוֹת לְמַפְרֵעַ, וְהַלֵּךְ אַחַר הַקַּל שֶׁבִּשְׁנֵיהֶן. אִם מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה יוֹתֵר עַל מֵעֵת לְעֵת, הַלֵּךְ אַחַר מֵעֵת לְעֵת וְלֹא תְּטַמֵּא אֶלָּא טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנָּגְעָה מֵאֶתְמוֹל בַּשָּׁעָה הַזֹּאת. וְאִם מֵעֵת לְעֵת יוֹתֵר עַל מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁבָּדְקָה עַצְמָהּ שַׁחֲרִית וּמָצְאָה טְהוֹרָה וְעַרְבִית מָצְאָה טְמֵאָה, לֹא מְטַמְּאִינַן אֶלָּא הָנָךְ טָהֳרוֹת דְּמִבְּדִיקַת שַׁחֲרִית וָאֵילָךְ. וְטַעֲמַיְהוּ דְּרַבָּנָן, לְפִי שֶׁחֲכָמִים תִּקְּנוּ לִבְנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בּוֹדְקוֹת שַׁחֲרִית וְעַרְבִית, שַׁחֲרִית לְהַכְשִׁיר טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁל לַיְלָה, וְעַרְבִית לְהַכְשִׁיר טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁל יוֹם. וְאִם בָּדְקָה שַׁחֲרִית וּמָצְאָה טְהוֹרָה, עַרְבִית וּמָצְאָה טְמֵאָה, הִפְסִידָה טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁל יוֹם, וְזוֹ שֶׁיָּשְׁבָה יָמִים רַבִּים וְלֹא בָּדְקָה כְּתַקָּנַת חֲכָמִים, הִפְסִידָה עוֹנָה אַחַת יְתֵרָה, מִשּׁוּם קְנָס. וּכְדֵי לְהַשְׁווֹת מִדּוֹתֵיהֶם שֶׁל חֲכָמִים שֶׁלֹּא תַּחֲלֹק בֵּין הֵיכָא דְּבָדְקָה שַׁחֲרִית לְהֵיכָא דְּבָדְקָה בַּצָהֳרָיִם, לֹא רָצוּ לְחַלֵּק וּלְמַעֵט וּלְהוֹסִיף בַּזְּמַן, וְנָתְנוּ זְמַן קָבוּעַ מֵעֵת לְעֵת, שֶׁלֹּא לִתֵּן דִּבְרֵיהֶם לְשִׁעוּרִין. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


מעת לעת ממעטת על יד מפקידה לפקידה (the term of twenty-four astronomical hours – of retrospective uncleanness – reduces the term of the interval from one examination to the other – i.e., we go by the shorter term whichever it may be) – two times that were mentioned regarding a woman to defile those who are pure retroactively, and we go according to the more lenient that is in both of them. If the period from the present examination to the last one is greater than the period of twenty-four astronomical hours, follow after the period of twenty-four astronomical hours and she does not defile other than those pure individuals that she came in contact with from yesterday at this time. But if the period of twenty-four astronomical hours is greater than from the present examination to the last one, as for example, she examined herself in the morning and found herself to be pure and in the evening, she found herself to be impure, we do not defile any other than those pure people that were from the examination in the morning and onward. And the reason of the Rabbis, is because the Sages ordained for the daughters of Israel would examine [themselves] I the morning and the evening, in the morning to permit/make fit the purity of the night, and in the evening to permit/make fit the purity of the morning. But if she examined [herself] in the morning and found herself to be pure, and in the evening and found herself to be impure, she lost the purity of the day, and this one [woman] who sat many days and did not examine herself according to the ordinance of the Sages, she lost one additional period, because of a fine, and in order to make the standards of the Sages consistent, so that there will be no dispute between whether she examined herself in the morning and where she examined herself in the afternoon, they did not want to distinguish or to eliminate or to add at the time. And they provided a fixed time, a period of twenty-four astronomical hours, to not have a rule that would vary according to circumstance [and not be the same for all. And the Halakha is according to the Sages (see Talmud Niddah 4b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:7)


**עַל יַד.** אַחַר. כְּמוֹ וְעַל יָדוֹ הֶחֱזִיק, שֶׁהוּא כְּמוֹ וְאַחֲרָיו, בְּסֵפֶר עֶזְרָא [נְחֶמְיָה ג]:


על יד – after, like )Nehemiah 3:8 – and not as it states, Chapter 3 of Ezra): “Next to them”/ועל ידו החזיק [Uzziel son of Harhiah, of the smiths repaired],” which is like “and after him.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:8)


**וֶסֶת.** קָבוּעַ, שֶׁנִּקְבַּע לָהּ זְמַן הָאֹרַח שָׁלֹשׁ פְּעָמִים וּבָדְקָה בִּשְׁעַת וֶסְתָּהּ וּמָצְאָה טְמֵאָה:


וסת (regular date/symptoms of menstruation) – fixed , that it was established for her the times of menstruation, and she examined herself at the time of menstruation and found herself to be impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:9)


**דַּיָּהּ שְׁעָתָהּ.** וְלֹא חָיְשִׁינַן דִּלְמָא מִקַּמֵּי הָכִי הֲוָה, דְּוַדַּאי אֹרַח בִּזְמַנּוֹ בָּא:


דיה שעתה – but we are not concerned that perhaps it was before this time, for certainly menstruation comes at its [set] time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:10)


**וְהַמְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בְּעִדִּים.** שֶׁבָּדְקָה עַצְמָהּ בְּעִדִּים, דְּהַיְנוּ בְּסָדִין, קֹדֶם תַּשְׁמִישׁ וּלְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


והמשמשת בעדים – she examined herself with “witnesses,” which are with a sheet prior to sexual intercourse, and after sexual intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:1:11)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ כִפְקִידָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי לְמַתְנִיתִין דְּתַרְתֵּי קָתָנֵי וְהָכִי קָאָמַר, וּמְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בְּעִדִּים כְּלוֹמַר מִצְוָה עַל אִשָּׁה שֶׁתְּשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּשְׁנֵי עִדִּים, אֶחָד לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ וְאֶחָד לְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ. וַהֲרֵי זוֹ כִּפְקִידָה, הָעֵד שֶׁל אַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ הֲרֵי הוּא כִּפְקִידָה. וּמְמַעֶטֶת עַל יַד מֵעֵת לְעֵת וְעַל יַד מִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה, דְּמַהוּ דְתֵימָא שֶׁמָּא תִּרְאֶה טִפַּת דָּם כְּחַרְדָּל וּתְחַפֶּנָּה שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע וְלֹא לֶהֱוֵי כִּפְקִידָה, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן. וְאִם שִׁמְּשָׁה בַּצָהֳרַיִם בְּעִדִּים, וּבָדְקָה אַחֲרֵי כֵן בָּעֶרֶב וּמָצְאָה טְמֵאָה, לֹא מְטַמְּאִינַן טָהֳרוֹת דְּמִבְּדִיקַת שַׁחֲרִית עַד תַּשְׁמִישׁ, שֶׁהֲרֵי בִּשְׁעַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ בָּדְקָה וּמָצְאָה טְהוֹרָה. וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְדִיקָה שֶׁקֹּדֶם תַּשְׁמִישׁ לָא חֲשִׁיבָא בְּדִיקָה מְעַלְּיָתָא, שֶׁמִּתּוֹךְ שֶׁהִיא בְּהוּלָה לְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֵינָהּ מַכְנֶסֶת הָעֵד לַחוֹרִים וְלַסְּדָקִין, בְּדִיקָה בָּעֵד שֶׁלְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ בְּדִיקָה מְעַלְּיָתָא הִיא וַחֲשִׁיבָא כִּפְקִידָה כְּדַאֲמַרַן:


הרי זה כפקידה – In the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 5a) it establishes that our Mishnah teaches two things, and this is what it said: and a piece of cloth used by women for ascertaining their condition of cleanness or uncleanness, that is to say, it is a Mitzvah upon the woman that she should use two pieces of cloth (i.e., literally, two witnesses), one prior to sexual intercourse and one following sexual intercourse. And that is like an examination. And it reduces the term of the interval from one astronomical period of twenty-four hours and through a present examination to a past examination. For you might have thought lest she see a drop of blood like a grain of mustard and it will release an effusion of semen and it will not be like an examination. Therefore, it comes to teach us that this is not the case. But if she had sexual intercourse in the afternoon with “testimony” (i.e., a piece of cloth that she used to examine herself beforehand), and she examined herself afterwards in the evening and found herself to be impure. We do not defile those with purity from her examination in the morning until her sexual intercourse, for behold, at the time of her sexual intercourse she examined herself and found herself to be pure. But even though that she examined herself prior to sexual intercourse it is not considered to be a good examination, for in the midst that she was hurrying to engage in sexual intercourse, she doesn’t bring in/pas the cloth/sheet (i.e., the “witness”) to the holes and slits, an examination with a “witness” (i.e., the cloth or sheet) is a good examination, and is considered like an examination that we mentioned.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:1)


**הָיְתָה יוֹשֶׁבֶת בַּמִּטָּה וַעֲסוּקָה בְטָהֳרוֹת.** הָא דְּלֹא תָּנֵי הָיְתָה עֲסוּקָה בְּטָהֳרוֹת וּפָרְשָׁה וְרָאֲתָה, וְקָתָנֵי הָיְתָה יוֹשֶׁבֶת בַּמִּטָּה, לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן דְּמִשּׁוּם דְּיֵשׁ לָהּ וֶסֶת וְדַיָּהּ שַׁעְתָּהּ, הַמִּטָּה נַמִּי טְהוֹרָה, כִּדְּקָתָנֵי וְכֻלָּן טְהוֹרוֹת, הָא אִם אֵין לָהּ וֶסֶת, דִּמְטַמִּינַן מֵעֵת לְעֵת, הַמִּטָּה נַמִּי טְמֵאָה, כְּמִשְׁכַּב נִדָּה עַצְמָהּ שֶׁמְּטַמְּאָה טֻמְאָה חֲמוּרָה לְטַמֵּא אָדָם לְטַמֵּא בְּגָדִים שֶׁעָלָיו:


היתה יושבת במטה ועסוקה בטהרות – but that it doesn’t teach [in the Mishnah] that she was engaged with [ritual] purity and she arose/turned aside and saw it (i.e., a drop of blood). And it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches that she was sitting on the bed, to inform/teach us that because she has regular premonitory systems of menstruation and she know her regular date, the bed is also pure, as it is taught, “and all of them are pure.” But if she doesn’t have a premonitory system of menstruation, that we declare her unclean from one astronomical period of twenty-four hours to another, the bed would also be impure, like the uncleanness of a menstruant woman that defiles a more serious uncleanness to defile a man and to defile the clothing that is upon him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:2)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָמְרוּ.** אִשָּׁה שֶׁאֵין לָהּ וֶסֶת מְטַמְּאָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת:


אע"פ שאמרו – that a woman who does not have a premonitory system of menstruation renders unclean [whatever she has touched] during the preceding twenty-four hour period.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:2:3)


**אֵינָהּ מוֹנָה.** יְמֵי נִדָּה אֶלָּא מִשָּׁעָה שֶׁרָאֲתָה:


אינה מונה – [she doesn’t count] the days of her being a menstruating woman other than from the time that she saw it (i.e., the drop of blood).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:3:1)


**אַרְבַּע נָשִׁים דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן.** דְּאֵינָן רְגִילוֹת לִרְאוֹת דָּם:


ארבע נשים דיין שעתן – that the don’t regularly see blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:3:2)


**אֲבָל הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר.** וְהָכִי מַסִּיק בַּגְּמָרָא דַּהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


אבל הלכה כרבי אליעזר – and this is what comes out from the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 7b) that the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:1)


**מִשֶּׁיִּוָּדַע עֻבָּרָהּ.** לְסוֹף שְׁלֹשָׁה חֳדָשִׁים מִיּוֹם שֶׁנִּתְעַבְּרָה:


משיוודע עוברה – at the end of three months from the day that she became pregnant.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:2)


**עַד שֶׁתִּגְמֹל.** עַד שֶׁיַּשְׁלִים לִבְנָהּ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֹדֶשׁ. וַאֲפִלּוּ מֵת בְּתוֹךְ עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֹדֶשׁ, דַּיָּהּ שַׁעְתָּהּ, שֶׁהַיּוֹלֶדֶת אֵבָרֶיהָ מִתְפָּרְקִים וְאֵין דָּמָהּ חוֹזֵר עַד אַחַר עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֹדֶשׁ. וּמִשָּׁם וָאֵילָךְ הֲרֵי הִיא כְּכָל הַנָּשִׁים וּמְטַמְּאָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת וּמִפְּקִידָה לִפְקִידָה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמֵּנִיקָה וְהוֹלֶכֶת אַרְבַּע וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנִים:


עד שתגמול – until will be completed for her son twenty-four months (i.e., two years). And even if he died within twenty-four months, sufficient for her is her time, for the woman who gives birth (and has been in confinement), her limbs are loose/shaky and her full strength (literally, “her blood”) doesn’t return until after twenty-four mouths (see Talmud Niddah 9a). But from then and onward, behold she is like all the women and becomes defiled in the time of twenty-four astronomical hours or from one examination to a previous examination, and even though she is continually nursing four or five years.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:4:3)


**גְּמָלַתּוּ אוֹ מֵת רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר מְטַמְּאָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי מֵאִיר דָּם נֶעְכָּר וְנַעֲשָׂה חָלָב. וּכְשֶׁאֵינָהּ מֵנִיקָה, אֲפִלּוּ קֹדֶם עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבָּעָה חֹדֶשׁ חוֹזֵר הַדָּם לִמְקוֹמוֹ וְחוֹזֶרֶת לִהְיוֹת רוֹאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


וגמלתו או מת ר' מאיר אומר מטמאה מפקידה לפקידה – for he (i.e., Rabbi Meir) holds that blood is disturbed (i.e., decomposed) and turns into milk (see Talmud Niddah 9a). But when she is not nursing, even prior to twenty-four months, the blood returns to its place and returns to be menstruating.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:1)


**כֹּל שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שָׁלֹשׁ עוֹנוֹת.** עוֹנָה בֵּינוֹנִית הִיא שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם. וּמִכֵּיוָן שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שָׁלֹשׁ עוֹנוֹת בֵּינוֹנִיּוֹת אַחַר שֶׁהִגִּיעָה סָמוּךְ לְזִקְנָתָהּ, דַּיָּהּ שַׁעְתָּהּ. וְסָמוּךְ לְזִקְנָתָהּ נִקְרָא, כָּל שֶׁקּוֹרִים לָהּ אִימָא אִימָא מֵחֲמַת זִקְנָתָהּ וְאֵינָהּ מַקְפֶּדֶת:


כל שעברו עליה שלש עונות – an intermediate-size period is thirty days. And since that these three intermediate-sized periods have gone by for her following her arrival at being close to old age, sufficient for her is her time. And she is called being close to old age, for all who call her: “Mommy, Mommy,” on account of her old age but she does mind.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:2)


**כָּל אִשָּׁה.** וַאֲפִלּוּ אֵינָהּ זְקֵנָה, וְעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שָׁלֹשׁ עוֹנוֹת וְלֹא רָאֲתָה, דַּיָּהּ שַׁעְתָּהּ:


כל אשה – [every woman] and even one who is not old and for whom three periods have passed and she did not see it (i.e., a drop of blood), sufficient for her is her time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:5:3)


**מְעֻבֶּרֶת וּמֵנִיקָה שֶׁעָבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶן שָׁלֹשׁ עוֹנוֹת.** אֲבָל לֹא עָבְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶן, לֹא. וּדְלֹא כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר מְעֻבֶּרֶת מִשֶּׁיִּוָּדַע הָעֻבָּר, וּמֵנִיקָה מִשֶּׁנּוֹלַד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה לֹא כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר וְלֹא כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


מעוברת ומניק שעברו עליהן שלש עונות – but not if for whom they have not passed, for it is not like the first Tanna/teacher who said (see Mishnah 4): “Once it is known that the fetus is present,” and a nursing mother once he has been born. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Eliezer nor according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:1)


**וּבַמֶּה אָמְרוּ.** אַרְבַּע נָשִׁים דַּיָּן שְׁעָתָן:


ובמה אמרו – that four women [fall into the category of those for whom the] time [of first seeing blood] suffices (see Mishnah 3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:2)


**בִּרְאִיָה רִאשׁוֹנָה.** שֶׁרָאֲתָה הַבְּתוּלָה, וְהַמְעֻבֶּרֶת וְהַמֵּנִיקָה, וְהַזְּקֵנָה לְאַחַר שֶׁעָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שָׁלֹשׁ עוֹנוֹת מֵחֲמַת זִקְנָתָהּ:


בראיה ראשונה - that she saw it (i.e., blood) as a virgin, and the pregnant woman [once it is known that the fetus is present] and the nursing mother [when her son is weaned], and the old woman after three periods have gone by for her on account of her old age.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:3)


**אֲבָל בַּשְּׁנִיָּה מְטַמְּאָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** שֶׁהֲרֵי חָזְרָה לְקַדְמוּתָהּ לִהְיוֹת דָּמֶיהָ מְצוּיִין בָּהּ:


אבל בשניה מטמאה מעת לעת – that she has returned to her previous condition that her blood would be found in her.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:6:4)


**מֵאֹנֶס.** כְּגוֹן דְּקָפְצָה וְרָאֲתָה, אוֹ מֵחֲמַת פַּחַד, אוֹ שֶׁאָכְלָה דָּבָר הַמַּגִּיר דָּם:


מאונס – as for example that she got excited and saw it (i.e., blood) or on account of fear, or that she consumed something which stirs up the blood.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:1)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאָמְרוּ.** אִשָּׁה שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ וֶסֶת דַּיָּהּ שַׁעְתָּהּ:


אע"פ שאמרו – a woman who has regular premonitory symptoms of menstruation sufficient for her is time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:2)


**צְרִיכָה לִהְיוֹת בּוֹדֶקֶת.** בְּכָל יוֹם שַׁחֲרִית וְעַרְבִית כִּשְׁאָר נָשִׁים:


צריכה להיות בודקת – on every day in the morning and in the evening like the other women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:3)


**חוּץ מִן הַנִּדָּה.** מִשֶּׁרָאֲתָה יוֹם אֶחָד, טְמֵאָה שִׁבְעָה, וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה בְּדִיקָה כָּל שִׁבְעָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּלֹא רְאִיָּה הִיא טְמֵאָה כָּל שִׁבְעָה:


חוץ מן הנדה – from when she saw it (i.e., blood) on day, she is unclean for seven days and does not need an examination all seven [days], for without an attack she is unclean all seven [days].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:4)


**וְהַיּוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל דַּם טֹהַר.** דְּלָמָּה לָהּ בְּדִיקָה, אֲפִלּוּ תִּרְאֶה, דַּם טֹהַר הוּא:


והיושבת על דם טוהר – for why should there be an examination for her, for even if she would sight it (i.e., blood), it is h blood of purity (i.e., vaginal bleeding experienced by a woman from eight to forty days after giving birth to a boy and from fifteen to eighty days after giving birth to a girl. This blood does not render a woman ritually impure, according to the Torah, even though current Halakhic practice renders a woman ritually impure).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:5)


**וּמְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת בְּעִדִּים.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ וֶסֶת, צְרִיכָה לִבְדֹּק לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ וּלְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁהִיא עֲסוּקָה בְּטָהֳרוֹת, דְּמִגּוֹ דְּבָעֲיָא בְּדִיקָה לְטָהֳרוֹת, בַּעְיָא נַמִּי בְּדִיקָה לְבַעְלָהּ. אֲבָל אִשָּׁה שֶׁאֵינָהּ עֲסוּקָה בְּטָהֳרוֹת, לְבַעְלָהּ לֹא בַּעְיָא בְּדִיקָה:


ומשמשת בעדים – even though she has premonitory symptoms of menstruation , she needs to examine [herself] before sexual intercourse and after sexual intercourse [with her husband]. And especially if she was engaged with [ritual] purity, for since requires examination for [ritual] purity, she requires examination also for her husband. But a woman who is not engaged with [ritual] purity, for her husband she does not require examination.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:6)


**וּבְתוּלָה שֶׁדָּמֶיהָ טְהוֹרִים.** אַרְבַּע לֵילוֹת לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי, וּלְבֵית הִלֵּל עַד שֶׁתִּחְיֶה הַמַּכָּה שֶׁל בְּעִילָה, וְהִיא אֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לְשַׁמֵּשׁ בְּעִדִּים, שֶׁהֲרֵי דָּמָהּ טָהוֹר, וַאֲפִלּוּ נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאִית דָּמֶיהָ, הוֹאִיל וְשִׁמְּשָׁה, דְּמִשּׁוּם תַּשְׁמִישׁ נִשְׁתַּנָּה. אֲבָל שַׁחֲרִית וְעַרְבִית, צְרִיכָה בְּדִיקָה הֵיכָא דְּלֹא שִׁמְּשָׁה אַחַר בְּעִילָה רִאשׁוֹנָה, שֶׁמָּא נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵה דָמִים שֶׁלָּהּ מִמַּרְאֵה דַם בְּעִילָה רִאשׁוֹנָה. וְאִם נִשְׁתַּנּוּ מַרְאֵה דָמִים שֶׁלָּהּ, טְמֵאָה, שֶׁרַגְלַיִם לַדָּבָר שֶׁאֵין זֶה דַּם בְּתוּלִים:


ובתולה שדמיה טהורים – four nights according to the School of Shammai, but according to the School of Hillel until the wound of sexual intercourse should heal, and she does not have to use pieces of cloth (literally, “witnesses”), for her blood is pure, and if the appearance of her blood has changed, for since she engaged in sexual intercourse, for on account of sexual intercourse, it changed, but in the morning and in the evening , she requires examination where she didn’t engage in sexual intercourse after the first coition, lest the appearance of her blood changed from her first coition. But if her blood changed in appearance, she is impure/unclean, for we are accustomed to speak where this is not the blood of a virgin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:7)


**שַׁחֲרִית וּבֵין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת.** שַׁחֲרִית לְהַכְשִׁיר טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁל לַיְלָה, שֶׁאִם תִּמְצָא טְהוֹרָה תֵּדַע שֶׁלֹּא רָאֲתָה בַּלַּיְלָה, וְלִכְשֶׁתִּמְצָא עַרְבִית טְמֵאָה יָצְאוּ אֵלּוּ מִסָּפֵק. וְכֵן בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת לְהַכְשִׁיר טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנִּתְעַסְּקָה בָּהֶן בַּיּוֹם:


שחרית ובין השמשות – in the morning to pronounce fit/kosher the purities of the night, that if she would find herself to be pure, she would know that she didn’t see it (i.e., blood) in the night, and when she minds in the evening that she is impure/unclean, these leave the realm of doubt, and similarly at twilight to pronounce fit/kosher the purities that she was engaged in during the daytime.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:8)


**וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁהִיא עוֹבֶרֶת לְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת בֵּיתָהּ.** שֶׁמְּכִינָה עַצְמָהּ לְשַׁמֵּשׁ עִם בַּעְלָהּ:


ובשעה שהיא עוברת לשמש את ביתה - when she prepares herself to engage in sexual relations with her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 1:7:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/1:7:9)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר אַף בִּשְׁעַת עֲבָרָתָן מִלֶּאֱכֹל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** אַף לְאַחַר שֶׁאָכְלוּ בַּתְּרוּמָה, צְרִיכוֹת לִבְדֹּק עַצְמָן, לְתַקֵּן שְׁיָרֵי תְּרוּמוֹת שֶׁבִּפְנִים. שֶׁאִם תִּמְצָא עַכְשָׁיו טְהוֹרָה, כְּשֶׁתִּבְדֹּק עַרְבִית כְּמִשְׁפָּטָהּ וְתִמְצָא טְמֵאָה, לֹא יִהְיֶה שְׁיָרֵי הַתְּרוּמָה בִּכְלַל טֻמְאָה לְמַפְרֵעַ, שֶׁהֲרֵי בָּדְקָה לְאַחַר הַעֲבָרָתָהּ. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר אף בשעת עברתן מלאכול מתרומה – even after they have consumed heave-offering/the priest’s due (i.e., if her husband is a Kohen), she needs to examine herself, to repair the remnants of heave-offerings that are before her that if she finds now as [ritually] pure., when she examines in the evening according to her appropriate manner and she finds that she is impure, there will not be remnants of the heave-offering at all that were retroactively impure, for she examined herself after finishing. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



## Chapter 2


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:1)


**כָּל הַיָּד הַמַּרְבָּה לִבְדֹּק.** שֶׁבּוֹדֶקֶת תָּמִיד שֶׁמָּא רָאֲתָה:


כל היד המרבה לבדוק – that always examines [herself] lest she see it (i.e., blood).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:2)


**וּבַאֲנָשִׁים.** שֶׁבּוֹדֵק עַצְמוֹ תָּמִיד בְּאַמָּתוֹ, שֶׁמָּא יָצָא מִמֶּנּוּ קֶרִי:


ובאנשים – [a man] who always examines himself in his membrum virile (see Tractate Niddah 13a), lest his emission went out from him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:3)


**תִּקָּצֵץ.** שֶׁמִּתְחַמֵּם וּמַרְגִּישׁ כְּשֶׁמְּמַשְׁמֵשׁ בָּאַמָּה, וּמוֹצִיא שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע לְבַטָּלָה:


תקצץ (should be cut off) – who warms himself and feels his membrum virile when he warms himself (i.e., masturbates), and removes/spills semen in vain.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:4)


**וְהַסּוּמָא.** אֵינָהּ מִשְׁנָה וּדְחוּיָה הִיא. אֶלָּא הִיא בּוֹדֶקֶת לְעַצְמָהּ וּמַרְאָה לְחַבְרוֹתֶיהָ:


וסומא – but it is not Mishnah and is inoperative/suspended, but rather, she examines herself and shows it to her friends.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:5)


**וְשֶׁנִּטְרְפָה דַעְתָּהּ.** מֵחֲמַת חֹלִי:


ושנטרפה דעתה (confused, not fully conscious) – on account of illness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:6)


**מְתַקְּנוֹת אוֹתָן.** בּוֹדְקוֹת אוֹתָן וּמַטְבִּילוֹת אוֹתָן:


מתקינות אותן – they examine them (i.e., those who are confused/not fully conscious) and immerse them [as needed].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:1:7)


**הָכִי גָּרְסִינַן, הַצְּנוּעוֹת מְתַקְּנוֹת לָהֶן שְׁלִישִׁי לְתַקֵּן אֶת הַבָּיִת.** כְּלוֹמַר לִבְדֹּק לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ וּלְהָכִין עַצְמָהּ לְבַעְלָהּ. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל הַנָּשִׁים כְּדִתְנַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁהִיא עוֹבֶרֶת לְשַׁמֵּשׁ אֶת בֵּיתָהּ, וְהָא דְּנָקַט צְנוּעוֹת, מִשּׁוּם דִּצְנוּעוֹת עֵד שֶׁבּוֹדֶקֶת בּוֹ לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ זֶה אֵין בּוֹדְקוֹת בּוֹ לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ אַחֵר, לְפִי שֶׁמְּטֻנָּף וְכָהָה מַרְאִית לִבּוּנוֹ בִּבְדִיקָה רִאשׁוֹנָה, וְשׁוּב אֵין נִרְאֵית בּוֹ טִפָּה כְּחַרְדָּל:


And this is the reading: הצנועות מתקנות להן שלישי לתקן את הבית – that is to say, to examine before sexual intercourse and to prepare herself for her husband, and the same law applies for all women as is taught in the first chapter [of Tractate Niddah] and at the time that she passes to passes to have intercourse with her husband, for it (i.e., the Mishnah) took [the terminology], "צנועות"/modest (i.e., pious) the cloth (literally, “the witness”) that she examines herself before this intercourse she does not examine herself with it for another intercourse because it is soiled/filthy and dull/shaded in its white appearance with the first examination, and furthermore, the drop [of blood] like mustard is not seen.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:1)


**נִמְצָא דָם עַל שֶׁלּוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ שָׁהָה זְמַן אָרֹךְ לְאַחַר בְּעִילָה קֹדֶם קִנּוּחַ, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁהָיָה דָּם בִּשְׁעַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ:


נמצא על שלו – even if he waited a long time after coition prior to wiping, it is known that there was blood at the time of sexual intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:2)


**טְמֵאִין.** שְׁנֵיהֶם, טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, כְּדִין בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה:


טמאין – both of them (i.e., the husband and the wife) are defiled for seven days, like the law of someone who has intercourse with a menstruant woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:3)


**אוֹתְיוֹם.** תֵּכֶף. וְאֵין לוֹ דּוֹמֶה בַּמִּשְׁנָה:


אותיום – immediately, and there is no [term] similar to this in the Mishnah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:4)


**טְמֵאִים מִסָּפֵק.** שְׁנֵיהֶם טְמֵאִים שִׁבְעָה יָמִים, וּמִיהוּ טֻמְאַת סָפֵק הִיא לִתְלוֹת אֲבָל לֹא לִשְׂרוֹף:


טמאים מספק – both of them are impure for seven days. But however, it is a doubtful impurity [in that she doubtfully saw blood at the time of intercourse and/or didn’t see blood at the time of intercourse, but only after the sexual intercourse) leaves it in abeyance but not to burn [a sin-offering].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:2:5)


**וּפְטוּרִים מִן הַקָּרְבָּן.** מֵחַטָּאת קָבוּעַ, אֲבָל חַיָּבִים בְּאָשָׁם תָּלוּי:


ופטורים מן הקרבן – from a regular sin-offering, but they are liable for an uncertain guilt-offering [brought by one who is uncertain as to whether he committed a sin that requires a sin-offering; it was also called the guilt-offering of the pious].



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:1)


**וְתָדִיחַ אֶת פָּנֶיהָ.** קִנּוּחַ שֶׁלְּמַטָּה:


ותדיח את פניה – wiping that of her face, i.e., her pubic area.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:2)


**וְאַחַר כָּךְ.** כְּלוֹמַר אִם שָׁהֲתָה יוֹתֵר מִשִּׁעוּר זֶה:


ואח"כ – meaning to say, if she delayed more than this measure [of time] (see Talmud Niddah 14b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:3)


**מְטַמְּאָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנִּתְעַסְּקָה בָּהֶן. כְּדִין מֵעֵת לְעֵת דְּאָמְרִינַן בְּפֶרֶק קַמָּא:


מטמאה מעת לעת- [ritually] pure things that she was engaged with. Like the law of checking every astronomical period of twenty-four hours as we stated in the first chapter (Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:4)


**וְאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ.** טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, אֶלָּא טֻמְאַת עֶרֶב כְּדִין כָּל נוֹגֵעַ בְּנִדָּה:


ואינה מטמאה את בועלה –[she does not impart uncleanness to him who had sexual relations with her] for a defilement of seven days, but rather, the defilement of one evening, according to the law of anyone who comes in contact with a menstruant woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:5)


**רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא אוֹמֵר אַף מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי עֲקִיבָא כָּל מֵעֵת לְעֵת מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה מִסָּפֵק. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא:


ר' עקיבא אומר אף מטמאה את בועלה – that Rabbi Akiva holds that every astronomical period of twenty-four hours she defiles the person who had sexual relations with her a doubtful seven day period of defilement. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Akiva.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:3:6)


**וּמוֹדִים חֲכָמִים לְרַבִּי עֲקִיבָא בְּרוֹאָה כֶתֶם.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְטֻמְאַת מֵעֵת לְעֵת אָמְרוּ חֲכָמִים שֶׁאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ לְפִי שֶׁטֻּמְאַת מֵעֵת לְעֵת אֵינָהּ אֶלָּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן, מוֹדִים הֵם בְּרוֹאָה כֶּתֶם, דְּאַף עַל גַּב דְּטֻמְאַת כְּתָמִים דְּרַבָּנָן, מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ טֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה:


ומודים חכמים לר' עקיבא ברואה כתם (a blood stain on part of her clothing where it is attributable to uterine bleeding) – and even though that in the defilement of an astronomical period of twenty-four hours the Sages stated that she does not defile the person who had sexual relations with her because the defilement of an astronomical period of twenty-four hours is not other than only from the Rabbis, they agree with regard to seeing a blood stain on her clothing, that even though the defilement of a blood stain is according to the Rabbis, it defiles the person who had sexual relations with her a seven day period of [ritual] defilement.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:1)


**כָּל הַנָּשִׁים בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה לְבַעֲלֵיהֶן.** וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לִשְׁאֹל לָהּ אִם הִיא טְהוֹרָה. וְהָנֵי מִילֵי, כְּשֶׁהִנִּיחָהּ בְּחֶזְקַת טְהוֹרָה:


כל הנשים בחזקת טהרה לבעליהן – and he doesn’t need to ask her if she is [ritually] pure. And these words [apply], when he left her in a presumption/state of [ritual] purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:2)


**הַבָּאִין מִן הַדֶּרֶךְ נְשֵׁיהֶן לָהֶן בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה.** דְּסָלְקָא דַעְתָּךְ אֲמֵינָא הָנֵי מִילֵי הֵיכָא דְּאִיתֵיהּ בְּמָתָא, דְּרַמְיָא אַנַּפְשַׁהּ וּבָדְקָה, אֲבָל הֵיכָא דְּלֵיתֵיהּ בְּמָתָא, דְּלֹא רַמְיָא אַנַּפְשַׁהּ, לֹא, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


הבאין מן הדרך נשיהן להן בחזקת טהורה – When they come from the road, their wives are presumed to be in a state of purity. You might have thought that this applies only when the husband is in the city with his wife, as she can check herself and verify her status; but when the husband is not in the city and she cannot check herself, maybe the presumption would not apply. This teaches us that even in such a situation, her status is still presumed to be pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:3)


**שְׁנֵי עִדִּים עַל כָּל תַּשְׁמִישׁ.** אֶחָד לְפָנָיו וְאֶחָד לְאַחֲרָיו, וּלְמָחָר תְּעַיֵּן בָּהֶם:


Two pieces of cloth for every sexual intercourse. One before and one afterwards, and on the morrow, she will examine them (see Tractate Niddah 15a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:4)


**אוֹ תְשַׁמֵּשׁ לְאוֹר הַנֵּר.** וְתִבְדֹּק לְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ עֵד שֶׁקִּנְּחָה בּוֹ:


או תשמש לאור הנר – and she will heck after sexual intercourse until she wipes herself with it (i.e., the cloth).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:4:5)


**דַּיָּהּ בִּשְׁנֵי עִדִּים כָּל הַלָּיְלָה.** אֶחָד לִפְנֵי תַּשְׁמִישׁ רִאשׁוֹן, וְאֶחָד לְאַחַר תַּשְׁמִישׁ אַחֲרוֹן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִשְׁמְשָׁה וְלֹא יָדְעָה אִי רָאֲתָה דָּם בֵּינֵי וּבֵינֵי, בֵּין לְבֵית שַׁמַּאי בֵּין לְבֵית הִלֵּל לֹא אִכְפַּת לָן, דְּלֹא הֻצְרְכוּ אֶלָּא לְחֹמֶר טָהֳרוֹת:


דיה בשני עדים כל הלילה – one prior to the first sexual intercourse, and one after the final sexual intercourse. But even though that she did not know if she had seen blood between one and the other [act of sexual intercourse], whether according to the School of Shammai or whether according to the School of Hillel, it does not matter to us, for it was not needed other than for matter of [ritual] purity.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:1)


**הַחֶדֶר וְהָעֲלִיָּה וְהַפְּרוֹזְדוֹר.** חֶדֶר מִבִּפְנִים, וּפְרוֹזְדוֹר מִבַּחוּץ, שְׁנֵיהֶם זֶה אֵצֶל זֶה, חֶדֶר לְצַד אֲחוֹרֶיהָ, וּפְרוֹזְדוֹר לְפָנֶיהָ, וַעֲלִיָּה בְּנוּיָה עַל שְׁתֵּיהֶן, וְנֶקֶב יֵשׁ בֵּין עֲלִיָּה לַפְּרוֹזְדוֹר וְנִקְרָא לוּל. וּפְעָמִים יוֹרֵד דָּם מִן הָעֲלִיָּה לַפְּרוֹזְדוֹר דֶּרֶךְ הַלּוּל. וְכָתְלֵי רֶחֶם, לְמַטָּה בְּאֶמְצַע פְּרוֹזְדוֹר, וְדֶרֶךְ שָׁם דָּמִים יוֹצְאִים:


החדר והעליה והפרוזדור – the inner part of the female genitals/upper end of the vagina/uterus {חדר) from within, and the forepart of the female genitals/lower end of the vagina/uterus(פרוזדור) from without, both of them are one next to the other, with the inner part of the female genitals/upper end of the vagina/uterus(חדר) to the side in back of it, and the lower end of the vagina/uterus in front of it, and an upper chamber (עליה) built o top of both of them, and there is an hole/perforation (נקב) between it and the lower end of the vagina/uterus called a לול/a passage from the vestibulum vaginae, and sometimes blood comes from the upper chamber to the lower end of the vagina/uterus (פרוזדור) via the passage from the vestibulum vaginae (לול) . And the sides/walls of the womb are below in the middle of the lower end of the vagina/uterus (פרוזדור) and through there the blood exits.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:2)


**דַּם הַחֶדֶר טָמֵא.** דְּהַיְנוּ דַּם הַמָּקוֹר:


דם החדר טמא – that is the interior of the womb from where the menses are discharged (המקור) .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:3)


**סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא.** מִסָּפֵק טָמֵא טֻמְאָה וַדָּאִית:


ספיקו טמא – it is [ritually] impure from doubt of a definite impurity/defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:5:4)


**שֶׁחֶזְקָתוֹ מִן הַמָּקוֹר.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן שֶׁמָּא מִן הָעֲלִיָּה בָּא, וּדְמֵי הָעֲלִיָּה טְהוֹרִים הֵם, אֶלָּא עֲשָׂאוּהוּ כְּאִלּוּ וַדַּאי בָּא מִן הַמָּקוֹר. וְהָנֵי מִילֵי, כְּשֶׁנִּמְצָא מִן הַלּוּל וְלִפְנִים לְצַד הַחֶדֶר, אֲבָל נִמְצָא מִן הַלּוּל וְלַחוּץ, אֵינוֹ טָמֵא טֻמְאָה וַדָּאִית לִשְׂרוֹף, אֶלָּא סְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא לִתְלוֹת, שֶׁהֲרֵי שְׁנֵי דָּמִים יוֹצְאִין דֶּרֶךְ שָׁם, דְּמֵי הַחֶדֶר וּדְמֵי הָעֲלִיָּה, וְלֹא יָדְעִינַן מֵהֵי אֲתָא:


שחזקתו מן המקור – but we don’t say that perhaps it (i.e., the blood) comes from the upper chamber (עליה) for the blood of the upper chamber is pure, but they made it as if it definitely comes from the interior of the womb where the menses are discharged(המקור) . But these words [apply] when it is found from the vestibulum vaginae (לול) and internally to the side of the upper end of the vagina/uterus(חדר) , but if it (i.e., the blood) is found from the vestibulum vaginae (לול) and outwards, it is not impure a definitive defilement to burn it - but it is a doubtful defilement to hold it in abeyance, for two [types] of blood go out through that passage – the blood of the upper end of the vagina/uterus (חדר) and the blood of the upper chamber (עליה) – and we don’t know which comes (see also Maimonides’ Mishneh Torah, Hilkhot Issurei Biah, Chapter 5, Halakhot 3-5).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:1)


**חֲמִשָּׁה דָמִים טְמֵאִים.** דִּשְׁתֵּי פְּעָמִים כְּתִיב דָּמֶיהָ בַּקְּרָא בְּטֻמְאַת דָּמִים שֶׁבָּאִשָּׁה, (ויקרא כ) וְהִיא גִּלְּתָה אֶת מְקוֹר דָּמֶיהָ, (שם יב) וְטָהֲרָה מִמְּקֹר דָּמֶיהָ, וּמִעוּט דָּמֶיהָ שְׁנַיִם, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה דָמִים. אֹדֶם, וְקֶרֶן כַּרְכֹּם, וּמֵימֵי אֲדָמָה, וּמָזוּג. וְהַשָּׁחֹר בִּכְלַל אָדֹם הוּא, וְהָכִי אָמְרִינַן בַּגְּמָרָא, הַאי שָׁחֹר אָדֹם הוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁלָּקָה:


חמשה דמים טמאים – in Scripture it is written "דמיה"/her blood - twice regarding the defilement of the blood that is in a woman (Leviticus 20:18): “and she has exposed her blood flow”/והיא גלתה את-מקור דמיה" and (Leviticus 12:7): “she shall then be pure from her flow of blood”/וטהרה ממקר דמיה and the limitation of her blood is two, which makes four bloods: red, the color of bright-colored crocus, and [blood] which is like water mixed with earth and [blood] which is like water mixed with wine. But the black [color] is included within red, and this we state in the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 19a) this black is red but that it was afflicted by disease.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:2)


**וּכְקֶרֶן כַּרְכֹּם.** כְּמַרְאֵה זִיו הַכַּרְכֹּם. לְשׁוֹן כִּי קָרַן עוֹר פָּנָיו (שמות לד):


וכקרן כרכום – like the appearance of the splendor of crocus/saffron, the language of (Exodus 34:29): “that the skin of his (i.e., Moses’) face was radiant.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:3)


**וּכְמֵימֵי אֲדָמָה.** כֻּלָּן מְפָרֵשׁ לָהֶן לְקַמָּן בְּמַתְנִיתִין:


וכמימי אדמה – all of them our Mishnah explains further on (see Mishnah 7).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:4)


**וּכְמָזוּג.** כְּיַיִן אָדֹם הַמָּזוּג בְּמַיִם:


וכמזוג - like red wine that is mixed in water.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:5)


**כְּמֵימֵי תִלְתָּן.** מַיִם שֶׁשּׁוֹרִין בָּהֶן תִּלְתָּן. פינקיריק"ו בְּלַעַ"ז:


וכמימי תלתן – water that they steep in it fenugree. PINKEERIKO in the foreign language.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:6)


**וּכְמֵימֵי בָשָׂר צָלִי.** מֹחַל הַיּוֹצֵא מִבָּשָׂר צָלוּי:


וכמימי בשר צלי – thin secretion that comes out from roasted meat.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:7)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְטַהֲרִין.** וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחֲלוֹקוֹת בַּדָּבָר. תַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר חֲמִשָּׁה דָּמִים טְמֵאִים וְתוּ לֹא, סְבִירָא לֵיהּ דְּמֵימֵי תִּלְתָּן וּמֵי בָּשָׂר צָלִי תּוֹלִין, לֹא אוֹכְלִין וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִין. וּבֵית שַׁמַּאי מְטַמְּאִין, וְשׂוֹרְפִים עֲלֵיהֶם תְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים. וּבֵית הִלֵּל מְטַהֲרִין לְגַמְרֵי. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא, דְּתוֹלִין:


ובית הלל מתירין – There are three disputes in the matter. The first Tanna/teacher who stated that there are five impure bloods and nothing more. He holds that the waters of fenugreek and the waters of waters of roasted meat are suspended, we don’t eat nor do we burn them. But the School of Shammai defiles them, and burns upon them heave-offering and holy things. But the School of Hillel makes them completely pure. And the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher, who suspends them/holds them in abeyance.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:8)


**עֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל מְטַמֵּא.** דִּסְבִירָא לֵיהּ הַאי יָרֹק כְּקֶרֶן כַּרְכֹּם הוּא, אֶלָּא שֶׁלָּקָה. וְיָרֹק דִּמְטַמֵּא עֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל, דְּהַיְנוּ כְּמַרְאֵה אֶתְרוֹג, וְלֹא כְּמַרְאֵה כַּרְתִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ נוֹטֶה לְאַדְמִימוּת כְּלָל:


עקביא בן מהללאל מטמא (see also Tractate Eduyot, Chapter 5, Mishnah 6) – that he holds that this green is like the color of the bright-colored crocus, but that it was afflicted with disease. But the green that Akavya ben Mehalalel defiles this is like the appearance of the Etrog, but not like the appearance of leek-green stuff, which does not turn to a reddish color at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:9)


**וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים.** וְשָׁלֹשׁ מַחֲלוֹקוֹת נַמִּי בְּיָרֹק, תַּנָּא קַמָּא סָבַר תּוֹלִין לֹא אוֹכְלִים וְלֹא שׂוֹרְפִים, וַעֲקַבְיָא בֶּן מַהֲלַלְאֵל מְטַמֵּא וְשׂוֹרְפִים, וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִין וְאוֹכְלִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּתַנָּא קַמָּא:


וחכמים מטהרין – and there are three disputes also regarding the green. The first Tanna/teacher holds that we hold it in suspense/abeyance we don’t consume it nor do we burn it. But Akavya ben Mehalalel defiles it and burns it. And the Sages purify it and consume it. But the Halakha is according to the first Tanna/teacher.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:10)


**אִם אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם כֶּתֶם.** לִהְיוֹתוֹ חָשׁוּב מִן הַדָּמִים הַמְטַמְּאִין בָּאִשָּׁה:


אם אינו מטמא משום כתם(if I does not impart uncleanness because it is a bloodstain) – to be considered from among the bloods that defile a woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:11)


**מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם מַשְׁקֶה.** וְחָשׁוּב הוּא דָּם לְהַכְשִׁיר הַזְּרָעִים לְקַבֵּל טֻמְאָה כְּכָל שְׁאָר דָּם, שֶׁהוּא אֶחָד מִן הַשִּׁבְעָה מַשְׁקִין שֶׁמַּכְשִׁירִין אֶת הַזְּרָעִים:


מטמא משום משקה – and considered as blood to make seeds susceptible to receive ritual impurity like all the rest of blood. And it is one of the seven liquids that make the seeds susceptible (see Tracttae Makhshirin, Chapter 6, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:6:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:6:12)


**לֹא כָךְ וְלֹא כָךְ.** לֹא מְטַמֵּא מִשּׁוּם כֶּתֶם, וְלֹא מַכְשִׁיר אֶת הַזְּרָעִים. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי, וְהוּא חֲכָמִים דִּפְלִיגֵי אַעֲקַבְיָא:


לא כך ולא כך – it does not defile because of being a bloodstain, nor does it make seeds susceptible [to receive ritual impurity]. But the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi, and he is “the Sages” that dispute against Akavya [ben Mehalalel].



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:1)


**כְּדַם הַמַּכָּה.** כְּדָם שֶׁל שׁוֹר שָׁחוּט שֶׁהוּא אָדֹם הַרְבֵּה, וּבִתְחִלַּת הַכָּאָתָהּ שֶׁל סַכִּין, וְלֹא כַּדָּם שֶׁל כֻּלַּהּ שְׁחִיטָה, שֶׁדַּם שְׁחִיטָה מִשְׁתַּנֶּה וְהוֹלֵךְ. וְאִית דְּאָמְרֵי, כְּדַם הַמַּכָּה שֶׁל הַקָּזָה:


כדם המכה – like the blood of slaughtered bull which is very red. And at the beginning of the striking/assault of a knife, but not like the blood of all of the slaughtering [process], for the blood of the slaughtering continually changes. And there are those who say, like the blood of the wound of opening of the vein (i.e., the splashing blood).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:2)


**כַּחֶרֶת.** כְּמַרְאֵה הַדְּיוֹ שֶׁבְּשׁוּלֵי הַכְּלִי, שֶׁהוּא שָׁחֹר הַרְבֵּה, וְלֹא כְּמַרְאֵה הַדְּיוֹ שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁ הַכְּלִי שֶׁהוּא קָלוּשׁ וְאֵינוֹ שָׁחֹר כָּל כָּךְ. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, חֶרֶת אדרמינ"ט בְּלַעַ"ז, שֶׁצּוֹבְעִין בּוֹ הָעוֹרוֹת. כְּמוֹ חַרְתָּא דְאוּשְׁכָפֵי [דִּמְגִלָּה דַף יט.]:


כחרת (sediment of ink) – like the drop of ink that is at the bottom of the vessel/printer, which is very black, and not like the appearance of the ink at the top of the vessel which is thin/weak and not so black. Another explanation: חרת is ADARMONDIT in the foreign tongue, where they color the leather skins like the blacking used by shoemakers (see Tractate Shabbat 104b), that is deeper than this, which is deep like the appearance of blackness, meaning to say, blacker than sediment of ink.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:3)


**עָמֹק מִכָּן.** שֶׁעָמֹק בְּמַרְאֵה שַׁחֲרוּרִית, כְּלוֹמַר שָׁחֹר יוֹתֵר מֵחֶרֶת:


דיהה (fainter) – that its appearance is faint and not so black. And the same law applies for all the impure bloods, that in all of them, if deeper than this, it is impure, but fainter than this, it is pure, except for mixed wine, that whether deeper or whether fainter, is pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:4)


**דֵּהֶה.** שֶׁנִּדְהֵית מַרְאִיתוֹ וְאֵינוֹ שָׁחֹר כָּל כָּךְ. וְהוּא הַדִּין לְכָל דָּמִים טְמֵאִים, דִּבְכֻלְּהוּ עָמֹק מִכָּאן טָמֵא, דֵּהֶה מִכָּאן טָהוֹר, חוּץ מִיַּיִן מָזוּג, דְּבֵין עָמֹק בֵּין דֵּהֶה טָהוֹר:


כברור שבו (like the brightest shade in it)– that has bright leaves in a reddish appearance more than their andp examine other than in the middle row, and the middle leaf that is in the middle row is more praiseworthy of them all for examination.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:5)


**כַּבָּרוּר שֶׁבּוֹ.** שֶׁיֵּשׁ עָלִין בְּרוּרִים בְּמַרְאֵה אֲדַמְדּוּמִית יוֹתֵר מֵחַבְרֵיהֶן. וְשָׁלֹשׁ שׁוּרוֹת שֶׁל עָלִין יֵשׁ בַּכַּרְכֹּם, וּבְכָל שׁוּרָה שְׁלֹשָׁה עָלִין, וְאֵין בּוֹדְקִין אֶלָּא בַּשּׁוּרָה הָאֶמְצָעִית, וְהֶעָלֶה הָאֶמְצָעִי שֶׁבַּשּׁוּרָה הָאֶמְצָעִית, מְשֻׁבָּח שֶׁבְּכֻלָּן לִבְדִיקָה:


מבקעת בית הכרם – a [well -] known place in the Land of Israel whose name is Bet Kerem (see Tractate Middot, Chapter 3, Mishnah 4).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:6)


**מִבִּקְעַת בֵּית כֶּרֶם.** מָקוֹם יָדוּעַ בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁשְּׁמוֹ בֵּית כֶּרֶם:


ומציף מים – places water on the dirt, until the water floats on top of the dirt [from Bet Kerem] and we estimate them as turbid, but we don’t estimate them as clear-flowing.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:7)


**וּמֵצִיף מָיִם.** נוֹתֵן מַיִם עַל הֶעָפָר, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ הַמַּיִם צָפִין עַל גַּבֵּי הָאֲדָמָה. וּמְשַׁעֲרִין אוֹתָן עֲכוּרִים, וְאֵין מְשַׁעֲרִים אוֹתָן צְלוּלִים:


וכמזוג – how?


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 2:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/2:7:8)


**וּכְמָזוּג.** כֵּיצַד: **שְׁנֵי חֲלָקִים מַיִם וְאֶחָד יַיִן מִן הַיַּיִן הַשָּׁרוֹנִי.** הַבָּא מֵאֶרֶץ הַשָּׁרוֹן. מָקוֹם יָדוּעַ בְּאֶרֶץ יִשְׂרָאֵל. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, יַיִן הֶעָשׂוּי מִן הַגְּפָנִים הַנְּטוּעִים בַּבִּקְעָה וּבָעֵמֶק. לְשׁוֹן חֲבַצֶּלֶת הַשָּׁרוֹן. וּבַזְּמַן הַזֶּה, כָּל מַה שֶּׁתֵּרָאֶה בְּאִשָּׁה מֵאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם, טָמֵא, חוּץ מִן הַלָּבָן וְהַיָּרֹק. וּמִיָּמֵינוּ לֹא שָׁמַעְנוּ מִי שֶׁהִתִּיר שׁוּם מַרְאֵה דָּם, אֶלָּא הַלָּבָן וְהַיָּרֹק כְּכַרְתִּי בִּלְבַד:


שני חלקים מים ואחד יין מן היין השרוני – that comes from the land of Sharon, a place well-known place in the Land of Israel. Another explanation: wine that is made from the vines planted in the group of fields/plain and in the valley. It is the language of (Song of Songs 2:1): “A rose of Sharon.” But in this time, all that you see in a woman from the same place, is [ritually] impure, except from the white and the green. And from our days, we did not hear of he who declared pure any appearance of blood, other than only the white and the green of leeks.



## Chapter 3


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:1)


**הַמַּפֶּלֶת.** אִם יֵשׁ עִמָּהּ דָּם טְמֵאָה. נִדָּה:


מפלת. אם יש עמה דם טמאה – [impure blood] of a menstruant woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:2)


**וְאִם לָאו טְהוֹרָה.** דְּסָבַר תַּנָּא קַמָּא, אֶפְשָׁר לִפְתִיחַת הַקֶּבֶר בְּלֹא דָּם. וַאֲפִלּוּ נִמְצָא דָּם בְּתוֹךְ הַחֲתִיכָה, טְהוֹרָה, דְּדַם חֲתִיכָה הוּא וְלֹא דַּם נִדָּה:


ואם לאו טהורה – for the first Tanna/teacher holds, that is possible for the opening of the uterus (to pass the embryo) without discharging blood (see Talmud Niddah 21a). And even if blood is found with in the piece (of a shapeless object), it is pure, for it is the blood of the piece and not the blood of a menstruant woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:1:3)


**רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ טְמֵאָה.** דְּסָבַר רַבִּי יְהוּדָה אִי אֶפְשָׁר לִפְתִיחַת הַקֶּבֶר בְּלֹא דָּם. וְכֵיוָן שֶׁהִפִּילָה חֲתִיכָה, וַדַּאי דָּם הָיָה שָׁם אֶלָּא שֶׁאָבַד בְּמִעוּטוֹ וְלֹא נִרְאָה. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה:


רבי יהודה אומר בין כך ובין כך טמאה – for Rabbi Yehuda holds that it is impossible for the opening of the uterus without blood. And since she aborted a piece (i.e., a shapeless object) – it is definitely blood that was there but that it was lost in its limitation, and was not seen. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yehuda.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:1)


**כְּמִין שַׂעֲרָה.** שֵׂעָר:


כמין שערה – hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:2)


**יַבְחוּשִׁין.** יַתּוּשִׁין:


יבחושין (looking like a mass of red insects) – mosquitos or gnats.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:3)


**תַּטִּיל לַמַּיִם.** פּוֹשְׁרִין, וְיַשְׁהֶה אוֹתָם בַּמַּיִם הַפּוֹשְׁרִים עֶשְׂרִים וְאַרְבַּע שָׁעוֹת:


תטיל למים – [let her place them into water] – that is tepid, and have them remain in that tepid water for twenty-four hours.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:4)


**אִם נִמֹּחוּ טְמֵאָה.** מִשּׁוּם נִדָּה. דְּדָם הוּא:


אם נמוחו טמאה (if they dissolve [into blood] – because she is a menstruant woman, that it is blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:5)


**אִם יֵשׁ עִמָּהֶן דָּם טְמֵאָה וְאִם לָאו טְהוֹרָה.** מַתְנִיתִין כְּרַבָּנָן דִּפְלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יְהוּדָה לְעֵיל, וְסָבְרֵי דְּאֶפְשָׁר לִפְתִיחַת הַקֶּבֶר בְּלֹא דָּם:


אם יש עמהן דם טמאה – our Mishnah is like the Sages who argue with Rabbi Yehuda above (i.e., in Mishnah 1) that it is possible for the opening of the uterus without blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:2:6)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כֹּל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מִצּוּרַת אָדָם אֵינוֹ וָלָד.** הַכֹּל מוֹדִים בְּנוֹלַד גּוּפוֹ כְּצוּרַת חַיָּה וּפָנָיו פְּנֵי אָדָם, שֶׁהוּא חָשׁוּב וָלָד. גּוּפוֹ אָדָם וּפָנָיו פְּנֵי חַיָּה, הֲרֵי הוּא חַיָּה וְאֵין זֶה וָלָד, דְּבָתַר צוּרַת פָּנִים אָזְלִינַן. לֹא נֶחְלְקוּ אֶלָּא בְּמִי שֶׁקְּצָת פָּנָיו כִּפְנֵי אָדָם, וּקְצָת כִּפְנֵי בְּהֵמָה, וַאֲפִלּוּ רֹב פָּנָיו כִּפְנֵי אָדָם וְעַיִן אַחַת שֶׁבְּרֹאשׁוֹ בִּלְבַד כְּעַיִן שֶׁל בְּהֵמָה, רַבִּי מֵאִיר אוֹמֵר, כָּל צוּרַת אָדָם צְרִיכָה, וְכֵיוָן שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ עַיִן כִּבְהֵמָה אֵין זֶה וָלָד. וְרַבָּנָן סָבְרֵי, כָּל שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ מִצּוּרַת אָדָם כְּלָל וַאֲפִלּוּ מִקְצָת צוּרָה אֵין לוֹ, אֵינוֹ וָלָד. אֲבָל יֵשׁ בּוֹ מִקְצָת צוּרָה, הֲרֵי זֶה וָלָד. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


וחכמים אומרים כל שאין בו מצורת אדם אינו ולד – everyone agrees that when it’s body is born in the form of a wild animal, and its face is that of a human, it considered a [human] fetus; its body like that of a human but its face like that face of wild animal, it is a wild animal and this is not a [human] fetus, for we go after the form of the face. They didn’t disagree other than with one for whom part of the face was like that of a human and part of like the face of an animal, and even if most of the face is like that of a human, but one eye that is in his head alone is like that of an animal, Rabbi Meir states that one requires the entire form of a human being, for since it has the eye like an animal, this is not a [human] fetus. But the Rabbis hold, that which lacks the form of a human being at all and even doesn’t have part of a human form, is not a [human] fetus, but if he has part of a [human] form, it is a [human] fetus. But the Halakha is according to the Sages.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:1)


**שְׁפִיר.** חֲתִיכָה שֶׁל בָּשָׂר. וְאִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ צוּרַת אָדָם נִקְרָא שְׁפִיר מְרֻקָּם. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי שֶׁהוּא כִּשְׁפוֹפֶרֶת שֶׁל בֵּיצָה, לְפִיכָךְ נִקְרָא שְׁפִיר:


שפיר (sac of a fetus) – a piece of flesh. If it has the form of a human being, it is called a fetus having an articulated shape. But I heard that it is like an egg-shell, therefore it is called a sac.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:2)


**מָלֵא גְנוּנִים.** מָלֵא גְּוָנִים הַרְבֵּה. פֵּרוּשׁ אַחֵר, גְּנִינִים, תּוֹלָעִים, שֶׁחֲתִיכַת הַבָּשָׂר חֲתוּכָה חֲתִיכוֹת דַּקּוֹת כְּעֵין תּוֹלָעִים:


מלא גנינים ( see Tractate Niddah 24b) – [an abortion consisting of] a bag full of many-colored substance. Another explanation: גנינים are worms, that the piece of flesh is cut into many thin pieces similar worms.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:3)


**אֵינָהּ חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְוָלָד.** וְאֵינָהּ טְמֵאָה לֵידָה. וְאִם אֵין עִמָּהֶן דָּם, אֵינָהּ חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת אֲפִלּוּ מִשּׁוּם נִדָּה:


אינה חוששת לולד – she is not defiled by giving birth. But if there is no blood with them, she should not worry even because of being a menstruating woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:3:4)


**תֵּשֵׁב לְזָכָר וְלִנְקֵבָה.** טְמֵאָה שְׁבוּעַיִם כְּיוֹלֶדֶת נְקֵבָה, וְאֵין לָהּ דַּם טֹהַר אֶלָּא שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים כְּיוֹלֶדֶת זָכָר. הוֹאִיל וְאֵינוֹ נִכָּר אִם זָכָר אִם נְקֵבָה, נוֹתְנִים עָלָיו חֻמְרֵי זָכָר וְחֻמְרֵי נְקֵבָה:


תשב לזכר ונקבה impure for two weeks like a woman who gives birth to a female. But she doesn’t have blood of purity (i.e., vaginal bleeding experienced by a woman from eight to forty days after giving birth to a boy and from fifteen to eighty days after giving birth to a girl), other than thirty-three days like a woman who gives birth to a male, for since it is not known/recognized if it is a male or a female, we place upon her the stringencies of the male and the stringencies of a female.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:1)


**סַנְדָּל.** חֲתִיכַת בָּשָׂר עֲשׂוּיָה כִּדְמוּת לָשׁוֹן שֶׁל שׁוֹר. וּמִפְּנֵי שֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ צוּרַת סַנְדָּל קוֹרִין לָהּ סַנְדָּל. וְהוּא רָגִיל לָבֹא עִם וָלָד. וְיֵשׁ מְפָרְשִׁים, סַנְדָּל, שָׂנאוּי (ולד) [וְדַל]:


סנדל – a piece of flesh made like the shape of the tongue of a bull. And because it has the form of a sandal, we call it a sandal. And it customarily comes with the fetus. But there are those who explain, "סנדל" that it is becoming [as] a fetus [one must say, and poor}.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:2)


**שִׁלְיָא.** כְּמִין קְרוּם שֶׁהַוָּלָד מֻנָּח בְּתוֹכָהּ. וּתְחִלָּתָהּ כְּחוּט שֶׁל עֵרֶב. וּכְשֶׁהִיא הוֹלֶכֶת וְרוֹוַחַת, רְחָבָה כְּתֻרְמוֹס, וַחֲלוּלָה כַּחֲצוֹצֶרֶת. וְאֵין שִׁלְיָא פְּחוּתָה מִטֶּפַח:


שליא – like a skin/membrane that was fetus is lying within it. And its beginning is like the thread of woof [which is thicker than that of the warp – see Tractate Niddah 35a] (i.e., the latitudinal direction), but when it expands wider like a lupine and hollowed like a trumpet. But the placenta is not less than a handbreadth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:3)


**הַבַּיִת טָמֵא.** מִשּׁוּם אֹהֶל הַמֵּת, דְּוָלָד הֲוָה בָּהּ וּמֵת:


הבית טמא – because of the tent of the deceased, for a [human] fetus was in it and died.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:4:4)


**נִמּוֹק הַוָּלָד.** וְנַעֲשָׂה דָּם וְנִתְעָרֵב בְּדַם הַלֵּידָה וּבָטֵל בְּרֹב, וְאֵין הַבַּיִת טָמֵא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


נימוק הולד (the fetus was mashed) – and the it became blood and was combined with the blood of birth and was nullified in the majority. But the house is not impure. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:1)


**תֵּשֵׁב לְזָכָר וְלִנְקֵבָה.** טְמֵאָה שְׁבוּעַיִם כִּנְקֵבָה, וִימֵי טֹהַר שֶׁלָּהּ שְׁלֹשִׁים וּשְׁלֹשָׁה יוֹם בִּלְבַד כְּזָכָר:


תשב לזכר ונקבה – she is [ritually] impure for two weeks like [the birth of] a female, and her days of purity are only thirty-three like [the birth of] a male.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:2)


**טֻמְטוּם וְזָכָר אַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס וְזָכָר.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁיָּלְדָה תְּאוֹמִים, אֶחָד טֻמְטוּם אוֹ אַנְדְּרוֹגִינוֹס, וְהָאֶחָד זָכָר וַדַּאי:


טומטום וזכר – as for example that she gave birth, one whose sexual organs are concealed or a hermaphrodite, and the other is a definite male.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:3)


**תֵּשֵׁב לְזָכָר וְלִנְקֵבָה.** וְלֹא אָמְרִינַן מִדְּהַאי זָכָר וַדַּאי, הַאי נַמִּי זָכָר:


תשב לזכר ולנקבה – but we don’t say that from this definite male [child], this other one is also a male.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:4)


**תֵּשֵׁב לִנְקֵבָה בִלְבָד.** דַּאֲפִלּוּ הָוֵי טֻמְטוּם זָכָר, בָּתַר נְקֵבוֹת אָזְלִינַן, דְּכָל יְמֵי זָכָר בֵּין לְטֻמְאָה בֵּין לְטָהֳרָה מֻבְלָעִים תּוֹךְ שֶׁל נְקֵבָה:


תשב לנקבה בלבד – and even if it is one whose sexual organs are concealed is a male, we follow after the females, for all the days of a male, whether for [ritual] impurity or whether for purity they are absorbed within that of the female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:5)


**מְחֻתָּךְ.** אֲבָרִים אֲבָרִים:


מחותך – limb by limb.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:6)


**מְסֹרָס.** לָשׁוֹן הָפוּךְ הוּא. כְּמוֹ, סָרֵס הַמִּקְרָא וְדָרְשֵׁהוּ [בָּבָא בַתְרָא קיט.]:


מסורס – the language of opposite, like [Tractate Bava Batra 119b]: “invert the order in which the persons are mentioned in that verse (in reference to Numbers 27:2) and interpret it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:7)


**מִשֶּׁיָּצָא רֻבּוֹ הֲרֵי הוּא כְיָלוּד.** וְרֹאשׁוֹ כְּרֻבּוֹ. הִלְכָּךְ מִשֶּׁיָּצָא רֹאשׁוֹ שֶׁל מְחֻתָּךְ, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁכָּל שְׁאָר הַגּוּף בִּפְנִים, הֲרֵי הוּא כְּיָלוּד:


משיצא רובו הרי הוא כילוד – and his head is like the majority. Therefore, when the head of someone who is cut up by limbs comes out, even though all the rest of the body is inside, it is like it is born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:5:8)


**פַּדַּחְתּוֹ.** מִצְחוֹ:


פחדתו – his forehead.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:1)


**וְאֵין יָדוּעַ מַה הוּא.** אִם זָכָר אִם נְקֵבָה:


ואין ידוע מה הוא – if it is a male or a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:2)


**תֵּשֵׁב לְזָכָר וְלִנְקֵבָה.** יְמֵי טֹהַר דְּזָכָר, וִימֵי טֻמְאָה דִּנְקֵבָה:


תשב לזכר ולנקבה – the days of purity of a male, and the days of [ritual] impurity of a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:6:3)


**לְזָכָר וְלִנְקֵבָה וּלְנִדָּה.** כְּלוֹמַר, כְּשֶׁתִּרְאֶה תְּהֵא נִדָּה, שֶׁאֵין לָהּ יְמֵי טֹהַר, דְּשֶׁמָּא לָאו וָלָד הוּא. וְטוֹבֶלֶת לְסוֹף אַרְבָּעִים כְּיוֹלֶדֶת זָכָר לְמַאן דְּאָמַר טְבִילָה בִּזְמַנָּהּ מִצְוָה. וְטוֹבֶלֶת יוֹם אָרֹךְ, כְּגוֹן יוֹלֶדֶת זָכָר שֶׁטָּבְלָה לְסוֹף שִׁבְעָה וְאֵינָהּ טְהוֹרָה לַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַמִּקְדָּשׁ עַד מְלֹאת הָאַרְבָּעִים, צְרִיכָה טְבִילָה אַחֶרֶת לְסוֹף הָאַרְבָּעִים, וְזֹאת נִצְרְכָה לִטְבֹּל לְסוֹף אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם כְּיוֹלֶדֶת זָכָר, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין טְבִילָה זוֹ מוֹעֶלֶת לָהּ כְּלוּם, שֶׁהֲרֵי אֵינָהּ טְהוֹרָה לַקֹּדֶשׁ וְלַמִּקְדָּשׁ עַד מְלֹאת שְׁמוֹנִים כְּיוֹלֶדֶת נְקֵבָה:


לזכר ונקבה ולנדה – meaning to say, that when you see that she is a menstruant woman, that she doesn’t have the days of purity, for perhaps it is not a fetus. And she immerses [in a Mikveh] at the conclusion of forty days like when she gives birth to a male [child] according to the one who states that ritual immersion at its appropriate time is a Mitzvah. And she immerses a long day, she needs another ritual -immersion at the end of the fortieth [day], and this is needed for immersion at the conclusion of forty days like one who gives birth to a male [child], even though this immersion does not have any effect upon her whatsoever, for she is not pure for Holy Things and for the Temple until her fulfillment of eighty days like giving birth to a female [child],



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:1)


**הַמַּפֶּלֶת לְיוֹם אַרְבָּעִים.** לִטְבִילָתָהּ:


המפלת ליום ארבעים – of her ritual immersion.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:2)


**אֵינָהּ חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְוָלָד.** שֶׁלֹּא נִגְמְרָה יְצִירָתוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּכְלוּ כָּל אַרְבָּעִים יוֹם:


אינה חוששת לולד – that she did not cause his formation until the completion of all forty days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:3)


**וּלְנִדָּה.** כְּשֶׁתִּרְאֶה, תְּהֵא נִדָּה, דִּלְמָא לֵיכָּא וָלָד וְאֵין כָּאן יְמֵי טֹהַר:


ולנדה - when she sees [blood], she will be a menstruant woman, for perhaps there isn’t a [human] fetus [after all], and there aren’t the days of purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:4)


**יוֹם אַרְבָּעִים וְאֶחָד תֵּשֵׁב לְזָכָר.** שִׁבְעָה יְמֵי טֻמְאָה. אֲפִלּוּ בְּלֵידָה יָבֵשְׁתָּא שֶׁאֵינָהּ נִדָּה. אֲבָל שְׁבוּעַיִם דִּנְקֵבָה, אֵינָהּ יוֹשֶׁבֶת, כִּדְּקָתָנֵי טַעֲמָא שֶׁיְּצִירַת נְקֵבָה לִשְׁמוֹנִים וְאֶחָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


יום ארבעים ואחד תשב לזכר – seven days of impurity. Even with her giving birth dried up/withered she is not a menstruant woman. But two weeks [after giving birth to] a female [child], she does not sit, as the reason is taught that the formation of a female is [after] eighty-one days. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yishmael.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 3:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/3:7:5)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים כוּ'.** חֲכָמִים הַיְנוּ תַּנָּא קַמָּא, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם דְּמִסְתַּבֵּר טַעֲמֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל טְפֵי מִדְּתַנָּא קַמָּא, הֲדַר רַבִּי וּשְׁנָאָהּ בִּלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים, לְאַשְׁמְעִינַן דַּהֲלָכָה כִּסְתָם רִאשׁוֹן, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁמַּחֲלֹקֶת רַבִּי יִשְׁמָעֵאל בְּצִדּוֹ:
