וחכמים אומרים כו' – The Sages are the first Tanna/teacher, but because that the reason of Rabbi Ishmael makes more sense than the first Tanna/teacher, Rabbi [Judah the Prince] retracted and taught it in the language of the Sages, to teach us that the Halakha is according to the first anonymous teaching, and even though there is the dispute of Rabbi Yishmael at its side.



## Chapter 4


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:1)


**בְּנוֹת כּוּתִים נִדּוֹת מֵעֲרִיסָתָן.** מִקַּטְנוּתָן, כְּשֶׁהֵן מֻנָּחוֹת בָּעֲרִיסָה, גָּזְרוּ בְּהוּ רַבָּנָן דְּלִיטַמּוּ מִשּׁוּם נִדָּה, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְאִשָּׁה כִּי תִהְיֶה זָבָה, וְתַנְיָא, אֵין לִי אֶלָּא אִשָּׁה שֶׁהִיא גְּדוֹלָה, תִּינוֹקֶת בַּת יוֹם אֶחָד לְנִדָּה מִנַּיִן, תַּלְמוּד לוֹמַר וְאִשָּׁה. וְכוּתִים לֹא דָּרְשֵׁי אִשָּׁה וְאִשָּׁה, וְכִי חָזְיָן קְטַנּוֹת לֹא מַפְרְשֵׁי לְהוּ, הִלְכָּךְ גָּזְרוּ בְּהוּ רַבָּנָן דְּלִיטַמּוּ כֻּלְּהוּ:


בנות כותים נדות מעריסתן – from their youth, when they are placed in the cradle, the Rabbis decreed concerning them that they defile on account of being menstruating women, as it is written (Leviticus 15:19): “When a woman has a discharge”/ואשה כי-תהיה זבה. And it is taught in a Baraita: I do not have [from this verse] anything other than a woman when she is an adult, from where do I learn that a baby one-day old is a menstruant woman? The inference teaches us: "ואשה"/when a woman. But the Cutheans (i.e., Samaritans) do not expound upon [the difference between]"אשה" "ואשה" - a woman, when a woman, for when they see them as minors (i.e., babies), they do not separate/set them aside, therefore, the Rabbis decreed concerning it that all of them [at whatever age] defile.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:2)


**מְטַמְּאִין מִשְׁכָּב הַתַּחְתּוֹן כָּעֶלְיוֹן.** תַּחְתּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה טָמֵא כְּעֶלְיוֹנוֹ שֶׁל זָב, מָה עֶלְיוֹנוֹ שֶׁל זָב אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין, אַף תַּחְתּוֹנוֹ שֶׁל בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אֶלָּא אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין. וְעֶלְיוֹנוֹ שֶׁל זָב, הוּא הַדָּבָר הַנִּשָּׂא עַל גַּבֵּי הַזָּב נַפְקָא לָן מִקְּרָא דְּאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא אָדָם וְכֵלִים אֶלָּא אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְהַנֹּגֵעַ בְּכֹל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה תַחְתָּיו יִטְמָא, מַאי תַחְתָּיו, אִילֵימָא תַּחְתָּיו דְּזָב, מִכָּל אֲשֶׁר יִגַּע בְּמִשְׁכָּבוֹ נַפְקָא, אֶלָּא הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה הַזָּב תַּחְתָּיו יִטְמָא, וּמִדְּלֹא עָרְבִינְהוּ וְנִכְתּוֹב וְכָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ בְּכָל אֲשֶׁר יִהְיֶה תַּחְתָּיו וְהַנּוֹשֵׂא אוֹתָם יְכַבֵּס בְּגָדָיו, וְאַפְסְקִינְהוּ בְּיִטְמָא, מִכְּלַל דְּהַאי יִטְמָא לָאו בְּאָדָם וְכֵלִים אַיְרֵי, אֶלָּא בְּאֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים. וְכוּתִים שֶׁהֵן בּוֹעֲלֵי נִדּוֹת, מְטַמְּאִים מִשְׁכָּב הַתַּחְתּוֹן שֶׁלָּהֶן אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין, אֲבָל לֹא אָדָם וְכֵלִים, כְּעֶלְיוֹנוֹ שֶׁל זָב שֶׁגַּם הוּא מְטַמֵּא אֳכָלִים וּמַשְׁקִים לֹא אָדָם וְכֵלִים. וְכָל הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ לֹא נֶאֶמְרוּ אֶלָּא בִּזְמַן שֶׁהָיוּ הַכּוּתִים מַחֲזִיקִים בַּתּוֹרָה וּמְדַקְדְּקִים בְּהַרְבֵּה מִצְוֹת כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל. אֲבָל לְאַחַר שֶׁבָּדְקוּ וּמָצְאוּ לָהֶם כִּדְמוּת יוֹנָה בְּרֹאשׁ הַר גְּרִיזִים שֶׁעוֹבְדִים אוֹתָהּ, עֲשָׂאוּם כְּנָכְרִים גְּמוּרִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם, בֵּין לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאָה וְטָהֳרָה, בֵּין לִשְׁאָר דִּינִים שֶׁבַּתּוֹרָה, אֵין הֶפְרֵשׁ בֵּינָם לְבֵין הַנָּכְרִים כְּלָל:


מטמאין משכב התחתון כעליון (they convey uncleanness to a couch beneath as to a cover above-see also Tractate Kelim, Chapter 1, Mishnah 3) – what is below that of someone who has sexual intercourse with a menstruating woman is unclean/impure just as that which is above a person with gonorrhea/זב, that just as what is above a person with gonorrhea does not defile anything other than foods and liquids, even that which is below the person having sexual intercourse with a menstruating woman does not defile anything other than foods and liquids. And that which is above the person with gonorrhea is that which is removed from upon the person with gonorrhea, we derive from Scripture that he does not defile a person and/or utensils but only food and liquids, as it is written (Leviticus 15:10): “whomever touches anything that was under him shall be impure [until evening],” what is תחתיו/under him? If you were to say that which is underneath the person with gonorrhea/זב – he derives from everything that he would touch when lying down, but the person who comes in contact with anything where the person with gonorrhea is below him becomes defiled, and since we do not combine them and write, “and anyone who comes in contact with everything that is below him and who carries them should wash his clothing,” and we make the religious ruling concerning him that he defiles, it follows that this, “and he defiles,” does not speak of people and utensils, but rather [only] foods and liquids. But Cutheans who engage in having sexual relationships with menstruating women, defile food and liquids that are lying underneath them , but not humans or utensils, like as to a cover above of a person with gonorrhea that he also defiles food and liquids but not a person or utensils. And all of these things were not stated other than at the time when the Cutheans uphold the Torah and are punctilious in many of the commandments like Israelites. But after they (i.e., the Rabbis) examined and found with them [things] like the image of a pigeon at the top of Mount Gerizim that they worship, they made them like complete heathens for all of their things, whether for matters of [ritual] impurity and purity or for the rest of the laws of the Torah, there is no difference between them and between heathens at all.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:3)


**וְהֵן יוֹשְׁבוֹת עַל כָּל דָּם.** כְּלוֹמַר, לְפִיכָךְ הַכּוּתִים הֵם בּוֹעֲלֵי נִדּוֹת, לְפִי שֶׁהֵן יוֹשְׁבוֹת עַל כָּל דָּם, דְּכָל דָּם שֶׁרוֹאָה בֵּין אָדֹם בֵּין יָרֹק יוֹשֶׁבֶת עָלָיו יְמֵי נִדּוּת, וְזוֹ תַּקָּלָה הִיא לָהֶם, שֶׁאִם רוֹאָה דָּם יָרֹק הַיּוֹם מַתְחֶלֶת לִמְנוֹת מֵהַיּוֹם שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי נִדָּה, וְאִם תִּרְאֶה בְּאוֹתָם יְמֵי נִדּוּת דָּם אָדֹם, אֵינָהּ מוֹנָה אֶלָּא מִיּוֹם רְאִיָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה, וְאוֹתוֹ דָּם טָהוֹר הָיָה, וּמֵרְאִיָּה שְׁנִיָּה הִיא צְרִיכָה לִמְנוֹת:


והן יושבות על כל דם (continue unclean for any sort of blood) – meaning to say, therefore, the Cuthians engage in sexual intercourse with menstruating women, therefore, they continue [to be] unclean for any sort of blood, for all blood that she sees, whether red whether green, she continues unclean for it the days of the menstruant woman, and this is a snare/stumbling block for them, that if she sees green blood today, she begins to count from today seven days of menstrual uncleanness , but if she sees during those actual days of menstrual uncleanness red blood, she does not count other than from the first sighting, but that blood was pure, and from the second sighting she needs to count.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:4)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִין עֲלֵיהֶן.** הַלּוֹבֵשׁ אוֹ מִתְכַּסֶּה בְּאוֹתָן בְּגָדִים וְנִכְנַס לַמִּקְדָּשׁ, פָּטוּר מִקָּרְבָּן. אוֹ אִם נָגְעוּ אוֹתָן בְּגָדִים שֶׁל מִשְׁכָּב שֶׁלָּהֶן בַּתְּרוּמָה, תּוֹלִין:


ואין חייבין עליהן - a person who wears or covers [himself] with those clothing and enters into the Sanctuary, is exempt from a sacrifice. Or if those clothing of their couch came in contact with heave-offering, we suspend them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:1:5)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁטֻּמְאָתָן סָפֵק.** דְּלֹא יָדְעִינַן אִי נִדָּה [הִיא] אִי לֹא. וּבַגְּמָרָא פָּרֵיךְ אַמַּתְנִיתִין מֵהָא דִתְנַן בְּמַסֶּכֶת טָהֳרוֹת פֶּרֶק ד'‏ (משנה ה) עַל שִׁשָּׁה סְפֵקוֹת שׂוֹרְפִין אֶת הַתְּרוּמָה, עַל סְפֵק בִּגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ, כְּלוֹמַר עַל בִּגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ אִם נָגְעוּ בָּהּ, נִשְׂרֶפֶת, מִשּׁוּם שֶׁמָּא טָמֵא הָיָה, וְהַאי כּוּתִי נַמִּי תֵּפּוּק לֵיהּ אִם נָגַע מִשְׁכָּבוֹ בַּתְּרוּמָה, תְּהֵא נִשְׂרֶפֶת, מִשּׁוּם דְּעַם הָאָרֶץ הוּא וַהֲוֵי לֵיהּ בִּגְדֵי עַם הָאָרֶץ. וּמְשָׁנֵי, דְּמַתְנִיתִין אַיְרֵי בְּכוּתִי שֶׁטָּבַל בְּפָנֵינוּ וְעָלָה וְדָרַס עַל בְּגָדָיו שֶׁל חָבֵר כְּשֶׁהוּא עָרֹם, אוֹ שֶׁלָּבַשׁ בִּגְדֵי חָבֵר, אוֹ שֶׁהִטְבִּיל אֶת בְּגָדָיו בְּפָנָיו, בְּאֹפֶן דִּבְגָדָיו שֶׁל כּוּתִי כְּשֶׁהֵן מִדְרָס לֹא נָגְעוּ בִּבְגָדָיו שֶׁל חָבֵר כְּשֶׁדָּרַס עֲלֵיהֶן, וְאַחַר כָּךְ נָגְעוּ בְּגָדָיו שֶׁל חָבֵר שֶׁדָּרַס כּוּתִי עֲלֵיהֶן בַּתְּרוּמָה, לֹא שָׂרְפִינַן תְּרוּמָה עֲלֵיהֶן. דְּאִי מִשּׁוּם טֻמְאַת עַם הָאָרֶץ, הָא טָבֵיל לֵיהּ. וְאִי מִשּׁוּם בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה, שֶׁמָּא לֹא בָּעַל בְּקָרוֹב וּכְבָר עָבְרוּ יְמֵי טֻמְאָתוֹ וְסָלְקָא לֵיהּ טְבִילָה. וְאִם תִּמְצָא לוֹמַר בָּעַל בְּקָרוֹב, שֶׁמָּא לֹא הָיְתָה אִשְׁתּוֹ יוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל דָּם טָמֵא אֶלָּא עַל דָּם טָהוֹר, וְהָוֵי סְפֵק סְפֵקָא, וְאַסְּפֵק סְפֵקָא לֹא שָׂרְפִינַן תְּרוּמָה:


מפני שטומאתן ספק – for we don’t know if she is a menstruating woman or not. But in the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 33b) it raises an objection on our Mishnah from that which is taught inTractate Tohorot, Chapter 4 (Mishnah 5): “On account of six matters of doubt do they burn the heave-offering,” [and one of them is] “because of a doubt concerning the clothing of a common, uneducated person [who is not punctilious in the observance of the laws of ritual purity],” that is to say on the clothing of a common, uneducated person if they came in contact with it (i.e., heave-offering), it is burned, because perhaps it was [ritually] impure, and this Cuthean also, we derive it that if his couch came in contact with heave-offering, it would be burned, because he is a common, uneducated person, and he has the clothing of a common, uneducated person. And it (i.e., the Talmud) responds that our Mishnah speaks regards a Cuthean who immersed [in a ritual bath] in our presence and came up [from the Mikveh] and tread upon the clothing of a member of the group dedicated to the precise observance of the Mitzvot (i.e., a חבר) who is naked, or that he wore the clothing of a member of the group dedicated to the precise observance of the Mitzvot, or immersed his clothing in his presence (i.e., of the member of the group dedicated to the precise observance of the Mitzvot) in a manner whereby the clothing of the Cuthean when they are trodden upon [from a person with gonorrhea immediate treading, leaning against] did not come in contact with the clothing of the member of the group dedicated to the precise observance of the Mitzvot when he treated upon him, and afterwards, the clothing of the member of the group dedicated to the precise observance of the Mitzvot that the Cuthean tread upon them with heave-offering, we don’t burn the heave-offering on their account. For if it was because of the ritual impurity of the common, uneducated person, he would immerse himself. But if it was because of someone engaging in sexual intercourse with a menstruating woman, perhaps he did not engage in close sexual contact, and the days of his [ritual] impurity had already passed and the ritual immersion is effective. But if you wish to say that he engaged in close sexual contact, perhaps his wife was not continuing unclean for any sort of blood, but rather only on pure blood, and this is a compound uncertainty, and we on a compound uncertainty, we don’t burn the heave-offering.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:2:1)


**פֵּרְשׁוּ לָלֶכֶת בְּדַרְכֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל הֲרֵי הֵן כְּיִשְׂרְאֵלִיּוֹת.** אֲבָל סְתָמָא, הֲרֵי הֵן כַּכּוּתִיּוֹת:


פירשו ללכת בדרכי ישראל הרי הן כישראליות – but undefined, they are like Cuthean women.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:2:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר לְעוֹלָם הֵן כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל.** וּסְתָמָא הֵן כְּיִשְׂרְאֵלִיּוֹת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר לעולם הן כישראל – but undefined, they are like Israelite women. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:1)


**דַּם נָכְרִית בֵּית שַׁמַּאי מְטַהֲרִין.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְרֻקָּהּ וּבְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ מוֹדוּ בֵּית שַׁמַּאי דְּרַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶן לִהְיוֹת כְּזָבִין לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶן, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי דָּמָהּ טָהוֹר, דְּשִׁיְּרוּ בָּהּ רַבָּנָן לְמִהְוֵי הֶכֵּירָא דְּטֻמְאָתָן דְּרַבָּנָן, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלֹא לִשְׂרְפוּ עֲלַיְהוּ תְּרוּמָה וְקָדָשִׁים:


דם מכרית וכו' בית שמאי מטהרין – but even though that [regarding] her spit and her urine, the School of Shammai agrees with the Sages that decreed upon them to be like those with gonorrhea for all their matters, even so, her blood is pure, for the Rabbis reserved for her that it would be a means of recognition of their Rabbinic [ritual] impurity, in order that they would not burn heave-offering or Holy Things upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:2)


**וְדַם טָהוֹר שֶׁל מְצֹרַעַת.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמַעְיָנוֹת שֶׁל מְצֹרַעַת כְּגוֹן רֻקָּהּ וּמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ הֲווּ אַב הַטֻּמְאָה כְּשֶׁל זָב, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי דַּם טֹהַר שֶׁלָּהּ טָהוֹר, דְּלָאו מַעְיָן הוּא, דְּדוּמְיָא דְּרֹק בָּעִינַן שֶׁמִּתְעַגֵּל וְיוֹצֵא:


ודם טהור של מצורעת – but even though that the discharges of a woman leper such as her spit and urine are primary sources of ritual impurity like that of someone with gonorrhea, even so, her blood of purity (i.e., the vaginal bleeding experienced by a woman from eight to forty days after giving birth to a boy and from fifteen to eighty days after giving birth to a girl) is pure, for it is not a discharge, for we require that which is similar to spit (i.e., secretion of the mouth) which is formed in globules on being discharged (see Tractate Niddah 56a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:3)


**כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ.** דִּמְטַמְּאִין לַחִין וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין יְבֵשִׁין. וְהַךְ הֶיכֵּרָא הוּא דְּעַבוּד גַּבֵּי דַּם הַנָּכְרִית דִּמְטַמֵּא מִדְּרַבָּנָן שֶׁגָּזְרוּ עֲלֵיהֶם שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כַּזָּבִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶן, דְּאִלּוּ דְּיִשְׂרְאֵלִית מְטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ, וּדְנָכְרִית לַח וְלֹא יָבֵשׁ, כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ דְּאֵין מְטַמְּאִין יְבֵשִׁין. וְדָם טָהוֹר שֶׁל מְצֹרַעַת נַמִּי, אַף עַל גַּב דְּדָם לָאו מַעְיָן הוּא, דִּין מַעְיָן אִית לֵיהּ, דְּרַחֲמָנָא רַבְּיֵהּ מִקְּרָא דִּכְתִיב לְזָכָר, לְרַבּוֹת מְצֹרָע לְמַעְיְנוֹתָיו, וְלִנְקֵבָה, לְרַבּוֹת מְצֹרַעַת לְמַעְיְנוֹתֶיהָ, וְאָמְרִינַן מַעְיְנוֹתֶיהָ דְּמַאי, אִילֵימָא שְׁאָר מַעְיְנוֹתֶיהָ כְּגוֹן רֻקָּהּ וּמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ דְּאָתוּ מִזָּב דִּכְתִיב בֵּיהּ וְכִי יָרֹק הַזָּב, וּמִנֵּיהּ נָפְקֵי שְׁאָר מַעְיָנוֹת, מִזָּכָר נַפְקָא, דְּכֵיוָן דְּאִתְרַבֵּי מְצֹרָע לְמַעְיְנוֹתָיו, אִתְרַבֵּי מְצֹרַעַת לְכָל מַעְיְנוֹתֶיהָ דְּאִיכָּא דִּכְוָתַיְהוּ בְּזָכָר, אֶלָּא לְדָמָהּ דְּלֵיתֵיהּ בְּזָכָר, וְכֵיוָן דְּרַבְּיֵהּ רַחֲמָנָא לְדָמָהּ שֶׁיְּהֵא כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ, אִם כֵּן מָה רֻקָּהּ וּמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ מְטַמְּאִין לַחִין וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין יְבֵשִׁים, אַף דָּמָהּ כֵּן:


כרוקה וכמימי רגליה – that defile while moist but do not defile while dry. But this recognition is what is used regarding the blood of a heathen woman which defiles according to the Rabbis who decreed upon them that they would be like those with gonorrhea for all their matters, whereas the Israelite women (i.e., Jewish women) blood defiles moist and dry, and that of the heathen [which defiles] moist but not dry, like her spit and her urine that do not defile dry. But the blood of a woman leper also, even though her blood is not an issue/spring, she has the law of a spring upon her, for the All-Merciful included her from Scripture as it is written for a male, to include the leper to his discharge/issue and for the female, to include the female leper to her discharge/issue, and we state that her discharge/issue is suspicious, for if you wish you can say the rest of the discharges, we derive it from the male, and since we include the [male] leper to his discharges, we include the female leper with her discharges, for there is something analogous to it in the male, but to her blood, there is nothing [like it] in the male, for since the All-Merciful included her blood, that it would be like her spit and like her urine, if so, just as her spit and her urine defile moist and don’t defile when dry, so too her blood is like this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:4)


**דַּם הַיּוֹלֶדֶת.** שֶׁעָבְרוּ שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי טֻמְאַת לֵידָה וְלֹא טָבְלָה:


דם היולדת- that passed the days of [ritual] defilement of a menstruant woman who did not immerse [as yet in the Mikveh].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:5)


**בֵּית שַׁמַּאי אוֹמְרִים כְּרֻקָּהּ וּכְמֵימֵי רַגְלֶיהָ.** מְטַמֵּא לַח וְאֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא יָבֵשׁ. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּדַם טֹהַר הוּא, דִּבְיוֹמֵי תָּלָה רַחֲמָנָא וְהָא מָלוּ, וְלֵיכָּא לְטַמּוֹיֵהּ נַמִּי מִשּׁוּם מַעְיָן דְּהָא אֵין מִתְעַגֵּל וְיוֹצֵא, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי רַבָּנָן גַּזּוּר טֻמְאָה עֲלֵיהּ, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא טָבְלָה וּטְמֵאָה הִיא, גְּזֵרָה אַחַר שִׁבְעָה אַטּוּ תּוֹךְ שִׁבְעָה. וּמִיהוּ לֹא גַּזּוּר שֶׁיְּטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ כְּלִפְנֵי שִׁבְעָה, כִּי הֵיכִי דְּלַהֲוֵי לֵיהּ הֶכֵּירָא דְּלֵידַע דְּטֻמְאָתוֹ דְּרַבָּנָן:


בית שמאי אומרים כרוקה וכמימי רגליה - it defiles when moist but does not defile when dry. But even though it is blood of purity, the All-Merciful ties it to days but these were filled, but not to make it defiled also because of a discharge because it doesn’t form globules on being discharged, nevertheless, the Rabbis decreed [ritual] defilement upon her, for since she did not immerse [in a Mikveh], she is [ritually] impure/defiled, as a decree that after seven [days] is like within seven [days]. But however, it was not decreed that that it would be defiled [both] moist and dry like before the seven [days], for just as there is recognition that they knew that his defilement is according to the Rabbis.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:6)


**וּבֵית הִלֵּל אוֹמְרִים מְטַמֵּא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ.** דְּטֻמְאָתוֹ מִן הַתּוֹרָה כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא טָבְלָה. דְּסָבְרֵי בֵּית הִלֵּל בְּיוֹמֵי וּטְבִילָה תָּלָה רַחֲמָנָא, וְכָל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא טָבְלָה, הֲרֵי דָּמָהּ לְאַחַר שִׁבְעָה כְּלִפְנֵי שִׁבְעָה:


ובית הלל אומרים מטמא לח ויבש – for his [ritual] impurity is from the Torah all the time that he didn’t immerse [in the Mikveh]. For the School of Hillel holds that the All-Merciful ties days and ritual immersion, all the while that she didn’t immerse, her blood after the seven [days] is like that of before the seven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:3:7)


**וּמוֹדִים בְּיוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב.** אִם בְּעֵת לֵדָתָהּ הָיְתָה זָבָה שֶׁהִיא צְרִיכָה לִסְפֹּר שִׁבְעָה נְקִיִּים קֹדֶם שֶׁתִּטְהַר, וְיָלְדָה וְלֹא סָפְרָה, מוֹדִים בֵּית שַׁמַּאי לְבֵית הִלֵּל שֶׁדָּמָהּ טָמֵא לַח וְיָבֵשׁ אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעָבְרוּ שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי לֵידָה לְזָכָר וּשְׁבוּעַיִם לִנְקֵבָה, שֶׁדָּמָהּ שֶׁל זָבָה לְעוֹלָם הוּא בְּטֻמְאָתוֹ מִן הַתּוֹרָה עַד שֶׁתִּסְפֹּר שִׁבְעָה נְקִיִּים:


ומודים ביולדת זוב – it at the time of her giving birth she had a flux, she would need to count seven clean days prior to becoming [ritually] pure, but if she gave birth and didn’t count [seven clean days], the School of Shammai agrees with the School of Hillel that her blood is impure moist and dry even though the seven days following her giving birth to a male had passed or two weeks for a female, for the blood of a woman with a flux always remains in its impurity from the Torah until she counts seven clean [days].



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:1)


**הַמַּקְשָׁה נִדָּה.** הָכִי קָאָמַר, הַמַּקְשָׁה בְּלֵידָתָהּ בִּימֵי נִדָּתָהּ וְרָאֲתָה דָּם בְּהַקְשׁוֹתָהּ, הֲרֵי הִיא נִדָּה, דְּלֹא טִהֲרָה תּוֹרָה לְדַם קֹשִׁי אֶלָּא בְּאַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם שֶׁל יְמֵי זִיבָה, אֲבָל בְּיָמִים שֶׁל נִדָּה לֹא טַהֲרֵיהּ רַחֲמָנָא:


המקשה נדה – this is what it says: A woman having difficulties in her giving birth during the days of her menstruating period and saw blood during her labor, she is a menstruant woman/Niddah, for the Torah did not purify the blood of her protracted labor other than during the eleven days of her days of protracted menstruation/זיבה, but the All-Merciful did not purify it during her days of being a menstruant woman/נדה .


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:2)


**קִשְּׁתָה.** מֵחֲמַת וָלָד שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים בְּתוֹךְ אַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם שֶׁל יְמֵי זִיבָה וְרָאֲתָה כָּל שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים רְצוּפִים:


קשתה- on account of the fetus for three days during the eleven days of her protracted menstruation/ימי זיבה, and she saw it (i.e., blood) for three consecutive days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:3)


**וְשָׁפְתָה.** שֶׁעָמְדָה בְּשֹׁפִי וּבְנַחַת מֵחֲבָלֶיהָ מֵעֵת לְעֵת בָּרְבִיעִי:


ושפתה (and she was relieved)- that she stood in relief and in comfort from her throes of birth agony for an astronomical period of twenty-four hours on the fourth day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:4)


**וְיָלְדָה.** אִגַּלַּאי מִלְּתָא דְּדָמִים שֶׁרָאֲתָה לֹא בָּאוּ מֵחֲמַת וָלָד, הִלְכָּךְ הֲרֵי זוֹ יוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב. אֲבָל אִם לֹא שָׁפְתָה סָמוּךְ לְלֵדָתָהּ, כָּל דָּמִים שֶׁרָאֲתָה, מֵחֲמַת וָלָד הוּא, וְרַחֲמָנָא טַהֲרִינְהוּ מִזִּיבָה, דִּכְתִיב זוֹב דָּמָהּ, דָּמָהּ מֵחֲמַת עַצְמָהּ וְלֹא דָּמָהּ מֵחֲמַת וָלָד:


וילדה (and she gave birth) – and the matter was revealed that the blood that she saw did not come on account of the fetus, therefore, this [woman] gives birth while having a flux. But if she did not have relief close to her giving birth, all of the blood that she saw, was on account of the fetus and the All-Merciful purified her from the blood of flux, as it is written (Leviticus 15:25): “When the woman had a discharge of blood/זוב דמה [for many days, not at the time of her menstrual impurity, she shall be impure, as though at the time of her menstrual impurity, as long as her discharge lasts],” her blood is on account of herself but not her blood on account of her fetus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:5)


**לַיְלָה וָיוֹם.** דְּאִם שָׁפְתָה בַּחֲצִי יוֹם שְׁלִישִׁי וְיָלְדָה בַּחֲצִי יוֹם רְבִיעִי, דְּשָׁפְתָה מֵעֵת לְעֵת, אֵין זֶה שֹׁפִי, אֶלָּא אִם כֵּן שָׁפְתָה לַיְלָה וְיוֹם שֶׁל אַחַר הַלַּיְלָה:


לילה ויום – that if she had relief for half of Tuesday and she gave birth during the middle of Wednesday, for she had a period of relief for an astronomical period of twenty-four hours, this is not comfort/relief, unless her relief was for a night and a day after the night,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:6)


**כְּלֵילֵי שַׁבָּת וְיוֹמוֹ.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


like Friday night and its day. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Eliezer.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:4:7)


**שֶׁשָּׁפְתָה מִן הַצַּעַר.** כְּלוֹמַר וְשָׁפְתָה זוֹ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ, אִם שָׁפְתָה מִן הַצַּעַר אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא שָׁפְתָה מִן הַדָּם, הָוֵי שֹׁפִי:


ששפתה מן הצער – meaning to say, that she was relieved that we stated [in the Mishnah], that she had relief from the pain even though she didn’t have relief from the blood, it is relief.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:1)


**כַּמָּה הוּא קִשּׁוּיָהּ.** דְּלֹא אָתְיָא לִידֵי זִיבָה בְּכָל דָּמִים שֶׁתִּרְאֶה:


כמה הוא קשויה – that she does not come to being a woman with a flux with all of the blood that she will see.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:2)


**אֲפִלּוּ אַרְבָּעִים וַחֲמִשִּׁים יוֹם.** אִם לֹא שָׁפְתָה יוֹם אֶחָד סָמוּךְ לַלֵּידָה, לֹא אָתְיָא לִידֵי זִיבָה וְלֹא הַוְיָא יוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב:


אפילו ארבעים וחמשים יום – if she did not have one day of relief near the birth, she didn’t come to protracted menstruation, and she doesn’t give birth while being a woman in flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:3)


**דַּיָּהּ חָדְשָׁהּ.** דַּיָּהּ אִם נְטַהֵר אֶת הַקֹּשִׁי חֹדֶשׁ אֶחָד, דְּהַיְנוּ חֹדֶשׁ תְּשִׁיעִי. אֲבָל קָשְׁתָה שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּתוֹךְ אַחַד עָשָׂר יְמֵי זִיבָה בְּחֹדֶשׁ שְׁמִינִי וַאֲפִלּוּ קָשְׁתָה וְהוֹלֶכֶת כָּל תְּשִׁיעִי, הֲרֵי זוֹ יוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב מֵחֲמַת שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים שֶׁל שְׁמִינִי:


דיה חדשה – it is enough for her if the labor is purified in one month, that is during the ninth month [of pregnancy], for she gives birth while with a flux on account of three days during the eighth [month].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:5:4)


**אֵין קִשּׁוּי יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת.** שֶׁאִם קָשְׁתָה וְרָאֲתָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים קֹדֶם שְׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת וְהָיְתָה מַקְשָׁה וְהוֹלֶכֶת כָּל הַשַּׁבָּתוֹת אַחֲרֵי כֵן, הֲרֵי זוֹ יוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב מֵחֲמַת שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים שֶׁהָיוּ קֹדֶם הַשְּׁתֵּי שַׁבָּתוֹת. וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי וְרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אין קשוי יותר שבתות -that she was not in labor and saw [blood] for three days prior to two weeks, and then had continuous hard labor all of the [two] weeks after that, this is a woman giving birth while in flux on account of the three days that came prior to the two weeks [of hard labor]. And the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi and Rabbi Shimon.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:1)


**הַמַּקְשָׁה בְתוֹךְ שְׁמוֹנִים שֶׁל נְקֵבָה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁשִּׁמְּשָׁה לְאַחַר אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר שֶׁל יְמֵי לֵידָה וְנִתְעַבְּרָה וְהִפִּילָה בְּתוֹךְ שְׁמוֹנִים:


המקשה בתוך שמונים של נקבה – as for example, that she engaged in sexual intercourse [after immersing in a Mikveh] fourteen days after giving birth [to a female] and became pregnant and then aborted [the fetus] within eighty days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:2)


**כָּל דָּמִים שֶׁהִיא רוֹאָה טְהוֹרִים.** דְּכָל תּוֹךְ מְלֹאת הָוֵי דָּם טָהוֹר:


כל דמים שהיא רואה טהורים – for all of it is within the completion [of her period of purification], which is the blood of purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:3)


**וְרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר מְטַמֵּא.** נִדָּה, דְּכֵיוָן דִּבְקֹשִׁי בָּא לָאו דַּם טֹהַר הוּא אֶלָּא מֵחֲמַת וָלָד שֶׁבְּמֵעֶיהָ, וּמִזִּיבָה הוּא דְּטַהֲרֵיהּ רַחֲמָנָא לַקִּשּׁוּי, וְלֹא מִנִּדָּה:


ור' אליעזר מטמא – [she is defiled as] a menstruant woman/Niddah. For since it (i.e., the blood) came through hard labor, it is not the blood of purity, but rather on account of the fetus that is in her womb. But the All-Merciful (i.e., God) purifies it from her being in flux on account of her labor, but not from being a menstruant woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:4)


**שֶׁהֶחְמִיר בְּדַם הַשֹּׁפִי.** כְּגוֹן שְׁאָר יוֹלֶדֶת שֶׁרוֹאָה שְׁלֹשָׁה בְּשֹׁפִי וְיָלְדָה, הֲרֵי זוֹ יוֹלֶדֶת בְּזוֹב:


שהחמיר בדם השופי - as for example, the other women giving birth who see three days of relief and then gave birth, is a woman who gives birth with a flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:5)


**הֵקֵל בְּדַם הַקֹּשִׁי.** לְטַהֲרָהּ מִזִּיבָה:


היקל בדם הקושי – to purify her from her flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:6)


**מְקוֹם שֶׁהֵקֵל בְּדַם הַשֹּׁפִי.** לְטַהֲרָהּ מִכָּל טֻמְאָה, כְּגוֹן תּוֹךְ מְלֹאת:


מקום שהיקל בדם השופי – to purify her from all defilement, as for example, within her completion [of the period of purification].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:7)


**אֵינוֹ דִּין שֶׁנָּקֵל בְּדַם הַקֹּשִׁי.** לְטַהֲרוֹ מִכָּל טֻמְאוֹת וַאֲפִלּוּ מִנִּדָּה:


אינו דין שנקל בדם הקושי – to purify the blood from all defilements and even from being a menstruating woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:6:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:6:8)


**דַּיּוֹ לַבָּא מִן הַדִּין.** כְּגוֹן קֹשִׁי שֶׁל תּוֹךְ מְלֹאת שֶׁאַתֶּם לְמֵדִים מִקֹּשִׁי שֶׁל עָלְמָא, דַּיּוֹ לִהְיוֹת כַּנִּדּוֹן כְּקֹשִׁי דְּעָלְמָא, מַה קֹּשִׁי דְּעָלְמָא טָמֵא בְּנִדָּה, אַף קֹשִׁי דְּתוֹךְ מְלֹאת טָמֵא בְּנִדָּה:


דיו לבא מן הדין [להיות כנדון] – as for example the labor that is within the completion that [of the period of purification following the birth of a child] that you learn from the undefined period of labor, it is enough to be like that which is judged to be like an undefined period of labor, for just as an undefined period of labor – the woman is defiled as a menstruating woman, so the labor that is within the completion [of that period of purification following the birth of a child] is defiled with the status of being a menstruating woman/Niddah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:1)


**כָּל אַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם.** שֶׁאַחַר שִׁבְעַת יְמֵי נִדָּה:


כל אחד עשר יום – after the seven days of menstruation/Niddah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:2)


**בְּחֶזְקַת טָהֳרָה.** וְאֵינָהּ צְרִיכָה לִבְדֹּק עַצְמָהּ. דַּהֲלָכָה לְמֹשֶׁה מִסִּינַי שֶׁאֵין בֵּין נִדָּה לְנִדָּה פָּחוֹת מֵאַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם:


בחזקת טהרה – and she doesn’t have to examine herself, for it is a Halakha transmitted to Moses at Sinai (albeit not written down) that there is no distinction between one menstruating cycle to another that is less than eleven days.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:3)


**יָשְׁבָה לָהּ וְלֹא בָדְקָה.** מַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, בִּימֵי נִדָּתָהּ בַּעְיָא בְּדִיקָה. יָשְׁבָה וְלֹא בָּדְקָה, שָׁגְגָה, נֶאֶנְסָה, הֵזִידָה וְלֹא בָּדְקָה, טְהוֹרָה:


ישבה לה ולא בקדה – our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: During the days of her menstruating period it requires examination. If she sat and did not examine herself inadvertently, or she was under constraint, or willfully didn’t examine herself, she is [ritually] pure/clean.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:4)


**הִגִּיעַ שְׁעַת וִסְתָּהּ וְלֹא בָדְקָה.** אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁבָּדְקָה אַחַר כָּךְ וּמָצְאָה טְהוֹרָה, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא בָּדְקָה בִּשְׁעַת וֶסְתָּהּ, טְמֵאָה, דְּאֹרַח בִּזְמַנּוֹ בָּא:


הגיעה שעת וסתה ולא בדקה – even though she examined herself afterwards and found herself to be pure/clean, since she didn’t examine herself at the time of her period, that she is impure/unclean, for her menstruation comes at its [appropriate] time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:5)


**בְּמַחֲבֵא.** מֵחֲמַת לִסְטִים אוֹ חַיִל שֶׁבָּא לָעִיר. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר:


במחבא (in hiding) – on account of robbers or an army that comes came to the city. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Meir.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:6)


**אֲבָל יְמֵי הַזָּב וְהַזָּבָה.** שִׁבְעַת יָמִים נְקִיִּים שֶׁצְּרִיכִים לִסְפֹּר:


אבל ימי הזב והזבה – the seven clean days that she needs to count.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:7)


**וְשׁוֹמֶרֶת יוֹם.** כְּגוֹן הָרוֹאָה יוֹם אֶחָד אוֹ שְׁנֵי יָמִים בִּימֵי זִיבָתָהּ, אֵינָהּ סוֹפֶרֶת אֶלָּא יוֹם אֶחָד בִּנְקִיּוּת, וְטוֹבֶלֶת לָעֶרֶב וּטְהוֹרָה לְבַעְלָהּ:


ושומרת יום – as for example, that she sees [blood] one day or two days during the days of her flux, she does not count other than one day in cleanliness and she ritually immerses [herself] in the evening and is pure/clean to her husband.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 4:7:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/4:7:8)


**הֲרֵי אֵלּוּ בְּחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה.** כֵּיוָן שֶׁנִּפְתַּח מַעְיָנָהּ וְרָאֲתָה הַיּוֹם, אַף עַל גַּב דְּתוֹךְ אַחַד עָשָׂר הִיא, הַוְיָא לְמָחָר בְּחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה:


הרי אלו בחזקת טומאה – since her fountain was opened up and she saw it (i.e., blood) today, even though it is within the eleven [days], she is on the morrow in the presumption of impurity/uncleanness.



## Chapter 5


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:1)


**יוֹצֵא דֹפֶן.** אִשָּׁה שֶׁפָּתְחוּ [מֵעֶיהָ] עַל יְדֵי סַם וְהוֹצִיאוּ הָעֻבָּר לַחוּץ וְנִתְרַפְּאָה:


יוצא דופן – a woman [whose womb] they opened through a drug and they removed the fetus and then she healed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:2)


**אֵין יוֹשְׁבִין עָלָיו יְמֵי טֻמְאָה.** שִׁבְעָה לְזָכָר וּשְׁבוּעַיִם לִנְקֵבָה:


אין יושבין עליו ימי טומאה – seven [days] for [the birth of] a male and fourteen [days] for a female.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:3)


**וִימֵי טָהֳרָה.** וְאֵין לָהּ דַּם טֹהַר:


וימי טהרה – she does not have blood of purity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:4)


**וְאֵין חַיָּבִין עָלָיו קָרְבָּן.** שֶׁל יוֹלֶדֶת. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יב) אִשָּׁה כִּי תַזְרִיעַ וְיָלְדָה, עַד שֶׁתֵּלֵד מִמָּקוֹם שֶׁמַּזְרַעַת:


ואין חייבין עליו קרבן – of a child-bearing mother, as it is written (Leviticus 12:2): “When a woman at childbirth bears a male, [she shall be impure seven days; she has be impure as at the time of her menstrual infirmity],” until she gives birth from the place where she emits a secretion at coition.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:5)


**רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן אוֹמֵר הֲרֵי זֶה כְיָלוּד.** שֶׁנֶּאֱמַר (שם) וְאִם נְקֵבָה תֵלֵד, לְרַבּוֹת יוֹצֵא דֹּפֶן. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


רבי שמעון אומר הרי זה כילוד (like one born naturally) – as it states (Leviticus 12:5): “ If she bears a female, [she shall be impure two weeks as during her menstruation, and she shall remain in a state of blood purification for sixty-six days],” to include that which goes for from the side (i.e., delivered by Caesarean section). But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Shimon.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:6)


**כָּל הַנָּשִׁים מִטַּמְּאוֹת.** בְּנִדָּה מִשֶּׁיָּצָא הַדָּם מִן הַמָּקוֹר וְהִגִּיעַ לַבַּיִת הַחִיצוֹן, וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּהֶעֱמִידוּהוּ כָּתְלֵי בֵּית הָרֶחֶם וְלֹא פֵּרַשׁ לַחוּץ. דִּכְתִיב [שָׁם טו] דָּם יִהְיֶה זֹבָהּ בִּבְשָׂרָהּ, מְלַמֵּד שֶׁמְּטַמְּאָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁעֲדַיִן הוּא בִּבְשָׂרָהּ. וּבַיִת הַחִיצוֹן מִקְרֵי מָקוֹם שֶׁהָאֵבָר דָּשׁ בּוֹ בִּשְׁעַת בִּיאָה:


כל השנים מיטמאות – as a menstruating woman/נדה from when the blood goes out from the interior of the womb where the menses are discharged and reached the vagina/בית החיצון , but even though the enclosures of the womb preserved it and it did not pass outwards. As it is written (Leviticus 15:19): “[When a woman has a discharge,] her discharge being blood from her body/דם יהיה זבה מבשרה,” it teaches that she defiles even though it is still within her body,” and the vagina/בית החיצון is called the place where the limb threshes (i.e., euphemism for sexual contact) at the time of sexual intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:1:7)


**אֵינָן מִטַּמְּאִין עַד שֶׁתֵּצֵא טֻמְאָתָן לַחוּץ.** דִּכְתִיב בַּזָּב (שם) אִישׁ כִּי יִהְיֶה זָב מִבְּשָׂרוֹ, עַד שֶׁתֵּצֵא זוֹבוֹ מִבְּשָׂרוֹ. וּבְבַעַל קֶרִי כְּתִיב (שם כב) אֲשֶׁר תֵּצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ שִׁכְבַת זָרַע:


אינן מטמאין עד שתצא טומאתן לחוץ – as is written concerning a person with gonorrhea/בזב (Leviticus 15:2): “When any man has a discharge issuing from his member/איש איש כי יהיה זב מבשרו, [he is impure],” until his flux leaves from his flesh, but regarding someone with a [nocturnal] emission, it is written (Leviticus 15:16 – the printed edition incorrectly lists chapter 22): “When a man has an emission of semen/ואיש אשר תצא ממנו שכבת זרע, [he shall bathe his whole body in water and remain impure until evening].”



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:1)


**שֶׁנִּזְדַּעְזְעוּ אֵבָרָיו.** שֶׁנֶּעֶקְרָה שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע מִגּוּפוֹ:


שנזדעזעו איבריו – that semen was removed from his body.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:2)


**אוֹחֵז בָּאַמָּה.** וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּכָל הָאוֹחֵז בָּאַמָּה וּמַשְׁתִּין כְּאִלּוּ מֵבִיא מַבּוּל לָעוֹלָם, הָכָא כֵּיוָן דְּנֶעֶקְרָה שִׁכְבַת זֶרַע שׁוּב אֵינוֹ חוֹזֵר וּמִתְחַמֵּם לַעֲקֹר עוֹד מִיָּד בְּאוֹתָהּ שָׁעָה:


אוחז באמה – and even though that whomever grabs hold of his membrum virile and urinates it is as if he brings a flood to the world, here since semen was removed, it does not return and he warms himself (i.e., masturbates) to immediately remove more at that time.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:3)


**וּמְטַמְּאִין.** זוֹב וְשִׁכְבַת זֶרַע:


ומטמאין – flux and semen,


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:2:4)


**בְּכָל שֶׁהֵן.** לָאָדָם הַיּוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ. אֲבָל הַנּוֹגֵעַ, אֵינוֹ טָמֵא עַד שֶׁיִּגַּע בְּכָעֲדָשָׁה:


At a miniscule amount, to a person that it comes out from. But coming into contact [through touching], that person is not impure until he touches a lentil’s bulk (i.e., usually, the standard size, which is the Egyptian lentil. See Tractate Kelim, Chapter 17, Mishnah 8).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:1)


**מִטַּמְּאָה בְנִדָּה.** אִם רָאֲתָה דָּם:


מיטמאה בנדה – if she (i.e., a one-day-old girl) saw blood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:2)


**בַּת עֲשָׂרָה יָמִים מִטַּמְּאָה בְזִיבָה.** אִם רָאֲתָה שְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים רְצוּפִים לְאַחַר שִׁבְעָה. דְּכָל דָּם שֶׁרוֹאָה בְּתוֹךְ שִׁבְעָה דַּם נִדָּה הוּא, הִלְכָּךְ אִי אֶפְשָׁר לָהּ לִטָּמֵא בַּזִּיבָה דְּהַיְנוּ לִהְיוֹת זָבָה גְּדוֹלָה, עַד עֲשָׂרָה יָמִים:


בת עשרה ימים מיטמאה בזיבה – if she saw it (i.e., blood) on three consecutive days after the seven [days of Niddah menstruation]. For all blood that she sees within seven days is the blood of Niddah/menstruating woman, therefore, it is impossible for her to become defiled through her flux – that is, to become having a large flux until ten days [have passed].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:3)


**תִּינוֹק בֶּן יוֹם אֶחָד מִטַּמֵּא בְזִיבָה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם טו) אִישׁ אִישׁ כִּי יִהְיֶה זָב, לִמֵּד עַל תִּינוֹק בֶּן יוֹמוֹ שֶׁמִּטַּמֵּא בְּזִיבָה:


תינוק בן יום אחד מיטמא בזיבה – as it is written (Leviticus 15:2): “When any man has a discharge issuing/איש איש כי יהיה זב [from his member, he is impure],” that teaches about a one-day-old male child that becomes unclean through contact with gonorrhea.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:4)


**וּמִטַּמֵּא בִנְגָעִים.** דְּגַבֵּי נְגָעִים כְּתִיב (שם יג) אָדָם כִּי יִהְיֶה בְעוֹר בְּשָׂרוֹ, וְתִינוֹק בֶּן יוֹמוֹ אָדָם הוּא, דְּאָדָם כָּל שֶׁהוּא מַשְׁמַע:


ומיטמא בנגעים – for concerning plagues it is written (Leviticus 13:2): “When a person has on the skin of his body/אדם כי-יהיה בעור-בשרו [a swelling, a rash, or a discoloration, and it develops into a scale affection on the skin of his body],” but a male baby who is one-day-old is a person, for implies a person of any sort [of age].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:5)


**וּמִטַּמֵּא בִטְמֵא מֵת.** דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יט) וְעַל הַנְּפָשׁוֹת אֲשֶׁר הָיוּ שָׁם, נֶפֶשׁ כָּל שֶׁהוּא:


ומיטמא בטמא מת – as it is written (Numbers 19:18): “and people who were there/ועל-הנפשות אשר היו-שם,” a person of any sort [of age].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:6)


**וְזוֹקֵק לְיִבּוּם.** אִם נוֹלַד יוֹם אֶחָד קֹדֶם מִיתַת אָחִיו. אֲבָל נוֹלַד לְאַחַר מִיתַת אָחִיו, אֵינוֹ זוֹקֵק, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כח) כִּי יֵשְׁבוּ אַחִים יַחְדָּו, שֶׁהָיְתָה לָהֶם יְשִׁיבָה אַחַת בָּעוֹלָם:


וזוקק ליבום (and imposes requirement of levirate marriage) – if he was born one day prior to the death of his brother. But if he was born after the death of his brother, he does impose the requirement [of levirate marriage], as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:5 – though the printed edition lists Chapter 28): “When brothers dwell together/כי-ישבו אחים יחדיו [and one of them dies and leaves no son, the wife of he deceased shall not be married to a stranger, outside the family.],that they had one dwelling [together] in the world.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:7)


**וּפוֹטֵר מִן הַיִּבּוּם.** אִם נוֹלַד לְאַחַר מִיתַת אָבִיו וְחָיָה שָׁעָה אַחַת וּמֵת. אֲבָל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁמְּעֻבֶּרֶת, אֵינוֹ מַתִּירָהּ לִנָּשֵׂא:


ופוטר מן היבום (and frees [a sister-in-law from the requirement of levirate marriage) – if he was born after the death of his father and lived one day and die. But all the time that she is pregnant, she is not permitted to marry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:8)


**וּמַאֲכִיל בַּתְּרוּמָה.** בַּת יִשְׂרָאֵל שֶׁנִּסֵּת לְכֹהֵן וּמֵת, וְנוֹלַד בֵּן לְאַחַר מִיתָתוֹ, אוֹכֶלֶת בִּשְׁבִילוֹ בּוֹ בַּיּוֹם שֶׁנּוֹלַד, כְּדִכְתִיב (ויקרא כב) וִילִיד בֵּיתוֹ הֵם יֹאכְלוּ בְלַחְמוֹ, וְדָרְשִׁינַן יַאֲכִילוּ אֶת אִמָּם. אֲבָל הִנִּיחָהּ מְעֻבֶּרֶת, אֵינָהּ אוֹכֶלֶת בִּשְׁבִילוֹ עַד שֶׁיִּוָּלֵד:


ומאכיל בתרומה – a Jewish daughter that married a Kohen and [he died], and a son was born after his death, she consumes [heave-offering] for him on the day that he was born, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): “and those that are born into his household may eat of his food”/ויליד ביתו הם יאכלו בלחמו , and we expound upon it that they feed their mother. But if she was left pregnant [when he died], she does not eat for him (i.e., the unborn fetus) until he is born.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:9)


**וּפוֹסֵל מִן הַתְּרוּמָה.** כְּגוֹן כֹּהֵן שֶׁהָיוּ לוֹ שְׁתֵּי נָשִׁים, אַחַת גְּרוּשָׁה שֶׁעָבַר וּנְשָׂאָהּ כְּשֶׁהִיא גְרוּשָׁה, וְאַחַת שֶׁאֵינָהּ גְּרוּשָׁה, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בָּנִים מִשֶּׁאֵינָהּ גְּרוּשָׁה, וַעֲבָדִים שֶׁנָּפְלוּ לָהֶן בִּירֻשָּׁה אוֹכְלִים בַּתְּרוּמָה בִּשְׁבִילָן, דִּכְתִיב (שם) וְכֹהֵן כִּי יִקְנֶה נֶפֶשׁ קִנְיַן כַּסְפּוֹ, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ בֶּן יוֹם אֶחָד מִן הַגְּרוּשָׁה וְהוּא חָלָל, וְיֵשׁ לוֹ חֵלֶק בָּעֲבָדִים, וּפוֹסֵל אֶת כֻּלָּן מִן הַתְּרוּמָה, דְּאֵין בְּרֵרָה לָדַעַת מִי מֵהֶן מַגִּיעַ לְחֶלְקוֹ. וְדַוְקָא בֶּן יוֹם אֶחָד דְּרָאוּי לִירֻשָּׁה. אֲבָל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁלֹּא נוֹלַד, אוֹכְלִים כֻּלָּן, דְּאֵין זְכִיָּה לְעֻבָּר:


ופוסל מן התרומה – as for example a Kohen that had two wives, one who was divorced that he transgressed and married when she was a divorcee and one who was not divorced, and he has children from the one who was not divorced, but servants that fell to them through inheritance consume heave-offering for them, as it is written (Leviticus 22:11): ‘but a person who is a priest’s property by purchase may eat of them”/וכהן כי-יקנה נפש קנין כספו הוא יאכל בו, and he has a son one day from the woman who was divorced [at the time of their marriage] and he is a חלל/son born to a priest and a woman whom the priest is forbidden to marry [and that child is considered an Israelite – and his daughter or widow may not marry a priest], but he has a portion in the servants, but he invalidates all of them from [consuming] the heave-offering, for there is no choice to know to which of them gets his portion, and especially a child who is one-day old who is worthy of inheritance. But all the while that he is not [yet] born, everyone consumes [heave-offering], for there is no claim/possession for a fetus.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:10)


**וְנוֹחֵל וּמַנְחִיל.** נוֹחֵל אֶת הָאֵם. אִם מֵתָה אִמּוֹ בַּיּוֹם שֶׁנּוֹלַד, הֲרֵי הוּא נוֹחֲלָהּ. וּכְשֶׁמֵּת הוּא, בָּאִין אֶחָיו מֵאָבִיו וְיוֹרְשִׁים הֵימֶנּוּ נִכְסֵי אִמּוֹ. אֲבָל עֻבָּר שֶׁבְּמֵעֶיהָ, אִם מֵתָה מְעֻבֶּרֶת אֵינוֹ נוֹחֵל אֶת אִמּוֹ לְהַנְחִיל לְאֶחָיו מֵאָבִיו, שֶׁהֲרֵי מֵת הוּא קֹדֶם אִמּוֹ, וְאֵין הַבֵּן יוֹרֵשׁ אֶת אִמּוֹ בַּקֶּבֶר לְהַנְחִיל לְאֶחָיו מֵאָבִיו:


ונוחל ומנחיל – and he inherits the mother. If his mother died on the day that he was born, he inherits her. But when he dies, his brothers from his father and inherit from him the property of his mother, but the fetus that is her womb, if she dies pregnant, he does not inherit his mother to transmit by legal succession/inheritance to his brothers from his father, for he died prior to his mother, and a son does not inherit his mother in the grave to transmit succession to his brothers from his father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:11)


**וְהַהוֹרְגוֹ חַיָּב.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כד) וְאִישׁ כִּי יַכֶּה כָּל נֶפֶשׁ, מִכָּל מָקוֹם:


וההורגו חייב – as it is written (Leviticus 24:17): “If anyone kills any human being/ואיש כי-יכה כל-נפש אדם, [shall be put to death],” in any case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:3:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:3:12)


**כְּחָתָן שָׁלֵם.** שֶׁחַיָּבִים לְהִתְאַבֵּל עָלָיו. וְהָנֵי מִלֵּי, דְּקִים לְהוּ בְּגַוֵּיהּ שֶׁכָּלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו, אֲבָל לֹא קִים לְהוּ שֶׁכָּלוּ לוֹ חֳדָשָׁיו, אֵין חַיָּבִין לְהִתְאַבֵּל עָלָיו עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים וְאֶחָד יוֹם:


כחתן שלם - that they are obligated to mourn over him, and these words, that I am sure about this person that he completed his months [of being alive beyond pregnancy], but if he is not certain about this person that he completed his months [of being alive beyond pregnancy], they are not obligated to mourn over him until he will be thirty-one days old.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:1)


**מִתְקַדֶּשֶׁת בְּבִיאָה.** אִם מְסָרָהּ אָבִיהָ לְכָךְ:


מתקדשת בביאה – if her father handed her over for this.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:2)


**וְאִם בָּא עָלֶיהָ יָבָם קְנָאָהּ.** לִהְיוֹת אִשְׁתּוֹ לְכָל דָּבָר, וְיוֹרְשָׁהּ וּמִטַּמֵּא לָהּ וְזָכָה בְּנִכְסֵי אָחִיו:


ואם בא עליה יבם קנאה – to be his wife for every matter, and he inherits her and defiles her and takes possession of the property of his brother.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:3)


**וְחַיָּבִין עָלֶיהָ מִשּׁוּם אֵשֶׁת אִישׁ.** אִם קִבֵּל בָּהּ אָבִיהָ קִדּוּשִׁין מֵאֶחָד, וּבָא עָלֶיהָ אַחֵר, נֶהֱרָג עָלֶיהָ, דְּבִיאָתָהּ בִּיאָה:


וחייבין עליה משום אשת איש – if her father received her betrothal for her from one, and another person came upon her [through an act of sexual intercourse], he is put to death on her account, for her act of sexual intercourse is sexual intercourse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:4)


**וּמְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ.** אִם הִיא נִדָּה. אֲבָל פָּחוֹת מִכָּן, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִיא מְטַמְּאָה בְּמַגָּע, אֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה אֶת בּוֹעֲלָהּ מִשּׁוּם בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה לְטֻמְאַת שִׁבְעָה, אֶלָּא מִשּׁוּם נוֹגֵעַ וּלְטֻמְאַת עֶרֶב:


ומטמאה את בועלה – if she was a menstruating woman/Niddah. But less than this (i.e., three-years and one-day old), even though she dies through contact, she does not defile the person who had intercourse with her because of [the transgression of] a person who has intercourse with a menstruating woman would lead to seven-days of defilement [which does not apply], but because of contact it is defilement for an evening.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:5)


**תַּחְתּוֹן.** שֶׁל בּוֹעֵל נִדָּה:


תחתון – of a person who has intercourse with a menstruating woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:6)


**כְּעֶלְיוֹנוֹ.** שֶׁל זָב, שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא אֳכָלִין וּמַשְׁקִין וְלֹא אָדָם וְכֵלִים:


כעליונו – of a person who has gonorrhea, which defiles food and liquids, but not persons or utensils.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:7)


**מִן הַפְּסוּלִין.** כְּגוֹן נָכְרִי, וְעֶבֶד, וְנָתִין, מַמְזֵר, אוֹ חָלָל:


מן הפסולין – as for example, a heathen and a slave and a descendant of the Gibeonites (see Joshua 9:27) and a Mamzer or the child of a Kohen who married a divorcee.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:8)


**פְּסָלָהּ.** מִתְּרוּמַת אָבִיהָ, אִם הִיא בַּת כֹּהֵן:


פסלה – from [consuming] the heave-offering of her father, if she is the daughter of a Kohen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:9)


**מִכָּל הָעֲרָיוֹת.** כְּגוֹן אָבִיהָ אוֹ חָמִיהָ:


מכל העריות – such as her father or her father-in-law.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:10)


**וְהִיא פְטוּרָה.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁהִיא קְטַנָּה וְאֵינָהּ בַּת עֳנָשִׁים:


והיא פטורה – because she is a minor and is not liable for punishments.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:4:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:4:11)


**כְּנוֹתֵן אֶצְבַּע בָּעָיִן.** שֶׁדּוֹמֵעַ וְחוֹזֵר לִכְמוֹת שֶׁהָיָה, אַף כָּאן חוֹזֶרֶת בְּתוּלָה כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיְתָה:


כנותן אצבע בעין – that he sheds tears and returns to the status that he was, even here, she returns to being a virgin like she was.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:1)


**שֶׁבָּא עַל יְבִמְתּוֹ קְנָאָהּ.** וְזָכָה בְּנִכְסֵי אָחִיו. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין קִנְיָן לְקָטָן, שָׁאנֵי הָכָא דְּמִן שְׁמַיָּא אַקְנוֹיַהּ נִיהָלֵיהּ:


שבא על יבמתו קנאה- and he takes possession of the property of his brother. But even though it is not a complete acquisition/purchase, it is different here for it is from Heaven (i.e., from the Torah) that it was acquired by him.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:2)


**וְאֵינוֹ נוֹתֵן גֵּט עַד שֶׁיַּגְדִּיל.** אִם בָּא לְגָרְשָׁהּ. דְּקִדּוּשֵׁי אָחִיו קִדּוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִים, וְגֵרוּשִׁין שֶׁל זֶה אֵינָן גֵּרוּשִׁין גְּמוּרִין. וְדַוְקָא כְּשֶׁבָּא עָלֶיהָ לְאַחַר שֶׁהִגְדִּיל, דַּי לָהּ בְּגֵט. אֲבָל אִם לֹא בָּא עָלֶיהָ לְאַחַר שֶׁהִגְדִּיל, צְרִיכָה גֵּט וַחֲלִיצָה:


ואינו נותן גט עד שיגדיל – if he comes to divorce her, for the betrothal of his brother is a complete betrothal/Kiddushin, but the divorce of this one (i.e., the levir of nine years of age) is not a complete divorce. And especially when he came upon her (i.e., in terms of sexual intercourse) after he had grown up (i.e., reached the age of thirteen years and one day), it is enough for her with a Jewish bill of divorce. But if he had not come upon her after he had grown up, she needs a Jewish bill of divorce and Halitzah (i.e., the removal by he widow of a special sandal from the foot of one of the deceased husband’s brothers).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:3)


**וּמִטַּמֵּא בְנִדָּה.** אִם בָּא עָלֶיהָ בְּנִדָּתָהּ. דְּבִיאָתוֹ בִּיאָה לְכָל דָּבָר:


ומטמא בנדה – if he came upon her while she was in her menstruating state (i.e., Niddah), for his act of sexual intercourse/coition is an act of sexual intercourse/coition in all manners.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:4)


**וּפוֹסֵל.** כְּגוֹן נָכְרִי וְעֶבֶד אוֹ חָלָל בֶּן תֵּשַׁע שָׁנִים שֶׁבָּא עַל הַכֹּהֶנֶת, פְּסָלָהּ מִתְּרוּמַת אָבִיהָ:


ופוסל – as for example, a heathen and/or a slave or the son born to a Kohen and a woman to whom the Kohen is forbidden to marry who is nine years of age who comes upon the daughter of a Kohen , has invalidated her [from partaking] from the heave-offering of her father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:5)


**וְאֵינוֹ מַאֲכִיל.** אִם הוּא כֹּהֵן וְנָשָׂא יִשְׂרְאֵלִית, אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִילָהּ בַּתְּרוּמָה, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין קִנְיָנוֹ קִנְיָן גָּמוּר. וַאֲפִלּוּ הוּא יָבָם דְּקַנְיַהּ בְּהַךְ בִּיאָה כְּדַאֲמַרַן, אֲפִלּוּ הָכִי אֵינוֹ מַאֲכִילָהּ בַּתְּרוּמָה:


ואינו מאכיל – if he is a Kohen and married an Israelite woman, he does not feed her heave-offering, because his acquisition [of her] is not a complete acquisition. And even if he a levir whose acquisition through this act of coition is as we stated, even so, he does not feed her heave-offering/Terumah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:6)


**וּפוֹסֵל אֶת הַבְּהֵמָה.** אִם רְבָעָהּ עַל פִּי עֵד אֶחָד:


ופוסל את הבהמה – if the beast copulates with her (i.e., a human) through the testimony of one witness.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:5:7)


**וְנִסְקֶלֶת עַל יָדוֹ.** אִם יֵשׁ שְׁנֵי עֵדִים:


נסקלת על ידו – if there are two witnesses.



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:1)


**נְדָרֶיהָ נִבְדָּקִים.** אִם יָדְעָה לְשֵׁם מִי נָדְרָה, לְשֵׁם מִי הִקְדִּישָׁה, נָדְרָה נֶדֶר:


נדריה נבדקין (her vows are examined) – if she knew in the name of whom she took a vow and in the name of whom she dedicated [something to the Temple], her vow is a [legitimate] vow.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:6:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:2)


**נְדָרֶיהָ קַיָּמִין.** וְהוּא שֶׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת. וְכֵן בֶּן שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד נְדָרָיו קַיָּמִים, דַּוְקָא כְּשֶׁהֵבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת:


נדריה קיימין – as long as she brought forth two [pubic] hairs. And similarly, a male who is thirteen years and one day old, his vows are confirmed, especially if he brought forth two [pubic] hairs.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:6:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:3)


**וּבוֹדְקִין כָּל שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה.** מַהוּ דְּתֵימָא הוֹאִיל וְאָמַר מַר שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם בַּשָּׁנָה חֲשׁוּבִים שָׁנָה, הֵיכָא דִּבְדָקְנוּהָ שְׁלֹשִׁים יוֹם קַמָּאֵי דִּשְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה וְלֹא יָדְעָה לְפָרֵשׁ לְשֵׁם מִי נָדְרָה, תּוּ לֹא נִבְדֹּק, וּלְחַזְקָהּ כִּקְטַנָּה, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


ובודקין כל שתים עשרה – that you might have though since that as the Master stated, thirty-days in a year is considered a [full] year, where she was that she was examined thirty days prior to [her becoming] twelve [years of age and one day], and she didn’t know how to explain in the name of whom she had vowed, further we don’t examine [her] and she has the presumption of being a minor, this comes to teach us that this is not the case.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:6:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:4)


**קֹדֶם לַזְּמַן הַזֶּה.** קֹדֶם רֹאשׁ הַשָּׁנָה שֶׁל שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה לְתִינוֹקֶת וְשֶׁל שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לְתִינוֹק:


קודם לזמן הזה – prior to the beginning of the twelfth year of a female child and [prior to the beginning of the] thirteenth [year] of a male child.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:6:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:6:5)


**אֵין נִדְרֵיהֶן נֶדֶר.** דִּקְטַנִּים הֵן:


אין נדריהם נדר – for they are minors.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:1)


**פַּגָּהּ.** סְמָדַר. כְּמוֹ הַתְּאֵנָה חָנְטָה פַגֶּיהָ (שיר השירים ב). כָּךְ הַתִּינוֹקֶת שֶׁאֵין לָהּ סִימָן לֹא בַּדַּדִּין וְלֹא בַּשְּׂעָרוֹת:


פגה – סמדר/in the budding stage, like (Song of Songs 2:13): “The green figs form on the fig tree/התאנה חנטה פגיה, [The vines in blossom/סמדר give off fragrance] Such is the young female child that lacks a sign, neither in the breasts nor in the hair.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:2)


**בֹּחַל.** הַפְּרִי הַקָּרוֹב לְהִתְבַּשֵּׁל. וּלְשׁוֹן חֲכָמִים הוּא, הַתְּאֵנִים מִשֶּׁיַּבְחִילוּ חַיָּבִים בְּמַעֲשֵׂר. כָּךְ כְּשֶׁיֵּשׁ לָהּ סִימַן דַּדִּין קְצָת, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת וְנַעֲרָה הִיא:


בוחל – the fruit that is close to ripening. And it is the language of the Sages. The figs are subject to tithes when they are in that state of ripening called בוחל (see Tractate Maaserot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 2 and Tractate Niddah 47a – which is defined as when their heads grow white). Such is the case when she (i.e., the woman) has the sign of breasts slightly = when it is known that she has brought forth two [pubic] hairs, and she is a young woman/נערה.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:3)


**צֶמֶל.** לְשׁוֹן נוֹטָרִיקוֹן יָצְתָה מְלֵאָה. שֶׁיֶּשׁ לָהּ סִימָן מֻבְהָק בְּדַדֶּיהָ וְהִיא בּוֹגֶרֶת:


צמל – it is the language of an abbreviation/acrostic, that she has fully blossomed/come forth completely. That she has a clear sign in her breasts that she is an adult woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:7:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:7:4)


**בָּזוֹ וּבָזוֹ.** אֲפִלּוּ כְּשֶׁהִיא נַעֲרָה, אָבִיהָ זַכַּאי בַּכֹּל. דִּכְתִיב (במדבר ל) בִּנְעֻרֶיהָ בֵּית אָבִיהָ, כָּל שְׁבַח נְעוּרֶיהָ לְאָבִיהָ:


בזו ובזו – even when she is a young woman, her father is in possession of everything, as it is written (Numbers 30:4): “while still in her father’s household by reason of her youth.”



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:1)


**אֵיזוֹ הִיא סִימָנֶיהָ.** אַצֶּמֶל קָאֵי:


איזו הוא סימניה (what are her signs of puberty) – it is referring to צמל/the stage of complete puberty in a woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:8:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:2)


**קֶמֶט.** שֶׁגָּדְלוּ דַּדֶּיהָ עַד שֶׁכְּפוּלִים מְעַט עַל הֶחָזֶה וְיֵרָאֶה כְּקֶמֶט:


קמט – her breasts grew until they are folded a bit upon the chest and they appear as a fold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:8:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:3)


**מִשֶּׁיַּטּוּ הַדַּדִּין.** גְּדוֹלִים יוֹתֵר מִקֶּמֶט:


משיטו דדיה (that her breasts hang down) – they are larger than a fold.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:8:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:4)


**הַפִּטֹּמֶת.** רֹאשׁ הַדַּד. כְּמוֹ נִטְּלָה פִּטְמָתוֹ [סֻכָּה פ"ג מ"ו]:


הפיטומת (when the surroundings of the nipple begin to grow dark) – the top of the breast, like (Tractate Sukkah, Chapter 3, Mishnah 6): ניטלה פטמתו/if the pestle-lie protuberance of an Etrog is taken out.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:8:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:8:5)


**עַל הָעֹקֶץ.** חֻדּוֹ שֶׁל דַּד שֶׁתִּינוֹק יוֹנֵק. וּפִטֹּמֶת הַיְנוּ סְבִיבוֹת הָעֹקֶץ. וְכָל הַסִּימָנִים הַלָּלוּ כְּשֶׁנִּרְאוּ בְּאִשָּׁה הֲרֵי הִיא בּוֹגֶרֶת, לְמַר כִּדְאִית לֵיהּ וּלְמַר כִּדְאִית לֵיהּ, וְשׁוּב אֵין לְאָבִיהָ רְשׁוּת בָּהּ. וַהֲלָכָה כְּדִבְרֵי כֻּלָּם לְהַחְמִיר. הִלְכָּךְ אִם נִרְאָה בָּהּ אֶחָד מִן הַסִּימָנִים הַלָּלוּ, דָּיְנִינַן בָּהּ דִּין בּוֹגֶרֶת לְחֻמְרָא. אֲבָל לֹא לְקֻלָּא, שֶׁאִם קִדְּשָׁה אֶת עַצְמָהּ, צְרִיכָה גֵּט שֶׁמָּא בּוֹגֶרֶת הִיא. וְאִם אָבִיהָ קִדְּשָׁהּ, צְרִיכָה נַמִּי גֵּט, דְּשֶׁמָּא עֲדַיִן נַעֲרָה הִיא, עַד שֶׁיִּהְיוּ בָּהּ כָּל הַסִּימָנִים הַלָּלוּ כֻּלָּם, וְאָז תֵּחָשֵׁב בּוֹגֶרֶת בֵּין לְקֻלָּא בֵּין לְחֻמְרָא:


על העוקץ -point of the nipple that the baby sucks on. And the surroundings of the nipple which is around the point, and all of these signs of puberty when they are appear in a woman, she is an adult. The one in accordance in with the opinion he holds (i.e., for example, Rabbi Akiva) and the other in accordance with the opinion he holds (i.e., for example, Rabbi Yossi), but furthermore, the father has no authority/control over her. But the Halakha is according to the words of all of them in the strictest opinions. Therefore, it one of these signs of puberty appear on her, we judge concerning her the law of an adult woman stringently. But not for a lenient decision, that if she betrothed herself, she needs a Jewish bill of divorce for perhaps she was an adult woman. But if her father betrothed her, she also needs a Jewish bill of divorce, lest she was a young woman/נערה, until there will be in her all of these signs of puberty entirely and then she will be considered an adult woman, whether for leniency or for stringency.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:1)


**וְהִיא אַיְלוֹנִית.** כְּלוֹמַר וְצָרִיךְ שֶׁיֵּרָאוּ בָּהּ נַמִּי סִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית. שֶׁאִם לֹא נִרְאוּ בָּהּ סִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁהִיא בַּת עֶשְׂרִים וְלֹא הֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, עֲדַיִן קְטַנָּה הִיא, עַד שֶׁתִּהְיֶה בַּת שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד. וְאִם לֹא הֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת אָז, אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִרְאוּ בָּהּ סִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית, הֲרֵי זוֹ אַיְלוֹנִית. וְסִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית פֵּרַשְׁנוּ [רֵישׁ] פֶּרֶק קַמָּא דִּיבָמוֹת:


והיא אילונית – meaning to say, and it is necessary that they will see I her signs of being a sexually undeveloped woman. For if signs of being a sexually undeveloped woman are not seen in her even though she is twenty-years of age and has not brought forth yet two [pubic] hairs she is still a minor, until she will be thirty-five years and one day old. But if she did not bring forth two [pubic] hairs, then, even though the signs of being an undeveloped woman have not appeared in her, this [woman] is a sexually undeveloped woman. And we explained the signs of a sexually undeveloped woman [at the beginning] of the first chapter of [Tractate] Yevamot (Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:2)


**לֹא חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** וּמֻתֶּרֶת לִנָּשֵׂא לַשּׁוּק. דְּרַחֲמָנָא פַּטְרַהּ, דִּכְתִיב (דברים כה) וְהָיָה הַבְּכוֹר אֲשֶׁר תֵּלֵד, פְּרָט לְאַיְלוֹנִית, שֶׁאֵינָהּ יוֹלֶדֶת:


לא חולצת ולא מתיבמת – and she is permitted to marry to any in the market/street, for the All-Merciful freed her, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:6): “The first son that she bears [shall be accounted to the dead brother, that his name not be blotted out in Israel],” excluding the sexually undeveloped woman, who does not give birth.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:3)


**וְהוּא סָרִיס.** כְּלוֹמַר שֶׁנִּרְאוּ בּוֹ סִימָנֵי סָרִיס. דְּאִי לֹא נִרְאוּ בּוֹ סִימָנֵי סָרִיס, עֲדַיִן הוּא קָטָן עַד שֶׁיִּהְיֶה בֶּן שְׁלֹשִׁים וְחָמֵשׁ שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד, דְּאָז אֲפִלּוּ לֹא נִרְאוּ בּוֹ סִימָנֵי סָרִיס, בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁהוּא סָרִיס, כֵּיוָן שֶׁלֹּא הֵבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת. וְסָרִיס לָאו בַּר חֲלִיצָה [וְיִבּוּם] הוּא, דִּכְתִיב (שם) לְהָקִים לְאָחִיו שֵׁם בְּיִשְׂרָאֵל, וְהַאי לָאו בַּר הָכִי הוּא:


והוא סריס – meaning to say, that there appeared in him the sins of being a eunuch/impotent. For if there hadn’t appeared in him the signs of being a eunuch, he is still a minor, until he will be thirty-five years and one day old, for then, even if there didn’t appear in him signs of being impotent, it is known/assumed that he is impotent, since he did not bring forth two [pubic] hairs. But a eunuch does not perform Halitzah, as it is written (Deuteronomy 25:7): “to establish a name in Israel for his brother,” for this one is not the offspring of this one.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:4)


**רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר אוֹמֵר הַזָּכָר כְּדִבְרֵי בֵית הִלֵּל.** בֶּן עֶשְׂרִים, אִם נִרְאוּ בּוֹ סִימָנֵי סָרִיס, הֲרֵי זֶה סָרִיס:


רבי אליעזר אומר הזכר כדברי בית הלל – age twenty, if there appeared in him the signs of a eunuch, behold this is a eunuch/someone impotent.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 5:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/5:9:5)


**וְהַנְּקֵבָה.** בַּת שְׁמֹנֶה עֶשְׂרֵה, אִם נִרְאוּ בָּהּ סִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית, הֲרֵי זוֹ אַיְלוֹנִית כְּדִבְרֵי בֵּית שַׁמַּאי. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


והנקבה – who is eighteen years old, if there appeared in her signs of a sexually impotent woman, according to the words of the School of Shammai. But the Halakha is not according to the Rabbi Eliezer.



## Chapter 6


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:1)


**בָּא סִימָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שֶׁהוּא סִימָן נַעֲרוּת:


בא סימן התחתון - two [pubic] hairs which is the sign of being a young woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:2)


**עַד שֶׁלֹּא בָא הָעֶלְיוֹן.** סִימָן שֶׁל דַּדִּין, וְהוּא בֹּחַל:


עד שלא בא העליון – the sign of breasts, and this is בוחל/the [intermediate] stage of female puberty between childhood and full womanhood.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:3)


**אוֹ חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** מִשּׁוּם דִּגְדוֹלָה הִיא. שֶׁהַשְּׂעָרוֹת סִימָן מֻבְהָק, וַעֲלַיְהוּ סָמְכִינַן:


או חולצת או מתיבמת – because she is an adult. That the [pubic] hairs are a prominent/distinguished sign, and we rely upon them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:4)


**אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאִי אֶפְשָׁר.** לְרַבָּנָן לָבֹא סִימָן הָעֶלְיוֹן בְּלֹא הַתַּחְתּוֹן. וּמִכִּי אֲתָא סִימָן הָעֶלְיוֹן, אַף עַל גַּב דְּבָדְקוּ וְלֹא אַשְׁכְּחוּ תַּחְתּוֹן, מֵימַר אָמְרִינַן סִימָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן וַדַּאי אֲתָא אֶלָּא שֶׁנָּשַׁר, מִכָּל מָקוֹם לְרַבִּי מֵאִיר לֹא חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת. דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר סְבִירָא לֵיהּ אֶפְשָׁר לָבֹא סִימָן עֶלְיוֹן בְּלֹא תַּחְתּוֹן, וַאֲנַן אַתַּחְתּוֹן סָמְכִינַן, הִלְכָּךְ עֲדַיִן קְטַנָּה הִיא וְלֹא חוֹלֶצֶת וְלֹא מִתְיַבֶּמֶת. וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר לְטַעֲמֵיהּ דְּאָמַר קָטָן וּקְטַנָּה לֹא חוֹלְצִין וְלֹא מִתְיַבְּמִין. קָטָן, שֶׁמָּא יִמָּצֵא סָרִיס. וּקְטַנָּה, שֶׁמָּא תִּמָּצֵא אַיְלוֹנִית:


אע"פ שאי אפשר – [even though it is impossible] for the Rabbis for the upper sign (i.e., breasts) to come without the lower one (i.e., the two pubic hairs), but from when the upper sign comes, even though they examined and did not find the lower one, we state the word that the lower sign definitely came but that it fell off, nevertheless, according to Rabbi Meir, she does not perform Halitzah nor does she engage in levirate marriage. For Rabbi Meir holds that it is possible that the upper sign comes without the lower one, but we rely upon the lower one, therefore she is still a minor and she doesn’t perform Halitzah nor does she engage in levirate marriage. But Rabbi Meir according to his rationale who stated that a male child and a female child do not perform Halitzah neither do they engage in levirate marriage. A male child, perhaps he will be found to be a eunuch, and a female child, perhaps she will be found to be a sexually impotent woman.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:5)


**אוֹ חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** וַאֲפִלּוּ הִיא קְטַנָּה מִתְיַבֶּמֶת לְרַבָּנָן, דְּלֹא חָיְשֵׁי לְמִעוּטָא שֶׁמָּא תִּמָּצֵא אַיְלוֹנִית, דְּרֹב נָשִׁים אֵינָן אַיְלוֹנִיּוֹת. מִיהוּ לַחֲלִיצָה בָּעִינַן שֶׁתְּהֵא גְּדוֹלָה אֲפִלּוּ לְרַבָּנָן, דְּאִישׁ כְּתִיב בַּפָּרָשָׁה [דְּבָרִים כה], וּמַקְּשִׁינַן אִשָּׁה לְאִישׁ. וַהֲלָכָה כַּחֲכָמִים:


או חולצת או מתיבמת – and even if she is a minor, she engages in levirate marriage according to the Rabbis, for they do not concern themselves to the limitation/narrowing qualification that perhaps she will be found to be a sexually impotent woman, for most women are not sexually impotent. However, regarding Halitzah (i.e., the removal of the shoe of her dead husband’s brother who refuses to engage in levirate marriage and spitting in his face), we require that she is an adult, even according to the Rabbis, because it is written (Deuteronomy 25;7), “But if the man/איש does not want to marry his brother’s widow,” in the portion, and we make an analogy of [the situation of the] woman to that of the man. And the Halakha is according to the Sages.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:1:6)


**מִפְּנֵי שֶׁאָמְרוּ אֶפְשָׁר לַתַּחְתּוֹן לָבֹא וְכוּ'.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּתָנָא לֵיהּ בְּרֵישָׁא הָדַר תַּנְיָא זִמְנָא אַחֲרִיתִי, מִשּׁוּם דְּבָעֵי לְמִסְמַךְ לֵיהּ כַּיּוֹצֵא בּוֹ וְכוּ'‏ דְּדָמֵי לֵיהּ:
