מפני שאמרו אפשר לתחתון לבוא וכו' – even though it (i.e., the Mishnah) teaches it in the first clause, it returns and teaches it another time, because it needs to give support to [Mishnah 2 where it states], “similarly, etc.” which is akin to it.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:1)


**כָּל כְּלִי חֶרֶס שֶׁהוּא מַכְנִיס.** מַשְׁקֶה. כְּשֶׁמַּנִּיחוֹ עַל הַמַּיִם, יֵשׁ בּוֹ נֶקֶב גָּדוֹל שֶׁהַמַּיִם נִכְנָסִים לְתוֹכוֹ, כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁאִם נוֹתֵן הַמַּיִם לְתוֹכוֹ יוֹצֵא הַמַּשְׁקֶה לַחוּץ. אֲבָל יֵשׁ נֶקֶב קָטָן שֶׁמּוֹצִיא וְאֵינוֹ מַכְנִיס. וְנַפְקָא מִנַּהּ לְהָא דְּקַיְמָא לָן דִּכְלִי חֶרֶס שָׁלֵם שֶׁנִּקַּב, אֵינוֹ פָּסוּל לְקַדֵּשׁ בּוֹ מֵי חַטָּאת, דְּהַיְנוּ לָתֵת בְּתוֹכוֹ מַיִם שֶׁל אֵפֶר פָּרָה, עַד שֶׁיִּנָּקֵב נֶקֶב גָּדוֹל בְּכוֹנֵס מַשְׁקֶה. אֲבָל נִקַּב נֶקֶב קָטָן כְּמוֹצִיא מַשְׁקֶה, לֹא נָפֵיק מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי וְלֹא מִפְּסִיל בְּהָכִי. וּלְעוֹלָם אֵין נֶקֶב קָטָן בְּמוֹצִיא מַשְׁקֶה מוֹצִיא מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי אֶלָּא הַגִּסְטְרָא בִּלְבַד, דְּהַיְנוּ שֶׁבֶר כְּלִי שֶׁיִּחֲדוֹ לְמַלְּאוֹת בּוֹ מַיִם וּלְהִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ, אִם נִקַּב בְּמוֹצִיא מַשְׁקֶה, בָּטַל מִתּוֹרַת כְּלִי, שֶׁאֵין אוֹמְרִים הָבֵא גִסְטְרָא אַחֶרֶת וְנָשִׂים תַּחַת גִּסְטְרָא זוֹ לְקַבֵּל הַמַּשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא. אֲבָל כְּלִי שָׁלֵם שֶׁנִּקַּב בְּמוֹצִיא מַשְׁקֶה, לֹא בָּטַל מִכְּלוּם, לְפִי שֶׁאָדָם חָס עָלָיו וְאֵינוֹ שׁוֹבְרוֹ, וּמִשְׁתַּמֵּשׁ בּוֹ, וּמֵבִיא שֶׁבֶר כְּלִי וּמַנִּיחַ תַּחְתָּיו לְקַבֵּל הַמַּשְׁקֶה הַיּוֹצֵא מִמֶּנּוּ. וּמֵעַתָּה הָכִי פֵּרוּשָׁא דְּמַתְנִיתִין, כָּל כְּלִי חֶרֶס הַפָּסוּל לְמֵי חַטָּאת פָּסוּל לְגִסְטְרָא, וְיֵשׁ פָּסוּל לְגִסְטְרָא וְכָשֵׁר לְמֵי חַטָּאת:


כל כלי חרס שהוא מכניס – liquid, when he places it on the water there is in a large incision that the water enters into it, all the more so that if he places the water into it, the liquid departs outside. But there is a small incision that lets it out but does not let it in. And we derive from this that which we hold – that a completely earthenware utensil that was perforated it is not invalid to sanctify in it the waters of the sin-offering [of the Red Heifer], that is, to place within it the water of the ashes of the Red Heifer, until he makes a large incision where the liquid goes in. But if he made a small incision where the liquid goes out, it does leave the category of a utensil and not find fault in this. But always a small incision is not like removing liquid that removes it from the category of being a utensil other than a large vessel which turned out to be defective/unwieldy alone (as for example, its handles being broken off or being cracked, and therefore used as a receptacle for refuse, as a pickling pot or as a receiver of drippings from a leaking vessel), that is broken vessel that is was designated to fill up with water and to use it, if it has a perforation/hold through letting liquid out, it is invalidated from the category of a utensil, for we don’t say, bring another large defective vessel and we will place it underneath this defective vessel to receive the liquid that departs. But a complete vessel that has been perforated while removing liquid is not invalidated from anything, because a person is concerned about it and doesn’t break it, and uses it and he brings the shard of a vessel and places it underneath to receive the liquid that comes out from it. And from here this is the explanation of our Mishnah: All earthenware utensils that are invalidated for the waters of the sin-offering [of the Red Heifer] is invalid for a large defective vessel, but there are those which are invalid for a large defective vessel but are fit/kosher for the waters of the sin-offering.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:2)


**כָּל אֵבָר שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ צִפֹּרֶן.** כְּגוֹן אֶצְבַּע יְתֵרָה שֶׁבְּאֶצְבְּעוֹת הַיָּד, אִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ צִפֹּרֶן, חֲשׁוּבָה אֵבָר, וּמְטַמֵּא בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא וּבְאֹהֶל, כְּאֵבָר שֶׁיֵּשׁ בּוֹ עֶצֶם שֶׁמְּטַמֵּא בְּאֹהֶל וַאֲפִלּוּ אִי לֵיכָּא כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר, דְּקַיְמָא לָן הָאֵבָרִים אֵין לָהֶם שִׁעוּר:


כל אבר שיש בו צפורן – as for example, an extra claw/finger that are on the fingers of the hand, if it has a fingernail/talon, it is considered a limb. And it defiles through contact and by carrying and in a tent, like a limb that has a bone that defiles in a tent, and even if it lacks an olive-bulk of flesh, for we hold that limbs have no [distinct] measurement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:2:3)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁיֶּשׁ בּוֹ עֶצֶם.** וְלֹא חֲשׁוּבָה אֵבָר אִם יוֹתֶרֶת הִיא, כֵּיוָן שֶׁאֵין בּוֹ צִפֹּרֶן, וּמְטַמְּאָה בְּמַגָּע וּבְמַשָּׂא, וְאֵינָהּ מְטַמְּאָה בְּאֹהֶל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁאֵין בָּהּ כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר. וְאִי לָאו יוֹתֵרֶת הִיא, וַדַּאי מְטַמְּאָה בְּאֹהֶל מִשּׁוּם אֵבָר, אַף עַל גַּב דְּאֵין בָּהּ צִפֹּרֶן וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּלֵיכָּא כַּזַּיִת בָּשָׂר:


ויש שיש בו עצם – but it is not considered a limb if it is extra, since it doesn’t have a nail/talon, and it defiles through contact and carrying, but does not defile in the tent all the while that there isn’t on it an olive’s-bulk of flesh. But if it is not extra, it definitely defiles in a tent because of being a limb, even though it doesn’t have a nail/talon and even though there isn’t an olive’s-bulk of flesh (see also Talmud Niddah 49b).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:3:1)


**כֹּל הַמִּטַּמֵּא בְּמִדְרָס.** הַזָּב, כָּל כְּלִי הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת אַב הַטֻּמְאָה בְּמִדְרַס הַזָּב, כְּגוֹן כְּלִי הַמְיֻחָד לְמִשְׁכָּב וּמוֹשָׁב, רָאוּי לִהְיוֹת אַב הַטֻּמְאָה אִם נָגַע בְּמֵת אוֹ נִטְמָא בְּאֹהֶל הַמֵּת. וְיֵשׁ שֶׁהוּא כְּלִי גָּמוּר לְקַבֵּל כָּל טֻמְאוֹת וְנַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה עַל יְדֵי מֵת, וְאֵינוֹ נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה עַל יְדֵי הַזָּב בְּמִדְרָס, כְּגוֹן אִם כָּפָה הַזָּב סְאָה וְיָשַׁב עָלֶיהָ, אוֹ תַּרְקַב וְיָשַׁב עָלֶיהָ, אֵין אוֹתוֹ כְּלִי טָמֵא מִדְרָס לִהְיוֹת נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה, אֶלָּא רִאשׁוֹן לַטֻּמְאָה בְּמַגָּעוֹ שֶׁל זָב, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) אֲשֶׁר יֵשֵׁב עָלָיו הַזָּב, מִי שֶׁמְּיֻחָד לִישִׁיבָה, יָצָא זֶה שֶׁאוֹמְרִים לוֹ עֲמֹד וְנַעֲשֶׂה מְלַאכְתֵּנוּ. אֲבָל אִם נָגַע בְּמֵת, נַעֲשֶׂה אַב הַטֻּמְאָה, שֶׁאֵין אוֹמְרִים בִּטְמֵא מֵת עֲמֹד וְנַעֲשֶׂה מְלַאכְתֵּנוּ:


כל המיטמא במדרס – the person with gonorrhea. All utensils that is worthy of becoming a primary source of ritual impurity through the Levitical uncleanness arising from a person with gonorrhea’s immediate contact by treading on/leaning against something, as for example a utensil that is designated for lying and/or sitting if a person came in contact with the dead or was defiled in the tent of a corpse. And there is a utensil that completely becomes susceptible to receive all defilements and becomes a primary source of ritual impurity through [contact with] the dead corpse but does not become a primary source of ritual impurity through the person with gonorrhea through lying and/or sitting, as for example, if the person with gonorrhea bent over/turned upside down a Seah and sat upon it, or a field requiring a Tarkav (i.e., a dry-measure, three Kabs) and sat upon it, that very utensil is not impure through treading to become a primary source of ritual impurity, but rather first-degree ritual impurity through its contact with a person with gonorrhea, as it is written (Leviticus 15:4): “[Whoever sits on an object] on which the one with a discharge has sat [shall wash his clothes, bathe in water], and remain impure [until evening],”he who is designated for sitting, excluding this one that they say to him: “Stand and let us our work.” But if he came in contact with a dead corpse, he becomes a primary source of ritual impurity, for we don’t say with someone who is defiled through contact with the dead, “Stand and let us do our work.”



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:1)


**כֹּל הָרָאוּי לָדוּן דִּינֵי נְפָשׁוֹת.** כָּל שֶׁכֵּן שֶׁרָאוּי לָדוּן דִּינֵי מָמוֹנוֹת:


כל הראוי לדון דיני נפשות – all the more so is appropriate/fit to judge monetary matters.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:2)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁרָאוּי לָדוּן דִּינֵי מָמוֹנוֹת.** כְּגוֹן מַמְזֵר, דְּכָשֵׁר לְדִינֵי מָמוֹנוֹת, וּפָסוּל לְדִינֵי נְפָשׁוֹת:


ויש שראוי לדון דימי ממונות – as for example, a Mamzer, who is fit/kosher to judge monetary matters but is invalid to judge capital crimes (see also, Tractate Sanhedrin, Chapter 4, Mishnah 2 and the Bartenura commentary on the concluding clause of that Mishnah).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:4:3)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁכָּשֵׁר לְהָעִיד וְאֵינוֹ כָשֵׁר לָדוּן.** כְּגוֹן סוּמָא בְּאַחַת מֵעֵינָיו. וּמַתְנִיתִין רַבִּי מֵאִיר הִיא דְּדָרֵישׁ קְרָא דִּכְתִיב (דברים כא) וְעַל פִּיהֶם יִהְיֶה כָּל רִיב וְכָל נָגַע, מֵקִישׁ רִיב לְנֶגַע. מַה נֶּגַע שֶׁלֹּא בְּסוּמָא דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יג) לְכָל מַרְאֵה עֵינֵי הַכֹּהֵן, אַף רִיב שֶׁלֹּא בְּסוּמָא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּהַאי סְתָמָא, דַּחֲכָמִים פְּלִיגֵי עֲלֵיהּ דְּרַבִּי מֵאִיר וְאָמְרֵי דְּכִי הֵיכִי דְּאַשְׁכְּחַן דִּגְמַר דִּין כָּשֵׁר בַּלַּיְלָה אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵין רוֹאִים נְגָעִים בַּלַּיְלָה, הָכִי נַמִּי סוּמָא בְּאַחַת מֵעֵינָיו כָּשֵׁר לָדוּן אַף עַל פִּי שֶׁאֵינוֹ כָּשֵׁר לִרְאוֹת נְגָעִים:


ויש שכשר להעיד ואינו כשר לדון – as for example, a person who is blind in one of his eyes. But our - Mishnah is according to Rabbi Meir who expounds on Scripture, as it is written (Deuteronomy 21:5): “and every lawsuit and case of assault is subject to their ruling/ועל-פיהם יהיה כל-ריב וכל-נגע. He makes an analogy between ריב/lawsuit and נגע/case of assault – that just as a case of assault is not with a blind person, as it is written (Leviticus 13:12): “whatever the priest can see/לכל-מראה א=עיני הכהן,” even a lawsuit is not with a blind person. But the Halakha is not like this anonymously taught [Mishnah], for the Sages dispute on that of Rabbi Meir and state that just as we find that the closing of legal proceedings is at night, even though we can’t see נגעים/people afflicted by plagues/suspected leprosy at night, that is so that a blind person in one of his eyes is fit to judge even though he is not kosher/fit to see plagues/suspected leprosy.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:5:1)


**כֹּל שֶׁחַיָּב בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת מִטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים.** דְּאֵין לְךָ דָּבָר חַיָּב בְּמַעֲשֵׂר שֶׁאֵינוֹ אֹכֶל:


כל שחייב במעשרות מיטמא טומאת אוכלים – for there isn’t a thing that is obligated in tithing that isn’t food (see also Tractate Maaserot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:5:2)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁמִּטַּמֵּא טֻמְאַת אֳכָלִים וְאֵינוֹ חַיָּב בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** כְּגוֹן בָּשָׂר וְדָגִים וּבֵיצִים, שֶׁאֵינָן גִּדּוּלֵי קַרְקַע:


ויש שמיטמא טומאת אוכלים ואינו חייב במעשרות – as for example meat, and fish and eggs, which do not grow in the ground (note: but it should be pointed out that cattle are tithed – see Tractate Rosh Hashanah, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1).



### Mishnah 6

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:6:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:6:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:6:1)


**כֹּל שֶׁחַיָּב בַּפֵּאָה חַיָּב בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת.** דְּגַבֵּי פֵּאָה תְּנַן, כְּלָל אָמְרוּ בְּפֵאָה, כָּל שֶׁהוּא אֹכֶל וְנִשְׁמָר וְגִדּוּלוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ וּמַכְנִיסוֹ לְקִיּוּם וּלְקִיטָתוֹ כְּאַחַת, חַיָּב בְּפֵאָה. וְאִלּוּ גַּבֵּי מַעֲשֵׂר תְּנַן, כָּל שֶׁהוּא אֹכֶל וְנִשְׁמָר וְגִדּוּלוֹ מִן הָאָרֶץ חַיָּב בְּמַעֲשֵׂר. וּמַכְנִיסוֹ לְקִיּוּם וּלְקִיטָתוֹ כְּאַחַת לֹא קָתָנֵי. נִמְצָא יָרָק שֶׁאֵין מַכְנִיסוֹ לְקִיּוּם, וּתְאֵנָה וְכַיּוֹצֵא בָּהֶם שֶׁאֵין לְקִיטָתָם כְּאַחַת, חַיָּבִין בַּמַּעַשְׂרוֹת וּפְטוּרִים מִן הַפֵּאָה:


כל שחייב בפאה חייב במעשרות – for concerning the corner of the field/פאה, they (i.e., the Rabbis) stated a general principle in [Tractate] Peah (Chapter 1, Mishnah 4): “All that is edible, privately owned, grown in the ground, harvested as a crop all at once (not singly as they become ripe) and can be preserved in storage is subject to [designation as] Peah.” Whereas regarding tithes/מעשר it is taught in the Mishnah [Tractate Maaserot, Chapter 1, Mishnah 1]: “All that is food, cultivated and which grows from the earth is subject to [the law of] tithes.” But, “preserved in storage and harvested as a crop all at once” is not taught. It is found that a vegetable is not preserved in storage and a fig and things similar to them are not harvested as a crop all at once, are subject to the laws of tithes but they are exempt from Peah.



### Mishnah 7

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:7:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:1)


**כֹּל שֶׁחַיָּב בְּרֵאשִׁית הַגֵּז.** רְחֵלִים וְאֵילִים בִּלְבַד:


כל שחייב בראשית הגז – ewes/sheep and rams alone.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:7:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:2)


**חַיָּב בַּמַּתָּנוֹת.** הַזְּרוֹעַ וְהַלְּחָיַיִם וְהַקֵּבָה:


חייב מתנות – the shoulder, the cheeks and the stomach (see Deuteronomy 18:3).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:7:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:7:3)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁחַיָּב בַּמַּתָּנוֹת.** שֶׁחִיּוּב הַמַּתָּנוֹת לְשׁוֹר וְעִזִּים וּכְבָשִׂים:


ויש שחייב במתנות – that the obligation of [priestly] gifts is for bulls and goats and sheep (at least one year old).



### Mishnah 8

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:8:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:8:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:8:1)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ שְׁבִיעִית וְאֵין לוֹ בִעוּר.** כְּגוֹן הַדְּבָרִים שֶׁשָּׁרְשָׁם נִשְׁאָר בַּקַּרְקַע בֵּין בִּימוֹת הַחַמָּה בֵּין בִּימוֹת הַגְּשָׁמִים, כְּמוֹ הַלּוּף שׁוֹטֶה וְהַדַּנְדַּנָּה, שֶׁקּוֹרִים בַּעֲרָבִי נַעְנַ"ע, וּבְלַעַ"ז מֵינְטַ"א, דְּכֵיוָן שֶׁאֵין כָּלִין לְעוֹלָם מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, אֵין חַיָּב לְבַעֲרָם מִן הַבַּיִת, דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא כה) וְלִבְהֶמְתְּךָ וְלַחַיָּה אֲשֶׁר בְּאַרְצֶךָ, כָּל זְמַן שֶׁחַיָּה אוֹכֶלֶת בַּשָּׂדֶה, הַאֲכֵל לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת, כָּלָה לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה כַּלֵּה לִבְהֶמְתְּךָ מִן הַבַּיִת, וְכָל הָנָךְ דְּלֹא כָּלוּ לַחַיָּה מִן הַשָּׂדֶה, אֵין צָרִיךְ לְבַעֲרָם מִן הַבַּיִת:


ויש שיש לו שביעית ואין לו ביעור – as for example the things whose roots remain in the ground whether during the months of heat/summer or during the rainy season, like the LOF (i.e., a plant similar to Colocasia, with edible leaves and root, and bearing beans – it is classified with onions and garlic, leaves of the wild LOF and the mint (see Tractate Niddah 51b), which they call NANA in Arabic, and MINTA in the foreign tongue, for since they do not ever cease from the field, there is no obligation to remove them from the house, as it is written (Leviticus 25:7): “and our cattle and the beasts in your land [may eat all its yield],” that all the time that beasts eat in the field, your cattle eats from the house, if the beasts cease from the field, the cattle cease from the house. And all of these that did not cease for the beasts from the field, there is no need to remove them from the house.



### Mishnah 9

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:9:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:1)


**קַשְׂקֶשֶׂת.** מַלְבּוּשׁ שֶׁל דָּג, הַקָּבוּעַ בּוֹ:


קשקשת – the clothing of the fish that is attached to it (i.e., scales).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:2)


**סְנַפִּיר.** כְּנָפַיִם שֶׁפּוֹרֵחַ בָּהֶן. וְכָל שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ קַשְׂקֶשֶׂת בְּיָדוּעַ שֶׁיֵּשׁ לוֹ סְנַפִּיר וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לִבְדֹּק אַחֲרָיו עוֹד, שֶׁדָּג טָהוֹר הוּא:


סנפיר- wings that it flies with them, and whatever has scales, it is known that it has fins and there is no need to examine after it further, for it is a pure fish.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:3)


**וְיֵשׁ שֶׁיֶּשׁ לוֹ סְנַפִּיר.** וְהוּא דָּג טָמֵא, שֶׁאֵין לוֹ קַשְׂקֶשֶׂת:


ויש שיש לו סנפיר – but it is an impure fish, for it lacks scales.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:4)


**יֶשׁ לוֹ טְלָפַיִם.** פַּרְסוֹתָיו סְדוּקוֹת. שֶׁאֵין קַרְנַיִם אֶלָּא לִבְהֵמָה וּלְחַיָּה טְהוֹרָה:


יש לו טלפים - its hoofs are cloven, for there are no horns other than for pure cattle and beasts.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:9:5)


**וְאֵין לוֹ קַרְנָיִם.** כְּגוֹן חֲזִיר, שֶׁפַּרְסוֹתָיו סְדוּקוֹת, וְאֵין לוֹ קַרְנַיִם:


ואין לו קרנים – as for example, swine, whose feet are cloven but they don’t have horns.



### Mishnah 10

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:10:1)


**וְיֵשׁ טָעוּן בְּרָכָה לְפָנָיו וְאֵין טָעוּן בְּרָכָה לְאַחֲרָיו.** כְּגוֹן צִיצִית, וּתְפִלִּין, וּמְזוּזָה, שׁוֹפָר, סֻכָּה, וְלוּלָב, שֶׁמְּבָרְכִין עֲלֵיהֶן עוֹבֵר לַעֲשִׂיָּתָן, וְאֵין טְעוּנִין בְּרָכָה לְאַחֲרֵיהֶן:


ויש שטעון ברכה לפניו ואין טעון ברכה לאחריו – such as ritual fringes/Tzizit, and Tefillin/phylacteries and Mezuzah, Shofar, Sukkah and Lulav, that we recite a blessing upon them before doing/performing them, but they don’t require a blessing afterwards.



### Mishnah 11

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:1)


**תִּינוֹקֶת שֶׁהֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת.** לְאַחַר שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד:


תינוקת שהביאה שתי שערות – after [reaching] twelve years of age and one day.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:2)


**וְחוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת.** אַף עַל גַּב דְּתָנָא חַיֶּבֶת בְּכָל מִצְוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בַּתּוֹרָה, אִצְטְרִיךְ לְמִתְנֵי חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת, דְּמַהוּ דְּתֵימָא אִישׁ כְּתִיב בַּפָּרָשָׁה (דברים כה), וְאִם לֹא יַחְפֹּץ הָאִישׁ, הִלְכָּךְ קָטָן לֹא חָלֵיץ. אֲבָל אִשָּׁה דְּלֹא כְּתִיב בַּפָּרָשָׁה אֶלָּא יְבִמְתּוֹ, בֵּין גְּדוֹלָה בֵּין קְטַנָּה חוֹלֶצֶת אוֹ מִתְיַבֶּמֶת, קָא מַשְׁמַע לָן דְּאִי אַיְיתִּיאַת שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת, אִין. אִי לֹא, לֹא. מַאי טַעֲמָא, דְּמַקְשִׁינַן אִשָּׁה לְאִישׁ:


וחולצת או מתיבמת – even though that it teaches [in the Mishnah] that she is obligated in all of the commandments that are stated in the Torah, it was necessary to teach, [either] she performs Halitzah or engages in levirate marriage, for you might have thought that [only] "איש"/a man – is written in the portion (Deuteronomy 25:7): “But if the man/איש does not want [to marry his brother’s widow,” therefore a male who is a minor does not perform Halitzah, but a woman, who is not written in the portion other than as (Deuteronomy 25:7): “his brother’s widow/יבמתו” – whether she is an adult or whether a child, performs Halitzah or engages in levirate marriage. It comes to teach us that if she produces/brings forth two [pubic] hairs, it is so, but if not, it is not so. What is the reason? That we make an analogy between a woman to a man.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:3)


**וְכֵן הַתִּינוֹק שֶׁהֵבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת.** לְאַחַר שְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד. וְקַמֵּי שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה לִנְקֵבָה וּשְׁלֹשׁ עֶשְׂרֵה לְזָכָר, אֵינוֹ סִימָן אֶלָּא שׁוּמָא:


וכן תינוק שהביא שתי שערות – after thirteen years and one day. And we establish twelve years for a female and thirteen for a male, is not a definitive sign, but rather an estimation/mark.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:4)


**וְרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת בֵּן סוֹרֵר וּמוֹרֶה מִשֶּׁיָּבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת עַד שֶׁיַּקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן.** אֲבָל קֹדֶם שֶׁיָּבִיא שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת לָאו בַּר עוֹנְשִׁים הוּא. וּלְאַחַר שֶׁהִקִּיף זָקָן הַתַּחְתּוֹן, רָאוּי לְהוֹלִיד, וְרַחֲמָנָא אָמַר בֵּן, וְלֹא הָרָאוּי לִהְיוֹת אָב:


וראוי להיות בן סורר ומורה משיביא שתי שערות עד שיקיף זקן התחתון – but prior to his bringing forth two [pubic] hairs, he is not liable for punishment. But after he has grown an encircling beard, he is worthy of giving birth (i.e., engaging in sexual intercourse for purposes of reproduction) and the All-Merciful stated (Deuteronomy 21:18): “If a man has [a wayward and defiant] son/בן,” but not one worthy of being a father.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:5)


**הַתַּחְתּוֹן וְלֹא הָעֶלְיוֹן.** כְּלוֹמַר, בְּאֵיזוֹ הַקָּפַת זָקָן אָמְרוּ, בְּתַחְתּוֹן שֶׁל מַטָּה, וְלֹא בָּעֶלְיוֹן:


התחתון ולא העליון – meaning to say, which encircling beard did they mention, with the one of the bottom, but not the one of the top (i.e., the beard of the face).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:11:6)


**עַד שֶׁיִּרְבֶּה הַשָּׁחֹר.** שֶׁיַּשְׁחִיר אוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם שְׂעָרוֹת הַרְבֵּה. וּפְסַק הֲלָכָה, שֶׁהַבַּת מְמָאֶנֶת עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא בַּת שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד. וּלְאַחַר זְמַן זֶה, אִם הֵבִיאָה סִימָנִים, אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לְמָאֵן. וְאִם לֹא הֵבִיאָה סִימָנִים, מְמָאֶנֶת וְהוֹלֶכֶת וַאֲפִלּוּ עַד שֶׁתְּהֵא בַּת עֶשְׂרִים שָׁנָה וְיֵרָאוּ בָּהּ סִימָנֵי אַיְלוֹנִית. וְהָנֵי מִילֵי, כְּשֶׁלֹּא בָּא עָלֶיהָ לְאַחַר שֶׁהָיוּ לָהּ שְׁתֵּים עֶשְׂרֵה שָׁנָה וְיוֹם אֶחָד. אֲבָל אִם בָּא עָלֶיהָ לְאַחַר זְמַן זֶה, שׁוּב אֵינָהּ יְכוֹלָה לְמָאֵן. וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא נִרְאוּ בָּהּ סִימָנִים, חָיְשִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא הֵבִיאָה שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת וְנָשְׁרוּ:


עד שירבה השחור (until the dark [hair] predominates) – that this place becomes darkened from man hairs. And the legal decision is, that the daughter may [express] refusal until she is twelve years of age and one day. But after this time, if she brought forth signs [of her young womanhood], she is not able to refuse. But if she did not bring forth signs, she can continue to refuse and even until she is twenty-years of age and she will demonstrate signs in her of being a sexually undeveloped woman. But these words [apply] when he did not come upon her [sexually] when she was twelve-years and one-day old, but if he came upon her after this time, she is not able to refuse any longer. But even though signs of [her womanhood] had not appeared in her, we are concerned that perhaps she brought forth the two [pubic] hairs and they fell off.



### Mishnah 12

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:1)


**שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת הָאֲמוּרוֹת בַּפָּרָה.** דְּקַיְמָא לָן (פרה פ"ב) שְׁתֵּי שְׂעָרוֹת שְׁחֹרוֹת פּוֹסְלוֹת בָּהּ:


שתי שערות האמורות בפרה – for we hold (Tractate Parah, Chapter 2, Mishnah 5) that two black hairs invalidate her (i.e., the Red Heifer).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:2)


**וּבַנְּגָעִים.** שֵׂעָר לָבָן, קַיְמָא לָן בְּתוֹרַת כֹּהֲנִים מִעוּט שֵׂעָר שְׁתַּיִם:


ובנגעים – a white hair, for we hold in the Torah of the Priests (The Midrash Halakha of Sifra) (see also Tractate Negaim, Chapter 4, Mishnah 4) that the minimum limitation [of the word] שער/hair is [at least] two.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:3)


**וְהָאֲמוּרוֹת בְּכָל מָקוֹם.** בְּתִינוֹק וּבְתִינוֹקֶת:


האמורות בכל מקום – regarding a young male child and a young female child.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:4)


**לִקְרֹץ.** לֶאֱחֹז מְעַט:


לקרוץ (to cut, make an incision) - to hold a bit.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:12:5)


**שֶׁיְּהוּ נִטָּלוֹת בְּזוּג.** שֶׁל מִסְפָּרַיִם, וְהָוֵי שִׁעוּרָא רַבָּא. וַהֲלָכָה כְּדִבְרֵי כֻּלָּן לְהַחְמִיר, וּמִשֶּׁנִּקְרֶצֶת בַּצִּפֹּרֶן דַּהֲוֵי שִׁעוּרָא זוּטָא מִכֻּלְּהוּ, לֹא מְמָאֶנֶת שֶׁמָּא גְּדוֹלָה הִיא. וּלְעִנְיַן חֲלִיצָה, אֵינָהּ חוֹלֶצֶת, עַד דְּאִיכָּא שִׁעוּרָא רַבָּא דְּכֻלְּהוּ:


שיהו ניטלות בזוג – of scissors, this is the greater measure. And the Halakha is according to all of them for stringency, and when it is cut with a fingernail, it is the smallest measure of all of them, but she does not complain, lest she is an adult woman, but regarding Halitzah, she does not engage in it, until there is the greatest measure of all of them (see Talmud Niddah 52b).



### Mishnah 13

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:1)


**הָרוֹאָה כֶתֶם.** שֶׁמָּצְאָה טִפַּת דָּם בַּחֲלוּקָהּ:


הרואה כתם (she sees a dark-red stain [on her clothing or body as an indication of uncleanness]) – that she found a drop of blood on her garment.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:2)


**הֲרֵי זוֹ מְקֻלְקֶלֶת.** אֵינָהּ יוֹדַעַת פֶּתַח נִדּוֹתֶיהָ לֵידַע מָתַי יַתְחִילוּ אַחַד עָשָׂר יוֹם שֶׁבֵּין נִדָּה לְנִדָּה, דְּהָא לֹא יָדְעָה אֵימָת חֲזַאי:


הרי זו מקולקלת (she is disarrayed) – she doesn’t know an opening to her menstruating to know when will begin the eleven days that are between one Niddah and the next one (the days of protracted menstruation/flux), for she doesn’t know when it appears.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:3)


**וְחוֹשֶׁשֶׁת מִשּׁוּם זוֹב.** אִם עָבְרוּ עָלֶיהָ שְׁלֹשָׁה יְמֵי זִיבָה מִשֶּׁלָּבְשָׁה חָלוּק זֶה, וּמָצְאָה עָלָיו כֶּתֶם גָּדוֹל שִׁעוּר שְׁלֹשָׁה גְּרִיסִין וָעוֹד, כְּלוֹמַר מְעַט יוֹתֵר מִשְּׁלֹשָׁה גְּרִיסִין, מְסַפְּקִינַן לְהוּ דִּלְמָא כָּל חֲדָא וַחֲדָא בְּחַד יוֹמָא, וְהַוְיָא זָבָה גְּדוֹלָה מִסָּפֵק, וְאַף עַל גַּב דִּבְמָקוֹם אֶחָד נִמְצְאוּ:


וחוששת משום זוב -if the three days of [consecutive] blood flow have passed from when she wore this garment, and she found upon it a large blood stain the measure of three pounded beans and more, meaning to say, a bit more than three pounded beans, they were doubtful that perhaps each one of them came on one day, and it would be from doubt a large protracted menstruation/flux, and even though there were found in one place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:13:4)


**וַחֲכָמִים אוֹמְרִים אֵין בַּכְּתָמִים מִשּׁוּם זוֹב.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקֵי מִלְּתַיְהוּ דַּחֲכָמִים דְּהָכָא, כִּיחִידָאָה, וְאָמַר דְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס הִיא. וּמוֹדֶה רַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס שֶׁאִם לָבְשָׁה שָׁלֹשׁ חֲלוּקוֹת לְבָנוֹת בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים בִּימֵי זִיבָה, חָלוּק לְיוֹם, וּמָצְאָה בְּכָל אַחַת מֵהֶן כֶּתֶם, דְּחוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְזִיבוּת. וְאֵין צָרִיךְ לוֹמַר אִם רָאֲתָה שְׁנֵי יָמִים דָּם מִגּוּפָהּ, וּבַשְּׁלִישִׁי לָבְשָׁה חָלוּק הַבָּדוּק לָהּ וּמָצְאָה בּוֹ כֶּתֶם, דִּפְשִׁיטָא דְּחוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְזִיבוּת. וְלֹא נֶחֱלַק רַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס דְּהוּא חֲכָמִים דְּמַתְנִיתִין, אֶלָּא עַל הַלּוֹבֶשֶׁת חָלוּק אֶחָד בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה יָמִים מִימֵי זִיבָתָהּ וּלְבַסּוֹף מָצְאָה כֶּתֶם גָּדוֹל שִׁעוּר שְׁלֹשָׁה גְּרִיסִין וָעוֹד בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד, אוֹ אֲפִלּוּ בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת, דְּאִיהוּ סָבַר בִּכְהַאי גַוְנָא אֵינָהּ חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְזִיבוּת, הוֹאִיל וְלֹא הָיוּ הַכְּתָמִים בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה חֲלוּקוֹת. וְרַבִּי מֵאִיר וְרַבָּנָן דְּאִיפְּלִיגוּ עֲלֵיהּ סָבְרֵי, כְּשֶׁמָּצְאָה שִׁעוּר שְׁלֹשָׁה גְּרִיסִין וָעוֹד בְּחָלוּק אֶחָד, בֵּין בְּמָקוֹם אֶחָד בֵּין בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה מְקוֹמוֹת, חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת לְזִיבוּת. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


וחכמים אומרים אין בכתמים משום זוב (bloodstains are not subject [to the laws of uncleanness] because of Zivah) – In the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 52b) it establishes the matter of the Sages here, as an individual opinion, and stated that it is [the statement] of Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos. But Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos admits that if she wore three dresses for girls on three days of her days of flux/protracted menstruation, a dress per day, and she found on each of them a blood stain, that she worries about flux. But one does not have to say that if she saw two days of blood from her body, but on the third day wore a dress that was examined for her and she found on it a blood stain, that it is obvious that she worries about protracted menstruation/flux. But it is not disputed that Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos is the “Sages” of our Mishnah, other than when she wears one dress for three days from the days of her protracted menstruation/flux and at the end found a large bloodstain the measurement of three pounded beans and more in one place, or even in three [different] places, for he holds that in this case she is not concerned/worried about protracted menstruation, since she did not have blood stains on three dresses. But Rabbi Meir and the Rabbis dispute upon him, holding, that when she found the measurement of three pounded beans and more on one dress, whether in one place or whether in three places, she worries about protracted menstruation/flux. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos.



### Mishnah 14

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:1)


**הָרוֹאָה יוֹם אַחַד עָשָׂר.** שֶׁהוּא סוֹף יְמֵי זִיבָה. וּלְמָחָר מַתְחִילִין יְמֵי נִדָּה:


הרואה יום אחד עשר – which is at the end of the days of protracted menstruation/flux and on the morrow begins the days of Niddah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:2)


**בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת.** סְפֵק יוֹם הוּא וְהָוֵי דַּם זִיבָה, סְפֵק לַיְלָה וּתְחִלַּת נִדָּה:


בין השמשות – it is doubtfully day which is the blood of protracted menstruation/flux, doubtfully the night and the beginning of Niddah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:3)


**תְּחִלַּת נִדָּה וְסוֹף נִדָּה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מְפָרֵשׁ דְּהָכִי קָתָנֵי, תְּחִלַּת נִדָּה וְסוֹף זִיבָה. וּבַשְּׁבִיעִי לְנִדָּתָהּ, סוֹף נִדָּה וּתְחִלַּת זִיבָה. כְּלוֹמַר, בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת שֶׁל אַחַד עָשָׂר, אִם לַיְלָה הוּא, הֲרֵי רְאִיָּתָהּ תְּחִלַּת נִדָּה. וְאִם יוֹם, הֲרֵי הַדָּם דָּם שֶׁל סוֹף זִיבָה. וְאִם רָאֲתָה שְׁנֵי יָמִים קֹדֶם לָכֵן, חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת מִשּׁוּם זִיבָה. וְכֵן אִם רָאֲתָה בִּשְׁבִיעִי לְנִדָּתָהּ בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, מְסַפְּקִינַן נַמִּי, אִם לַיְלָה הוּא, הֲרֵי הַדָּם דָּם שֶׁל תְּחִלַּת זִיבָה, וְאִם תִּרְאֶה שְׁנֵי יָמִים אַחֲרֵי כֵן, חוֹשֶׁשֶׁת מִשּׁוּם זִיבָה. וְאִם יוֹם הוּא, הֲרֵי רְאִיָּתָהּ סוֹף יְמֵי נִדָּה, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁתִּרְאֶה שְׁנֵי יָמִים אַחַר כָּךְ אֵינָהּ זָבָה:


תחלת נדה וסוף נדה – in the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 53b) it explains that this is what it teaches: the beginning of Niddah and the conclusion of the days of protracted menstruation/flux. But on the seventh day of her menstruation/Niddah and the beginning of her protracted menstruation/flux. That is to say, at twilight of the eleven [days], if it is night, it is the appearance of the beginning of Niddah, but if it is day time, the blood is the blood of the end of flux. But if she say it (i.e., blood) two days prior to this, she worries because of flux. And similarly, if she saw it (i.e., blood) on the seventh day of her Niddah at twilight, we are doubtful also, if it is night, for the blood is the blood of the beginning of flux/protracted menstruation, but if she saw it two days afterwards she worries because of flux. But if it is day time, her seeing it [i.e., blood) is the end of the days of Niddah/menstruation , and even though she sees [blood] two days afterwards it is not flux.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:4)


**יוֹם אַרְבָּעִים לַזָּכָר וְיוֹם שְׁמוֹנִים לַנְּקֵבָה בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת לְכֻלָּן.** כְּלוֹמַר בְּאֵיזוֹ מֵאֵלּוּ שֶׁתִּרְאֶה בֵּין הַשְּׁמָשׁוֹת, סָפֵק דָּם טָמֵא סָפֵק דָּם טָהוֹר. וְאִם תִּרְאֶה שְׁנֵי יָמִים אֲחֵרִים חוּץ מִזּוֹ, הֲרֵי הִיא זָבָה מִסָּפֵק, וּמְבִיאָה קָרְבָּן וְאֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל, שֶׁכָּל הַטּוֹעוֹת מְבִיאוֹת קָרְבָּן וְאֵינוֹ נֶאֱכָל:


יום ארבעים לזכר ויום שמונים לנקבה. בין השמשות לכולן – meaning to say on which of these that she should see [blood] at twilight, it is doubtful that it is [ritually] impure blood and doubtful if it is pure/clean blood,, but if she sees [blood] two other days outside of this, it is blood of protracted menstruation/flux from doubt, and she brings a sacrifice which is not consumed, for all who err bring a sacrifice and it is not consumed.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:5)


**הַשּׁוֹטוֹת.** לַטּוֹעוֹת שֶׁאֵינָן יוֹדְעוֹת מָתַי יְמֵי נִדָּה וִימֵי זִיבָה, קָרֵי שׁוֹטוֹת:


השוטות (the foolish) – to the foolish who do not know when are the days of Niddah/menstruation and when are the days of flux, they are fools.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:6)


**עַד שֶׁאַתֶּם מְתַקְּנִים אֶת הַשּׁוֹטוֹת.** אֶת הַטּוֹעוֹת הַלָּלוּ שֶׁרָאוּ בִּשְׁעַת הַסָּפֵק:


עד שאתם מתקנים את השוטות – these foolish women who saw [blood] at the doubtful time period.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 6:14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/6:14:7)


**בֹּאוּ וְתַקְּנוּ אֶת הַפִּקְחוֹת.** שֶׁרוֹאוֹת בְּשָׁעָה וַדָּאִית וְהֵן צְרִיכוֹת תִּקּוּן וּלְפָרֵשׁ יְמֵי שִׁמּוּרָן וְתַשְׁמִישָׁן. כְּגוֹן הָנָךְ דְּמַיְתֵי בַּבְּרַיְתָא, מִי שֶׁרוֹאָה יוֹם אֶחָד טָמֵא וָיוֹם אֶחָד טָהוֹר כָּל יָמֶיהָ, וְתָמִיד הִיא רוֹאָה בַּיּוֹם וְלֹא בַּלַּיְלָה, מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת שְׁמִינִי לְיוֹם שֶׁרָאֲתָה בּוֹ רְאִיָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי טְהוֹרָה הִיא, שֶׁהֲרֵי בְּשִׁבְעָה לָעֶרֶב טָבְלָה וְלֹא תִּרְאֶה עַד תְּשִׁיעִי, הִלְכָּךְ מְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת שְׁמִינִי שָׁלֵם לַיְלָה וְיוֹם, וְלַיְלָה שֶׁלְּאַחֲרָיו שֶׁהוּא נַגְהֵי תְּשִׁיעִי. וּמְשַׁמֶּשֶׁת אַרְבָּעָה לֵילוֹת מִתּוֹךְ שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר יוֹם לִרְאִיָּה רִאשׁוֹנָה, שֶׁהֲרֵי אִשָּׁה זוֹ לֹא תְּהֵא זָבָה לְעוֹלָם, שֶׁאֵינָהּ רוֹאָה שְׁלֹשָׁה רְצוּפִים. וּכְשֶׁהִיא רוֹאָה תְּשִׁיעִי, תְּהֵא שׁוֹמֶרֶת עֲשִׂירִי וּתְשַׁמֵּשׁ בַּלַּיְלָה. וְתִרְאֶה בְּאַחַד עָשָׂר. וְתִשְׁמֹר שְׁנֵים עָשָׂר וּתְשַׁמֵּשׁ בַּלַּיְלָה. הֲרֵי שְׁנַיִם. וְתִרְאֶה בִּשְׁלֹשָׁה עָשָׂר, וְתִשְׁמֹר אַרְבָּעָה עָשָׂר וּתְשַׁמֵּשׁ בַּלַּיְלָה, הֲרֵי שְׁלֹשָׁה. וְתִרְאֶה חֲמִשָּׁה עָשָׂר, וְתִשְׁמֹר שִׁשָּׁה עָשָׂר וּתְשַׁמֵּשׁ בָּעֶרֶב, הֲרֵי אַרְבָּעָה. וּבְשִׁבְעָה עָשָׂר תִּרְאֶה, וְתִשְׁמֹר שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר. הֲרֵי כָּלוּ שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר וְלֹא שִׁמְּשָׁה אֶלָּא אַרְבָּעָה לֵילוֹת. לְבַד שְׁמִינִי וְלֵילוֹ. וְתַשְׁמִישׁ אַחֲרוֹן אֵינוֹ בְּתוֹךְ שְׁמֹנָה עָשָׂר. וּלְמָחָר בְּתִשְׁעָה עָשָׂר כְּשֶׁתִּרְאֶה, תְּהֵא תְּחִלַּת נִדָּה, שֶׁהֲרֵי שָׁלְמוּ אַחַד עָשָׂר שֶׁבֵּין נִדָּה לְנִדָּה, וְחוֹזֶרֶת לְמִנְיָנָהּ שֶׁאָמַרְנוּ. וְהָכִי אִיכָּא טוּבָא שֶׁצְּרִיכוֹת תִּקּוּן לִימֵי תַּשְׁמִישָׁן. וְכֻלְּהוּ מַיְתֵי לַהּ בַּבְּרַיְתָא בַּגְּמָרָא בְּפִרְקִין:


בואו ותקנו את הפקחות = that see [blood] at the certain hour and they need repair and to explain/separate the days of their watchfulness and their [days of] sexual intercourse, as for example these that we brought in the Baraita (Tractate Niddah 54a): She who sees [blood] one day is [ritually] impure and one day pure all of her days, and she always sees [blood] during the day but not at night, she engages in sexual intercourse on the eight day from when she saw [blood] for the first time, for she is pure, for behold that on the seventh [day] in the evening she immersed [in the Mikveh] and she didn’t see [blood] until the ninth day, therefore, she engages in sexual intercourse the complete eighth day night and day and the night afterwards which is the morning of the ninth day. And she engages in sexual intercourse for four nights out of eighteen days of her first sighting [of blood], for behold this woman will not be ever be a woman with flux/protracted menstruation, since she doesn’t see [blood] for three consecutive [days]. But when she sees [blood] on the ninth [day], she would observe the tenth [day in abstinence from sexual intercourse] and engage in sexual intercourse at night. And then she would see [blood] on the eleventh [day], and would observe the twelfth [day in abstinence from sexual intercourse] and then engage in sexual intercourse at night, that is two. And she would see [blood] on the thirteenth [day] and observe the fourteenth [day in abstinence from sexual intercourse] and engage in sexual intercourse at night, that is three [days]. And then she would see [blood] on the fifteenth [day] and she would observe the sixteenth [day in abstinence from sexual intercourse] and engage in sexual intercourse in the evening, that is four [days]. And on the seventeenth [day] she would see [blood], and she would observe the eighteenth [day in abstinence from sexual intercourse], behold the eighteen days have been completed and she engaged in sexual intercourse for only four nights. Except for the eighth day and its night, the last act of sexual intercourse is not within the eighteen [days]. And on the morrow on the nineteenth [day], when she sees [blood], this is the beginning of her Niddah/menstrual cycle, for the eleven days between one menstrual period and the other have been completed, and she returns to her count as we have stated. But there is more that requires amendment for the days of their sexual relations, and all of it is brought in the Baraita (Tractate Niddah 54a) in the Gemara in our chapter.



## Chapter 7


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:1)


**דַּם הַנִּדָּה.** מְטַמְּאִין לַחִין. דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְהַדָּוָה בְּנִדָּתָהּ, מַה שֶּׁזָּב מִמֶּנָּה טָמֵא כְּמוֹתָהּ, וּבְשָׁעָה שֶׁזָּב לַח הוּא:


דם נדה. מטמאין לחין – as it is written (Leviticus 15:33): “And concerning her who is in menstrual infirmity, [and concerning anyone, male or female, who has a discharge, and concerning a man who lies with an impure woman],” just as a male has flux from her is impure like her, and at the time he has a flux, it is moist.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:2)


**וּמְטַמְּאִים יְבֵשִׁים.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) דָּם יִהְיֶה זֹבָהּ, בַּהֲוָיָתוֹ יְהֵא, וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁנִּתְיַבֵּשׁ:


ומטמאין יבשין – as it is written (Leviticus 15:19): “[When a woman has a discharge], her discharge being blood from her body, [she shall remain in menstrual impurity seven days],” he will be in his condition/status , and even though it dried up.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:3)


**וּבְשַׂר הַמֵּת.** מְטַמֵּא יָבֵשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (במדבר יב) אוֹ בְּעֶצֶם אָדָם, אָדָם דּוּמְיָא דְּעֶצֶם, מָה עֶצֶם יָבֵשׁ אַף בָּשָׂר יָבֵשׁ:


ובשר המת – it defiles dry, as it is written (Numbers 19:16 -the printed text errs in mentioning chapter 12): “or human bone,” a person is similar to a bone; just as a bone [defiles] when dry, also the flesh [of a dead person] defiles when dry.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:4)


**וְהַנִּיעַ.** רֹק רַךְ שֶׁיּוֹצֵא עַל יְדֵי נִעְנוּעַ. וַאֲנִי שָׁמַעְתִּי, לֵחָה הַיּוֹרֶדֶת מִן הַחֹטֶם. וּמִמַּעְיְנוֹת הַזָּב הֵן וַהֲווּ אַב הַטֻּמְאָה:


והניע (the phlegm) -soft spittle that comes out through shaking. But I heard, secretion that descends from the nose. But they are from the discharges of the man with a flux/gonorrhea which are the primary sources of [ritual] impurity.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:5)


**וְהַשֶּׁרֶץ.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא יא) הַנֹּגֵעַ בָּהֶם בְּמֹתָם, כְּעֵין מוֹתָם, דְּהַיְנוּ כְּשֶׁהֵם לַחִים כְּמוֹ שֶׁהָיוּ בְּעֵת מוֹתָם. אֲבָל כָּל זְמַן שֶׁהַשִּׁדְרָה קַיֶּמֶת וְהָעֲצָמוֹת מְחֻבָּרוֹת בַּשִּׁדְרָה, הוֹאִיל וְצוּרָתוֹ נִכֶּרֶת, כְּלַח הוּא נֶחֱשָׁב:


והשרץ – as it is written (Leviticus 11:31): “whoever touches them when they are dead [shall be impure until evening],” like they are dead, that is, when they are moist as they were at the time of their death. But all the time that the spinal column exists and the bones are attached to the spinal column, since its form is recognized, it is considered as moist.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:6)


**וְהַנְּבֵלָה.** דִּכְתִיב (שם) כִּי יָמוּת, כְּעֵין מִיתָה:


והנבלה – as it is written (Numbers 19:14): “[This is the ritual:] When a person dies [in a tent],” similar to death.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:7)


**וְהַשִּׁכְבַת זֶרַע.** שֶׁצָּרִיךְ שֶׁיְּהֵא רָאוּי לְהַזְרִיעַ:


והשכבת זרע – that it needs to be appropriate to emit semen.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:8)


**מְטַמְּאִין לַחִין וְאֵין מְטַמְּאִין יְבֵשִׁין.** הַזּוֹב מְטַמֵּא לַח וְלֹא יָבֵשׁ, דִּכְתִיב (שם טו) רָר בְּשָׂרוֹ אֶת זוֹבוֹ, כְּמִין רִיר שֶׁהוּא לַח. כְּמוֹ וַיּוֹרֶד רִירוֹ עַל זְקָנוֹ (שמואל א כא):


מטמאין לחין ואין מטמאין יבשין – the flux [of someone with gonorrhea] defiles moist but not dry, as it is written (Leviticus 15:3): “[The impurity from his discharge shall mean the following ] whether his member runs with the discharge [or is stopped up so that there is no discharge],” it is similar to discharge/spittle which is moist. It is like (I Samuel 21:14): “and let his saliva run down his beard.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:9)


**וְהַנִּיעַ וְהָרֹק.** דִּכְתִיב (ויקרא טו) וְכִי יָרֹק, וּבָעֵת שֶׁיָּרֹק לַח הוּא:


והניע והרוק – as it is written (Leviticus 15:8): “If one with a discharge spits [on one who is pure],” and at the time that he spits, it is moist.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:10)


**וְכַמָּה הִיא שְׁרִיָּתָן.** דְּנֵימָא אִי הָדְרָא בְּכִי הַאי שִׁעוּרָא לַחִין הֵן:


וכמה היא שירייתן, (and how long must they be soaked?) – that let us say, it retracts in this measurement that they are moist [once again].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:11)


**בְּפוֹשְׁרִים מֵעֵת לְעֵת.** אֲבָל אִי צְרִיכֵי טְפֵי מַיִם חַמִּים, אוֹ יוֹתֵר מִזְּמַן מֵעֵת לְעֵת, יְבֵשִׁין הֵן:


בפורשים מעת לעת – but if more hot water is needed, or more than an astronomical period of twenty-four hours, they are dry (see Talmud Niddah 58a).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:1:12)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בְּשַׂר הַמֵּת יָבֵשׁ, טָהוֹר.** מִלְּטַמֵּא בְּכַזַּיִת. אֲבָל מְטַמֵּא טֻמְאַת רָקָב כִּמְלֹא תַּרְוָד. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


ר' יוסי אומר בשר המת יבש – it is pure from defiling with an olive’s bulk. But it defiles a mass of earth from a grave containing a spoonful of parts of a decayed human body which causes uncleanness. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Yossi (see also Tractate Ahilot, Chapter 2, Mishnah 1).



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:1)


**מְטַמֵּא לְמַפְרֵעַ.** כָּל טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנִּמְצְאוּ בַּמָּבוֹי. דְּמָבוֹי רְשׁוּת הַיָּחִיד הוּא לְעִנְיַן טֻמְאָה, וּסְפֵקוֹ טָמֵא:


מטמא למפרע – all of the pure things that were found in the alleyway [are considered] the private domain concerning [ritual] impurity, and where there is a doubt, it is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:2)


**אוֹ עַד שְׁעַת כִּבּוּד.** מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחֶזְקַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל בּוֹדְקִין מְבוֹאוֹתֵיהֶן בִּשְׁעַת כִּבּוּדֵיהֶן, וְאִם הָיָה שָׁם שֶׁרֶץ הָיָה רוֹאֶה אוֹתוֹ:


או עד שעת כיבוד – because it is the presumption that the Children of Israel (i.e., Jews) examine their alleyways at the time that they are cleaning, and if there had been creeping thing there, he would see it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:3)


**וְכֵן כֶּתֶם שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְחָלוּק מְטַמֵּא לְמַפְרֵעַ.** כָּל טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁעָשְׂתָה הָאִשָּׁה מִיּוֹם כִּבּוּס. אֲבָל אוֹתָן שֶׁל קֹדֶם כִּבּוּס, לֹא, מִפְּנֵי שֶׁחֶזְקַת בְּנוֹת יִשְׂרָאֵל בּוֹדְקוֹת חֲלוּקֵיהֶם בִּשְׁעַת כִּבּוּסֵיהֶן:


וכן כתם שנמצא בחלוק מטמא למפרע – all of the purities that the woman made from the day of . not those that were prior to washing, because it is the presumption that the daughters of Israel examine their garments at the time of their being washed (see Tractate Niddah 58b).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:4)


**הַיָּבֵשׁ מְטַמֵּא לְמַפְרֵעַ.** עַד שְׁעַת כִּבּוּד. דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר לְאַחַר כִּבּוּד מִיָּד נָפַל:


היבש מטמא למפרע – until the time of sweeping. For it is possible to state that after the sweeping it fell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:2:5)


**אֲבָל הַלַּח אֵינוֹ מְטַמֵּא לְמַפְרֵעַ.** אֶלָּא עַד שָׁעָה שֶׁנּוּכַל לוֹמַר אִם נָפַל בְּאוֹתוֹ יוֹם עֲדַיִן יָכוֹל לִהְיוֹת עַכְשָׁיו לַח. אֲבָל עַד שְׁעַת הַכִּבּוּד לִזְמַן מְרֻבֶּה, לֹא, דְּהָא לַח הוּא, וְאִי מֵהַהִיא שַׁעְתָּהּ נָפַל, הֲוָה מִתְעֲבֵיד יָבֵשׁ. וְדַוְקָא בְּשֶׁרֶץ הוּא דְּפָלֵיג רַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן בֵּין לַח לְיָבֵשׁ. אֲבָל בְּכֶתֶם, אֲפִלּוּ לַח מְטַמֵּא לְמַפְרֵעַ עַד שְׁעַת כִּבּוּס, דְּאֵימַר יָבֵשׁ הָיָה וּמַיָּא הוּא דְּנָפַל עֲלֵיהּ. אֲבָל בְּשֶׁרֶץ לֵיכָּא לְמֵימַר הָכִי, דְּאִי אִיתָא דְּמַיִם נָפוּל עֲלֵיהּ, אִמַּרְטוֹטֵי אִמַּרְטוּט. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי שִׁמְעוֹן:


אבל הלח אינו מטמא למפרע – but until the time that we are able to state that if if fell on that day it is still able to now be moist. But not until the hour of sweeping, if it is a long time, for it is moist, and if from that time it fell, it became dry. And especially with a creeping thing that Rabbi Shimon disputes between moist and dry. But regarding a bloodstain, even moist defiles retroactively until the hour of washing, for one can say that it was dry and water fell upon it. But regarding a creeping thing, one cannot state this, for if blood were to fall upon it, it would have been dismembered (see Tractate Niddah 56b).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:1)


**הַבָּאִים מֵרְקָם.** שֶׁהֵן נָכְרִים וְדָמָן טָהוֹר. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּרַבָּנָן גָּזְרוּ עַל הַנָּכְרִים שֶׁיִּהְיוּ כַּזָּבִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם, עַל כִּתְמֵיהֶם לֹא גָּזְרוּ:


כל הבאים מרקם – (see Tractate Gittin, Chapter 1, Mishnayot 1-2) – that they are heathens and their blood is pure. But even though the Rabbis decreed on the heathens that they would be like those with gonorrhea/flux for all their matters, they did not make this decree on their bloodstains.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:2)


**מֵרֶקֶם.** בֵּין קָדֵשׁ וּבֵין שׁוּר, מְתַרְגְּמִינַן בֵּין רֶקֶם וּבֵין חַגְּרָא:


מרקם – between Kadesh and Shur, and we translate in Aramaic: between Rekem and Hagra (a town and province in the desert of Shur).


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:3)


**שֶׁהֵן גֵּרִים.** וְדָמָן טָמֵא:


שהן גרים- and their blood is impure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:4)


**וְטוֹעִים.** כְּלוֹמַר, לֹא צְנִיעֵי כֻּלֵּי הַאי, וְאֵין מַצְנִיעִים אֶת כִּתְמֵיהֶן, הִלְכָּךְ חָיְישִׁינַן שֶׁמָּא כְּתָמִים שֶׁל אִשָּׁה נִדָּה הֵן:


וטועים – meaning to say, that they are not that chaste/modest, and they don’t hide their bloodstains. Therefore, we are concerned lest the bloodstains of women [indicate] that they are menstruating/Niddah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:5)


**הַבָּאִים מִבֵּין הַגּוֹיִם טְהוֹרִים.** דְּלֹא גָּזְרוּ רַבָּנָן עַל כִּתְמֵיהֶן, כֵּיוָן דְּדָמָן וַדַּאי טָהוֹר מִן הַתּוֹרָה:


הבאים מבין הגויים טהורים- that the Rabbis did not decree on their bloodstains, since their blood is certainly pure from the Torah.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:3:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:3:6)


**מִבֵּין יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִבֵּין הַכּוּתִים רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַמֵּא.** מַתְנִיתִין חַסּוֹרֵי מִחַסְּרָא וְהָכִי קָתָנֵי, מִבֵּין יִשְׂרָאֵל וּמִבֵּין הַכּוּתִים, טְמֵאִים, דְּכוּתִים גֵּרֵי אֱמֶת הֵן וְדָמָן טָמֵא. כְּתָמִים הַנִּמְצָאִים בְּעָרֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דְּהַיְנוּ בְּמָקוֹם גָּלוּי, טְהוֹרִים, דְּלֹא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עַל כִּתְמֵיהֶן וְאַצְנוֹעֵי מַצְנְעֵי לְהוּ. הַנִּמְצָאִין בְּעָרֵי כּוּתִים, רַבִּי מֵאִיר מְטַמֵּא, דְּכוּתִים נֶחְשְׁדוּ עַל כִּתְמֵיהֶן. וַחֲכָמִים מְטַהֲרִים, שֶׁלֹּא נֶחְשְׁדוּ עַל כִּתְמֵיהֶן וּמַצְנְעֵי לְהוּ, וְתָלִינַן לְהַאי כֶּתֶם בְּדַם חַיָּה אוֹ בְּהֵמָה, דְּאִלּוּ דַּם אִשָּׁה נִדָּה, לֹא הָיָה נִמְצָא בְּמָקוֹם גָּלוּי, דְּאִינְהוּ נַמִּי מַצְנְעוּ לְהוּ כְּיִשְׂרָאֵל. וּכְבָר נִפְסְקָה הַהֲלָכָה דְּבַזְמַן הַזֶּה גָּזְרוּ עַל כּוּתִים לִהְיוֹת כַּנָּכְרִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם, וְכִתְמֵיהֶן טְהוֹרִים כְּכִתְמֵי הַנָּכְרִים:


מבין ישראל ומבין הכותים ר' מאיר מטמא - Our Mishnah is deficient and should be read as follows: From those among the Israelites and the Cutheans are unclean/impure, for Cutheans are true converts and their blood is unclean/impure. Bloodstains that are found in the cities of Israel, that is in an open place are pure, for they are not suspected on their bloodstains and they certainly are reserved/private. Those that are found in Cuthean cities – Rabbi Meir defiles as the Cutheans were suspect on their bloodstains. But the Sages declare pure, for they were not suspected on their bloodstains and were modest/reserved, and we attach/assign this bloodstain to the blood of a wild beast or animal, for if it had been the blood of a menstruating woman, it would not be found in an open/revealed place, for they also are modest/refined like Israel. And the Halakha was already decided for at this time period, they (i.e., the Rabbis) decreed regarding the Cutheans to be considered as heathens for all their matters, and their bloodstains are pure like the bloodstains of heathens.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:1)


**בְּכָל מָקוֹם.** בִּמְקוֹם יִשְׂרָאֵל:


בכל מקום – in an Israelite place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:2)


**טְהוֹרִים.** שֶׁחֶזְקָתָן אֵינָן מִדַּם נִדָּה, דְּאַצְנוֹעֵי מַצְנְעֵי לְהוּ:


טהורים – that their presumption is that they are not from the blood of a menstruating woman/Niddah, for they are modest/chaste.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:3)


**בֵּית הַטֻּמְאוֹת.** חֶדֶר שֶׁהַנָּשִׁים מִשְׁתַּמְּשׁוֹת בּוֹ בִּימֵי נִדּוּתָן:


בית הטומאות – the room that the woman use during the days of their menstruation.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:4:4)


**קוֹבְרִין שָׁם.** לְפִי שָׁעָה, כְּדֵי שֶׁיְּסַלְּקוּ אוֹתָן לְאַחַר זְמַן. וּלְפִי שֶׁאֵין אָנוּ יוֹדְעִין אִם הֵסִירוּ אוֹתָן אִם לָאו, מְטַמְּאִים בְּאֹהֶל:


קוברין שם – according to hour, in order that they can remove them for another time. And because we don’t know if they removed them or not, they defile in the tent through overshadowing.



### Mishnah 5

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:1)


**נֶאֱמָנִים לוֹמַר קָבַרְנוּ.** דְּטֻמְאַת מֵת מִידִי דְּאוֹרַיְתָא הִיא. וּכְגוֹן שֶׁכֹּהֵן כּוּתִי עוֹמֵד שָׁם וּתְרוּמָה בְּיָדוֹ וְאוֹכְלָהּ, דְּלֵיכָּא לְמֵיחַשׁ שֶׁמָּא תְּרוּמָה טְמֵאָה הִיא. וּבְלָאו הָכִי אֵין נֶאֱמָנִין, דְּלֵית לְהוּ לִפְנֵי עִוֵּר לֹא תִתֵּן מִכְשׁוֹל, וְלֹא אִכְפַּת לָהֶם אִם אָנוּ חוֹטְאִין עַל פִּיהֶם:


מאמנים לומר קברנו - for defilement by the dead is from the Torah. And for example, that a Cuthean priest stands there with heave-offering in his hand and consumes it, that we don’t have to be concerned that perhaps it is impure heave-offering. But without this they are not believed, for they lack (Leviticus 19:14): “or place a stumbling-block before the blind,” and they don’t care if we end up sinning through their mouths.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:2)


**אִם בִּכְּרָה.** וְהוּא שֶׁרְאִינוּהוּ עוֹבֵד וְגוֹזֵז בּוֹ. דְּאִי לָאו דְּיָדַע דְּלָאו בְּכוֹר הוּא, לֹא עָבַר אַדְּאוֹרַיְתָא, דִּכְתִיב (דברים טו) לֹא תַעֲבֹד בִּבְכֹר שׁוֹרֶךָ וְלֹא תָגֹז בְּכוֹר צֹאנֶךָ:


אם בכרה – and that we see [him] working and shearing it (i.e., the cattle), for if he didn’t know that it is not a firstling, he didn’t transgress that which is in the Torah, as it is written (Deuteronomy 15:19): “you must not work your firstling ox or shear your firstling sheep.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:3)


**נֶאֱמָנִים עַל צִיּוּן קְבָרוֹת.** לְצַיֵּן מְקוֹם הַקֶּבֶר. וְסוֹמְכִין עֲלֵיהֶן. וְאַף עַל גַּב דְּמִדְּרַבָּנָן הוּא, כֵּיוָן דִּכְתִיבָא, זְהִירִין בֵּיהּ. דִּכְתִיב (יחזקאל לט) וְרָאָה עֶצֶם אָדָם וּבָנָה אֶצְלוֹ צִיּוּן:


נאמנים על ציון קברות – to put up a mark to indicate the place of the grave and we rely on them and even though it is from the Rabbis, since it is written, we are careful, as it is written (Ezekiel 39:15): “any one of them who sees a human bone shall erect a marker beside it.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:4)


**לֹא עַל הַסְּכָכוֹת.** אִילָן הַמֵּסֵךְ עַל הָאָרֶץ וַעֲנָפָיו מֻבְדָּלִים וְיָדוּעַ וַדַּאי שֶׁיֵּשׁ קֶבֶר תַּחַת אַחַת הָאֳהָלוֹת וְאֵין יָדוּעַ אֵיזֶה הוּא, וּבָא כּוּתִי וְהֵעִיד עַל אֶחָד שֶׁזֶּה טָהוֹר, אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן, לְפִי שֶׁאֵינוֹ חָשׁ עַל הַסָּפֵק:


לא על הסככות – a tree (with a seat of uncleanness on one of its boughs throwing a shadow (forming a tent) over the ground and its branches/foliage are separated and it is known that there is definitely a grave under one of the tents, but it is not known which it is, and a Cuthean comes and testifies on one that this is pure, he is not believed, because he is not concerned with that which is doubtful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:5)


**וְלֹא עַל הַפְּרָעוֹת.** אֲבָנִים גְּדוֹלוֹת בּוֹלְטוֹת מִן הַגָּדֵר, וְקֶבֶר תַּחַת אַחַת מֵהֶן וְאֵין אָנוּ יוֹדְעִים תַּחַת אֵיזוֹ מֵהֶן, וּבָא כּוּתִי וְהֵעִיד עַל מִקְצָתָן שֶׁהֵן טְהוֹרוֹת:


ולא על הפרעות – large protruding stones from the fence and a grave is under one of them and we don’t know which of them, and a Cuthean comes and testifies on part of them that they are pure.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:6)


**אֵינוֹ נֶאֱמָן.** מִשּׁוּם דְּלִסְפֵקָא לֹא חָיֵשׁ:


אינו נאמן – because he is not concerned about something doubtful.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:7)


**בֵּית הַפְּרָס.** שָׂדֶה שֶׁנֶּחֱרַשׁ בָּהּ קֶבֶר. וְכָל מֵאָה אַמָּה שֶׁל סְבִיבוֹת הַקֶּבֶר אוֹקְמִינְהוּ רַבָּנָן בְּחֶזְקַת טֻמְאָה, שֶׁמָּא גִּלְגְּלָה הַמַּחֲרֵשָׁה וְהוֹלִיכָה עֶצֶם כַּשְּׂעֹרָה מִן הַמֵּת. וּפְרָס, לְשׁוֹן דָּבָר פָּרוּס וְשָׁבוּר, שֶׁעֲצָמוֹת שֶׁל מֵת נִפְרְסוּ שָׁם. וּמִפִּי אֲחֵרִים שָׁמַעְתִּי, עַל שֵׁם שֶׁפַּרְסוֹת בְּנֵי אָדָם נִמְנָעִים לָלֶכֶת שָׁם מִפְּנֵי הַטֻּמְאָה:


בית הפרס (an area in which uncertainty exists concerning the location of a grave or a corpse)- a field in which a grave is plowed there. And all one hundred cubits surrounding the grave the Rabbis established as having the presumption of [ritual] impurity, lest perhaps the plough rolled and dragged a bone like the size of a barley from the corpse. And [the word] פרס is the language of split and broken, that the bones of the dead person were split there. And from others I heard, that on account of the fact that feet of human beings are prevented from walking there because of the defilement.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 7:5:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/7:5:8)


**זֶה הַכְּלָל.** לַאֲתוֹיֵי תְּחוּמִים וְיֵין נֶסֶךְ. שֶׁאֵין כּוּתִי נֶאֱמָן לוֹמַר עַד כָּאן תְּחוּם שַׁבָּת, דִּתְחוּמִין דְּרַבָּנָן, וְכוּתִים לֹא סְבִירָא לְהוּ. וְכֵן אֵין נֶאֱמָנִים עַל יֵין נֶסֶךְ, לְפִי שֶׁאֵין נִזְהָרִים מִמַּגַּע נָכְרִי. וְכָל הַדְּבָרִים הַלָּלוּ לֹא נֶאֶמְרוּ אֶלָּא בַּדּוֹרוֹת הָרִאשׁוֹנִים, אֲבָל בַּזְּמַן הַזֶּה, הֵן כַּנָּכְרִים לְכָל דִּבְרֵיהֶם:


זה הכלל – to include the [Sabbath] limits and wine poured as a libation/יין נסך. For a Cuthean is not believed to state that the Sabbath limit extends to here, for the [Sabbath] limits are Rabbinic [ordinances] and the Cutheans do not hold by them. And similarly, they are not believed on wine poured as a libation, for they are not careful from contact with a heathen. And all of these things were not stated other than during the early generations, but at this time, they are considered as heathens for all their matters.



## Chapter 8


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:1)


**הָרוֹאָה כֶתֶם.** כְּנֶגֶד בֵּית הַתֻּרְפָּה. בֵּית הָעֶרְוָה:


הרואה כתם. כנגד בית התורפה (A woman who sees a bloodstain, corresponding to her pudenda) – place of nakedness/lewdness..


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:2)


**טְמֵאָה.** דְּאִיכָּא לְמֵימַר, דָּם מֵאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם נָפַל:


טמאה - for one cans ay that the blood from that place fell.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:3)


**עַל עֲקֵבָהּ.** זִמְנִין דְּמִתְרְמֵי וְנָגַע בְּאוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם:


על עקבה – that at times that by chance and it touched that place.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:4)


**וְעַל רֹאשׁ גּוּדָלָהּ.** דְּבַהֲדֵי דְּפָסְעָה מִתְרְמֵי גּוּדָל שֶׁבְּרַגְלָהּ לִהְיוֹת תַּחַת אוֹתוֹ מָקוֹם וְדָם נוֹטֵף עָלָיו:


ועל ראש גודלה – that while she stepped, it happened to be that the big toe that is on her foot was under that place and blood dripped upon it.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:5)


**עַל שׁוֹקָהּ וְעַל פַּרְסוֹתֶיהָ מִבִּפְנִים טְמֵאָה.** כְּשֶׁאָדָם עוֹמֵד וּמְחַבֵּר רַגְלָיו וְשׁוֹקָיו זוֹ בָּזוֹ, כָּל מַה שֶּׁמְּחֻבָּר מֵהֶם זוֹ עִם זוֹ, קָרוּי לִפְנִים:


על שוקה ועל פרסותיה מבפנים טמאה (on her thigh or on her foot) – when a person stands and fastens his legs and thighs one to the other, all that is attached one with the other from them is called “on the inside.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:6)


**מִבַּחוּץ.** מִכָּאן וּמִכָּאן:


מבחוץ – from here and from there.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:7)


**וְעַל הַצְּדָדִין.** אֲחוֹרֵי הַשּׁוֹק כָּל גָּבְהָן כְּנֶגֶד הֶעָקֵב, וּלְפָנָיו כָּל גָּבְהָן כְּנֶגֶד הָרֶגֶל, קְרוּיִים צְדָדִים:


ועל הצדדין – behind the thigh all of their height corresponding to the ankle, and in front of it all its high corresponding to the leg, are called “the sides.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:8)


**מִן הַחֲגוֹר וּלְמַטָּה.** כְּנֶגֶד בֵּית הַתֻּרְפָּה הוּא:


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:9)


**אִם מַגִּיעַ כְּנֶגֶד בֵּית הַתֻּרְפָּה.** כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְּרֹאשׁ בֵּית יָדָהּ סָמוּךְ לַיָּד, שֶׁפְּעָמִים שֶׁפּוֹשַׁטְתָּן מִזְּרוֹעַ וְנוֹפֵל כְּנֶגֶד מַטָּה:


אם מגיע כנגד בית התורפה – as for example, that it (i.e., the bloodstain) is found at the top of the sleeve near the hand, that sometimes she stretches them from the arm and it correspondingly falls corresponding downwards.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:10)


**וְאִם לָאו.** שֶׁלֹּא כְּנֶגֶד בֵּית הַתֻּרְפָּה, כְּגוֹן שֶׁנִּמְצָא סָמוּךְ לַכְּתֵפוֹת:


ואם לאו – that it does not correspond with the pudenda, as for example, that it (i.e., the bloodstain) is found near the shoulders.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:11)


**שֶׁהוּא חוֹזֵר.** פְּעָמִים שֶׁרֹאשׁ הֶחָלוּק נֶהֱפָךְ כְּנֶגֶד פָּנֶיהָ שֶׁל מַטָּה:


שהוא חוזר – sometimes that the top of he garment is reversed opposite her face below.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:1:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:1:12)


**וְכֵן בַּפּוֹלְיוֹן.** מִטְפַּחַת שֶׁהִיא מִתְכַּסָּה בּוֹ:


וכן בפוליון (and similarly a pallium/a sheet worn as a cloak and used for a bed-cover) -towel/apron that she covers herself with.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:1)


**וְתוֹלָה.** הַכֶּתֶם שֶׁמָּצְאָה:


ותולה (she hangs it/blames it) – the bloodstain that she found.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:2)


**בְּכָל דָּבָר שֶׁהִיא יְכוֹלָה לִתְלוֹת.** לוֹמַר שֶׁלֹּא מִמֶּנָּה הָיָה:


בכל דבר שהיא יכולה לתלות – to state that it (i.e., the bloodstain) was not from here.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:3)


**מַאֲכֹלֶת.** כִּנָּה:


מאכולת (louse) – vermin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:4)


**עַד כַּמָּה הִיא תוֹלָה.** בְּדַם מַאֲכֹלֶת:


עד כמה הוא תולה – with the blood of a louse/vermin.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:5)


**עַד כַּגְּרִיס.** אֲבָל טְפֵי מֵהָכִי אֵין דַּם מַאֲכֹלֶת כָּל כָּךְ גָּדוֹל. וּבְהָכִי מִיהָא תַּלְיָא וְאַף עַל פִּי שֶׁלֹּא יָדְעָה שֶׁהָרְגָה. וְרַבִּי חֲנִינָא פָּלֵיג אַתַּנָּא קַמָּא דְּאָמַר הָרְגָה אִין, לֹא הָרְגָה לֹא. וְאֵין הֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי חֲנִינָא בֶּן אַנְטִיגְנוֹס:


עד כגריס (the size of a bean/split bean) – but more than this, the blood of a louse is not that great. And in this, however, she blames, and even though she didn’t know that she killed it. But Rabbi Hanina [ben Antigonos] disputes the first Tanna/teacher who stated, that yes (i.e., she may place the blame upon it), if she killed it, but not, if she didn’t. But the Halakha is not according to Rabbi Hanina ben Antigonos.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:6)


**וְתוֹלָה בִבְנָהּ וּבְבַעְלָהּ.** הַשּׁוֹכְבִים אֶצְלָהּ אִם יֵשׁ בָּהֶן מַכָּה, לוֹמַר שֶׁכֶּתֶם זֶה מֵהֶן הָיָה:


ותולה בבנה ובבעלה – that lie next to her, if they have a wound, to state that this bloodstain was from them.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:7)


**וְאִם יֵשׁ בָּהּ מַכָּה.** שֶׁהֶעֶלְתָה קְרוּם:


ואם יש בה מכה – that the skin/membrane lifted off [the wound].


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:2:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:2:8)


**וְהִיא יְכוֹלָה לְהִתְגַּלַּע.** לְהִגָּלוֹת. מִלְּשׁוֹן לִפְנֵי הִתְגַּלַּע הָרִיב (משלי יז). כְּלוֹמַר, שֶׁיָּסִיר הַקְּרוּם וִיקַלֵּף וְתִתְגַּלֶּה הַמַּכָּה וְיֵצֵא מִמֶּנָּה דָּם, תּוֹלָה בָהּ:


והיא יכולה להתגלע (and she is able to scratch it open) – to be revealed. From the language (Proverbs 17:14): “Before a dispute flares up/לפני התגלע הריב [drop it]," that is to say, that he should the skin/membrane and peel it and the wound will be revealed and blood comes out from it, she may blame it (i.e., rely upon it).



### Mishnah 3

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:3:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:3:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:3:1)


**דָּם וְלֹא כָתֶם.** וַחֲכָמִים הוּא דְּגַזּוּר עַל הַכְּתָמִים, לְפִיכָךְ הוֹלְכִים בָּהֶם לְהָקֵל:


דם ולא כתם – but it is the Sages who decreed on the bloodstains, therefore, we follow them for a lenient ruling.



### Mishnah 4

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:4:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:1)


**עֵד שֶׁהוּא נָתוּן תַּחַת הַכַּר.** הַבֶּגֶד שֶׁמְּקַנַּחַת בּוֹ אֶת עַצְמָהּ בִּשְׁעַת תַּשְׁמִישׁ, קָרוּי עֵד:


עד שהוא נתון תחת הכר – the clothing that uses to clean/wipe herself at the time of sexual intercourse is called a “testing or examining rag”/piece of cloth used by women for ascertaining their condition of cleanness or uncleanness


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:4:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:2)


**דָּם עָגֹל טָהוֹר.** דְּדַם מַאֲכֹלֶת הוּא:


דם עגול טהור – for it is the blood of a louse.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:4:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:3)


**מָשׁוּךְ טָמֵא.** וְאִם טִפָּה מְשׁוּכָה הִיא, טְמֵאָה, שֶׁחֶזְקָתָהּ שֶׁהִיא מִמֶּנָּה, וּבְעֵת שֶׁקִּנְּחָה עַצְמָהּ נִתְלָה זֶה הַדָּם בָּעֵד:


משוך טמא – but if it is an elongated drop, it is impure, for the presumption is that it (i.e., the bloodstain) is from it, and at the time that she wiped herself, she blames this blood on the examining rag.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 8:4:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/8:4:4)


**דִּבְרֵי רַבִּי אֱלִיעֶזֶר בַּר רַבִּי צָדוֹק.** וְכֵן הֲלָכָה:


דברי ר' אליעזר בר ר' צדוק – and such is the Halakha.



## Chapter 9


### Mishnah 1

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:1:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:1)


**הָאִשָּׁה שֶׁהִיא עוֹשָׂה צְרָכֶיהָ.** מַשְׁתֶּנֶת מַיִם:


האשה שהיא עושה צרכיה - urinates.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:1:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:2)


**אִם עוֹמֶדֶת טְמֵאָה.** דְּאָמְרִינַן מֵי רַגְלַיִם הֲדוּר לַמָּקוֹר וַאֲתָא דָּם מִן הַמָּקוֹר בַּהֲדַיְהוּ:


אם עומדת טמאה – since we say that urine that returns to the interior of the womb [from where the menses are discharged] and blood comes from the interior of the womb together.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:1:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:3)


**יוֹשֶׁבֶת טְהוֹרָה.** בַּגְּמָרָא מוֹקְמִינַן לַהּ בְּיוֹשֶׁבֶת עַל שְׂפַת הַסֵּפֶל וּמְקַלַּחַת מֵי רַגְלַיִם לְתוֹכוֹ בְּחֹזֶק. וּכְשֶׁהִיא מְקַלַּחַת בְּחֹזֶק, אֵין דֶּרֶךְ דַּם הַמָּקוֹר לָצֵאת עִם מֵי רַגְלַיִם. וְאִי לְבָתַר דְּתַמּוּ מַיָּא בָּא דָּם זֶה מִן הַמָּקוֹר, לֹא הָיָה הַדָּם נִמְצָא בְּתוֹךְ הַסֵּפֶל אֶלָּא עַל שְׂפַת הַסֵּפֶל, שֶׁדָּם הַבָּא מִן הַמָּקוֹר, שׁוֹתֵת וְיוֹרֵד וְאֵינוֹ מְקַלֵּחַ. וְדָם זֶה שֶׁנִּמְצָא בְּתוֹךְ הַסֵּפֶל, עַל כָּרְחָךְ מִמְּקוֹם מֵי רַגְלַיִם בָּא וּמַכָּה יֵשׁ בָּהּ שָׁם, וְלֹא מִן הַמָּקוֹר. וּבְעוֹמֶדֶת עַל שְׂפַת הַסֵּפֶל וּמְקַלַּחַת בְּתוֹךְ הַסֵּפֶל, לֹא הָיָה אֶפְשָׁר לְהַעֲמִידָהּ, דְּעוֹמֶדֶת אִי אֶפְשָׁר לָהּ לְקַלֵּחַ, וּלְהָכִי אִצְטְרִיךְ תַּנָּא לְמִנְקַט יוֹשֶׁבֶת:


יושבת טהורה – In the Gemara (Tractate Niddah 59b in the words of Rabbi Abba) we establish that while she is siting on the edge of the bowl/basin and the urine splashes/brings forth an uninterrupted jet into it with power, but there is no path for the blood of the interior of the womb to go out with the urine, but if after the water has stopped, this blood comes from the interior of the womb, the blood was not found within the bowl /basin, but on the edge, for the blood that comes from the interior of the womb flows gently/comes down slowly and does not splash/bring forth an uninterrupted jet, but this blood that is found within the bowl/basin perforce comes from the place of the urine and there is a force there, but not from the interior of the womb. But while she is standing at the edge of the basin and splashes an uninterrupted jet within the bowl, it was not possible to straighten her up, for when she is standing it is impossible for her to splash/bring forth an uninterrupted jet, for this reason it was necessary for the Tanna/teacher [of the Mishnah] to hold [the tradition] of “sitting.”


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:1:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:1:4)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי אוֹמֵר בֵּין כָּךְ וּבֵין כָּךְ טְהוֹרָה.** וַהֲלָכָה כְּרַבִּי יוֹסֵי:


רבי יוסי אומר בין כך ובין כך טהורה – and the Halakha is according to Rabbi Yossi.



### Mishnah 2

###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:2:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:2:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:2:1)


**שֶׁעָשׂוּ צְרָכֵיהֶן.** הִטִּילוּ מֵי רַגְלַיִם:


שעשו צרכיהם – that they urinated.


###### Bartenura on Mishnah Niddah 9:2:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:2:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Bartenura%20on%20Mishnah%20Niddah/r/9:2:2)


**רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַהֵר.** דְּהָא אֲפִלּוּ בְּאִשָּׁה גְּרֵידְתָא דְּלֵיכָּא אֶלָּא חֲדָא סְפֵקָא, סָפֵק בָּא מִן הַמָּקוֹר, סָפֵק בָּא מִמְּקוֹם מֵי רַגְלַיִם, מְטַהֵר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לְעֵיל, וְכָל שֶׁכֵּן הָכָא דְּדִלְמָא מֵאִישׁ אָתֵי. וְלֹא הָדַר תַּנָּא הָכָא רַבִּי יוֹסֵי מְטַהֵר, אֶלָּא לְאַשְׁמוֹעִינַן מִמִּשְׁנָה יְתֵירְתָא דַּאֲפִלּוּ לְכַתְּחִלָּה מְטַהֵר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי לְאִשָּׁה שֶׁעוֹשָׂה צְרָכֶיהָ וְרָאֲתָה דָּם, שֶׁתִּתְעַסֵּק בְּטָהֳרוֹת. דְּלֹא תֵימָא דַּוְקָא דִּיעֲבַד מְטַהֵר רַבִּי יוֹסֵי טָהֳרוֹת שֶׁנִּתְעַסְּקָה בָּהֶן, אֲבָל לְכַתְּחִלָּה לֹא אָמְרִינַן לַהּ שֶׁתִּתְעַסֵּק, קָמַשְׁמַע לָן:


רבי יוסי מטהר – for even for a woman alone for which there is only one doubt, that is the doubt of whether it (i.e., the blood) comes from the interior of the womb or whether it comes from the place of the urine, Rabbi Yossi declares her pure above (see previous Mishnah), and all the more so if here if it comes from a man.
